TL;DR: nuReasoning是一个大规模真实世界数据集和基准，包含20,000个20秒长片段，涵盖多城市多模态数据（摄像头、LiDAR、HD地图）及三种推理标注（空间推理、决策推理、反事实推理），用于评估和提升自动驾驶系统在长尾场景中的推理能力。实验表明，在该数据集上微调VLM可显著提升驾驶问答性能，且推理监督可改善规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集和基准主要针对感知、预测或规划任务，对长尾场景中需要常识知识、空间关系理解、智能体交互推理的安全决策缺乏充分的推理监督。

Method: 构建包含20,000个片段（每段20秒）的多城市真实世界数据集，提供多模态数据（多相机图像、LiDAR、HD地图、目标标注）及人工验证的三类推理标注（空间推理、决策推理、反事实推理），支持推理评估和规划评估。

Result: 微调VLM显著提升驾驶专用问答准确性；将推理监督融入VLA训练可提升规划性能，即使推理文本输出在推理时被禁用。

Conclusion: nuReasoning可作为评估和提升鲁棒、可解释、推理驱动的自动驾驶系统在真实长尾场景中的基础基准。

Abstract: Reasoning is essential for autonomous driving (AD) in long-tail scenarios, where vehicles must apply commonsense knowledge, understand spatial relations, infer agent interactions, and make safe decisions. However, existing AD datasets and benchmarks mainly target perception, prediction, or planning, and provide limited supervision for reasoning over realistic long-tail driving scenes. We introduce nuReasoning, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for reasoning-centric AD. Following the lineage of nuScenes and nuPlan, nuReasoning advances real-world AD datasets and benchmarks toward reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. The dataset contains 20,000 clips, each 20 seconds long, collected across multiple cities, with synchronized multi-camera images, LiDAR data, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations spanning Spatial Reasoning, Decision Reasoning, and Counterfactual Reasoning. Unlike prior datasets that focus primarily on visual question answering, nuReasoning supports both reasoning evaluation and planning evaluation, enabling a direct study of how reasoning supervision affects driving performance. Experiments show that fine-tuning VLMs on nuReasoning substantially improves driving-specific question answering, while incorporating reasoning supervision into VLA training improves planning performance even when textual reasoning outputs are disabled at inference time. These results establish nuReasoning as a foundation for evaluating and improving robust, interpretable, reasoning-driven AD systems in realistic long-tail settings.

</details>


### [75] [Joint Multi-Camera LiDAR Extrinsic Calibration via Learned Pairwise Initialization and Geometric Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31576)
*Aziz Al-Najjar,Marzieh Amini,James R. Green,Felix Kwamena*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段联合多相机-激光雷达外参标定框架，通过先独立预测每对相机-激光雷达的初始外参和2D-3D对应关系，再联合进行多帧光束法平差优化，将各相机独立预测结果转化为全局一致的多相机标定结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的相机-激光雷达标定方法将每对相机-激光雷达独立处理，忽略了多相机平台中相机之间的刚性几何耦合，导致各相机标定结果在系统层面不一致。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段独立使用CMRNext对每个相机生成初始外参估计和密集2D-3D对应关系；第二阶段通过多帧光束法平差联合优化，包含重投影项、每相机先验项和相对位姿先验项，将成对预测转换为全局一致的多相机标定。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上达到0.89cm平移误差和0.038°旋转误差；在Walkley数据集上将平移误差从108.6cm降至3.1cm。

Conclusion: 显式建模多相机耦合能够在单相机预测不可靠时显著提升标定精度和一致性，该方法在域内和域外数据集上均表现优异。

Abstract: Most learning-based camera-LiDAR calibration methods treat each camera-LiDAR pair independently, ignoring the rigid geometric coupling in multi-camera platforms. As a result, per-camera estimates may be individually accurate yet inconsistent at the system level. We present a two-stage framework for joint multi-camera LiDAR extrinsic calibration that combines learned pairwise matching with geometric refinement. First, CMRNext is applied independently to each camera to produce initial extrinsic estimates and dense 2D-3D correspondences. These predictions are then jointly refined through a multi-frame bundle adjustment with reprojection, per-camera prior, and relative-pose prior terms. This approach converts pairwise predictions into a globally consistent multi-camera calibration. Experiments on KITTI (in-domain for CMRNext) and Walkley (out-of-domain) datasets show improved per-camera accuracy and inter-camera consistency. On KITTI, the method achieves 0.89 cm translation error and 0.038 rotation error. On Walkley, it reduces translation error from 108.6 cm to 3.1 cm, highlighting the benefit of explicit multi-camera coupling when single-camera predictions are less reliable.

</details>


### [76] [SurGe: Improved Surface Geometry in Point Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31577)
*Karim Knaebel,Gonzalo Martin Garcia,Christian Schmidt,Ilya Fradlin,Lucas Nunes,Daan de Geus,Bastian Leibe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出点图法线度量来评估3D重建的局部表面几何质量，并引入点梯度匹配损失和邻域注意力解码器（NAD）两个互补组件来改善局部几何预测。所提出的SurGe模型在八个零样本单目几何基准上取得了最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建方法在全局几何预测上表现良好，但局部表面几何不准确，且常用评估指标无法充分反映这一缺陷。作者希望使这些误差在评估中更明确，并减少这些误差。

Method: 1) 提出点图法线度量（point map normal metric），通过评估相邻3D预测点诱导的局部表面方向来量化局部几何质量；2) 提出点梯度匹配损失（point gradient matching loss），对深度归一化的3D有限差分进行监督；3) 提出邻域注意力解码器（Neighborhood Attention Decoder, NAD），通过渐进式上采样和邻域注意力机制进行局部特征融合。

Result: 在八个零样本单目几何基准上，SurGe在全局点图AbsRel指标上取得最佳平均排名，并在局部点图和点图法线评估上实现一致改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的点图法线度量能更明确地暴露局部几何误差，而点梯度匹配损失和NAD有效改善了局部表面几何质量，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Recent feedforward 3D reconstruction methods predict point maps and estimate global 3D geometry remarkably well. However, their predictions still exhibit inaccurate local surface geometry, which is clearly visible qualitatively but only weakly reflected in common metrics. To make these errors more explicit in evaluation, we introduce a point map normal metric that evaluates the local surface orientation induced by neighboring 3D predictions. To reduce these errors, we propose two complementary components: a point gradient matching loss that supervises depth-normalized 3D finite differences, and a Neighborhood Attention Decoder (NAD) that progressively upsamples features and uses Neighborhood Attention for local feature mixing. Across eight zero-shot monocular geometry benchmarks, our model, SurGe, achieves the best average rank for global point map AbsRel and consistently improves local point map and point map normal evaluations.

</details>


### [77] [Recognizing Co-Speech Gestures in-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31589)
*Sindhu B Hegde,K R Prajwal,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRW数据集，首个大规模、细粒度的野外手势识别基准，包含156,688个手动标注视频片段，覆盖150种语义手势词汇，支持语义手势分类、词汇识别和时间定位三项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型难以捕捉语义共语手势，主要瓶颈在于缺乏精确标注的训练数据。

Method: 构建GRW数据集（156,688个手动标注视频，150词分类体系），训练视频模型完成三项任务：语义手势二分类、对应词汇识别、时间定位。

Result: 建立了三项任务的基准测试，验证了数据集的有效性。

Conclusion: GRW填补了语义共语手势细粒度标注数据的空白，为多模态手势理解提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: While humans naturally gesture during speech, only a sparse subset of these movements are visually depictive and semantically linked to specific spoken words. Current multimodal models struggle to capture these semantic co-speech gestures, heavily bottlenecked by a lack of precisely annotated training data. To address this, we introduce the Gesture Recognition in the Wild (GRW) dataset, the first large-scale benchmark designed to map unconstrained human gestures to specific words with frame-accurate temporal boundaries. Comprising 156,688 manually annotated video clips, GRW spans a highly diverse 150-word taxonomy of physical actions, spatial descriptors, and abstract concepts. We leverage GRW to train video models to (a) classify gestures as semantic or not, (b) recognize the word corresponding to a co-speech gesture, and (c) temporally localize the gesture. We also use GRW to establish benchmarks for these three tasks.

</details>


### [78] [TunerDiT: Training-free Progressive Steering of Diffusion Transformer for Multi-Event Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31590)
*Ruotong Liao,Guowen Huang,Qing Cheng,Guangyao Zhai,Lei Zhang,Xun Xiao,Thomas Seidl,Daniel Cremers,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TunerDiT，一种无需额外训练、基于扩散过程内在转折点的渐进式引导方法，通过事件分区掩码和跨事件提示融合，显著提升多事件文本到视频生成的性能，并在自建基准Meve上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频生成在生成长时序、多事件的视频时面临挑战，现有方法难以同时保证事件分离和视频整体一致性。

Method: (1) 发现DiT去噪轨迹中存在内在转折点，文本条件在此从全局布局过渡到细粒度细节；(2) 提出Event-Partitioned Masking强制执行事件边界并允许交叉事件过渡带；(3) 提出Cross-Event Prompt Fusion注入相邻事件语义用于后期细化。

Result: 在8项指标上达到SOTA性能，文本对齐度随事件数量增加而提升，展现出随事件数扩展的可能性。

Conclusion: TunerDiT作为一种训练免费方法，在视频一致性和事件分离之间提供了可调节的权衡，并具有随事件数量增长而扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation faces challenging questions when generating videos with long horizons containing multiple events. Inspired by the intrinsics of the diffusion process, we probe video diffusion transformers (DiTs) and uncover intrinsic turning points in the DiT denoising trajectory where conditioning text affects generation from global layout to fine-grained details. Building on this finding, we present TunerDiT, a simple yet effective progressive steering method that requires no additional training for multi-event generation. TunerDiT comprises two steering handles: (1) Event-Partitioned Masking that enforces event boundaries while allowing cross-event transition bands; (2) Cross-Event Prompt Fusion that injects neighboring event semantics for late-stage refinement. We contribute a self-curated prompt suite for benchmarking multi-event generation, i.e., Meve. TunerDiT achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 metrics and offers a tunable trade-off between video consistency and event separation, compared with other training-free methods. The improvement in text alignment increases with the event count, indicating a scaling possibility with increasing event count.

</details>


### [79] [CoFiDA-M: Concept-Aware Feature Modulation for Cross-Domain Adaptation with Image-Only Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31591)
*Nurjahan Sultana,Moi Hoon Yap,Xinqi Fan,Wenqi Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFiDA-M框架，通过在训练时利用MONET基础模型提供的临床概念概率作为特权信息，训练一个包含教师网络和学生网络的模型。教师网络使用概念概率引导FiLM调制器将视觉特征转化为语义编辑后的特征空间，学生网络仅使用图像即可复现该特征表示。该方法解决了领域自适应中语义不变性被忽略的问题，以及测试时元数据不可用的部署悖论，在跨数据集皮肤癌筛查任务上显著优于现有方法，尤其在黑色素瘤召回率方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI皮肤癌筛查模型从专家皮肤镜图像（源域）迁移到消费者级临床图像（目标域）时性能严重下降。现有领域自适应方法忽略了关键的语义不变量（如临床概念），而MONET等基础模型虽能提供密集概率评分形式的语义信息，但测试时该元数据不可用，形成了部署悖论。

Method: 提出CoFiDA-M特权信息框架：(1) 教师网络：利用MONET概念概率作为特权信息，通过FiLM调制器对视觉特征进行语义"编辑"；(2) 学生网络（轻量级，仅图像输入）：通过知识蒸馏学习复现教师网络的编辑后特征表示，而非仅模仿最终预测结果，将临床推理"烘焙"进学生网络权重中。

Result: 在多个数据集组成的挑战性基准上，仅使用图像的学生网络显著优于现有最先进方法，尤其在黑色素瘤召回率方面表现突出，展现出强大的跨数据集鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CoFiDA-M提供了一个实用且通用的框架，能够利用噪声概率元数据作为特权信息，在测试时无需额外元数据即可实现高性能，展现了在皮肤科以外的真实世界部署潜力。

Abstract: Models for AI-based skin cancer screening suffer a severe performance drop when shifting from expert dermoscopic (source) images to consumer-grade clinical (target) images, hindering real-world deployment. Existing domain adaptation methods often ignore crucial semantic invariants, such as clinical concepts. While new foundation models like MONET can provide this semantic information as dense, probabilistic scores, this metadata is unavailable at test time, creating a deployment paradox for practical image-only screening tools. We address this gap by proposing CoFiDA-M, a privileged information framework that learns from concepts at training time but deploys as an image-only model. Our method trains a teacher network that uses MONET concept probabilities to guide a FiLM modulator, transforming visual features into a semantically ``edited" feature space. A lightweight, image-only student is then trained to reproduce this edited representation, not just the teacher's final predictions. This distillation ``bakes" the clinical reasoning into the student's weights. On a challenging multi-dataset benchmark, our image-only student significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, especially in melanoma recall. Our work provides a practical and generalizable framework for leveraging noisy, probabilistic metadata as privileged information, demonstrating strong cross-dataset robustness and potential for real-world deployment beyond dermatology. Implementation code is available at: https://github.com/mmu-dermatology-research/CoFiDA.git

</details>


### [80] [Learning Global Motion with Compact Gaussians for Feed-Forward 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31595)
*Mungyeom Kim,Minkyeong Jeon,Honggyu An,Jaewoo Jung,Hyuna Ko,Jisang Han,Hyeonseo Yu,Donghwan Shin,Sunghwan Hong,Takuya Narihira,Kazumi Fukuda,Yuki Mitsufuji,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C4G提出了一种基于可学习高斯查询令牌的4D重建框架，通过紧凑的令牌集合聚合全时间上下文特征，实现全局一致的运动建模，并引入视频扩散模型增强渲染质量，在无需相机位姿的情况下以更少高斯实现强新视角合成和运动建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈方法逐帧逐像素预测3D高斯，存在重复高斯和视角依赖偏差问题，阻碍场景运动的有效学习。

Method: C4G构建于一组紧凑的时间戳条件可学习高斯查询令牌上，每个令牌聚合全时间上下文特征并解码出位置受目标时间戳调制的3D高斯；引入视频扩散模型增强渲染；将特征提升扩展到4D特征场以支持点跟踪和动态场景理解。

Result: C4G在显著减少高斯数量的情况下实现强新视角合成性能，无需相机位姿，展现出更强的运动建模能力和对大时间间隔的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于紧凑可学习查询令牌的4D重建框架能够有效解决前馈方法中的重复高斯和视角偏差问题，实现全局一致的运动建模，并具有扩展到更广泛动态场景理解任务的潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing feed-forward methods predict 3D Gaussians pixel-wise for each frame, suffering from duplicated Gaussians and view-dependent biases that hinder effective learning of scene motion. We present C4G, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework built upon a compact set of timestamp-conditioned learnable Gaussian query tokens. Each token aggregates corresponding features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian whose position is modulated by the target timestamp, enabling globally coherent motion modeling without per-scene optimization. To capture fine-grained details, we further introduce a video diffusion model-based rendering enhancement module. Since our framework effectively aggregates features into Gaussians, we extend this capability to feature lifting, producing a 4D feature field that supports point tracking and dynamic scene understanding. C4G achieves strong novel-view synthesis performance using significantly fewer Gaussians and without requiring camera poses, while exhibiting stronger motion modeling and robustness to large temporal gaps.

</details>


### [81] [KLIP: localized distribution shift detection via KL-divergence with diffusion priors in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31596)
*Alireza Kheirandish,Jihoon Hong,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型先验与后验分布之间KL散度的OOD检测度量方法，无需校准数据或对偏移分布的了解，即可检测全图或局部OOD，并在肝脏CT肿瘤检测等任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法通常需要偏移分布的先验知识、难以检测细微或局部分布偏移，且操作在完整图像上而非逆问题中的间接测量上，存在局限性。

Method: 提出基于扩散先验与后验分布之间KL散度的OOD检测度量，无需校准数据或对偏移分布的了解，可检测全图OOD并定位图像内的OOD区域。

Result: 该度量能够检测细微且语义上有意义的分布偏移（如健康肝脏CT到有肿瘤肝脏CT的偏移），并能在不同类型的扩散模型、数据集和逆问题中泛化。

Conclusion: 提出的KL散度度量是一种无需先验知识、能检测细微微小偏移且可定位OOD区域的有效方法，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown promising performance as data-driven priors for computational imaging, as well as some capacity to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) images. However, existing approaches to OOD detection often require some knowledge of the shifted distribution, fail to detect subtle or localized distribution shifts, and operate on full images, rather than the indirect measurements available in inverse problems. We propose an OOD detection metric based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the diffusion prior and the posterior distribution, that (i) does not require any calibration data or knowledge of the shifted distribution, and (ii) can detect whole images as OOD as well as localize OOD patches within an image. Experimentally, we show that this metric can detect subtle yet semantically meaningful distribution shifts, such as the shift from healthy liver CT scans to those with tumors, and generalizes across different types of diffusion models, datasets, and inverse problems. Our code can be found at https://github.com/voilalab/KLIP.

</details>


### [82] [SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31597)
*Olaf Dünkel,Basavaraj Sunagad,Haoran Wang,David T. Hoffmann,Christian Theobalt,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SOCO基准，一个包含100个类别、超过100万对应对的语义对应基准，带有功能意义的关键点标注和语言描述，用于系统评估视觉基础模型和大型视觉语言模型的结构化部分级理解能力。实验表明：视觉基础骨干编码了强语义结构但跨类别对应迁移差；LVLMs在文本提示定位上强于跨图像视觉匹配；对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测密集下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型中结构化物体理解的评估因不一致的评估协议和有限的部分级监督而面临挑战。现有方法缺乏系统性的评估框架，尤其是在跨实例和跨类别下物体部分匹配的能力评估上存在不足。

Method: 提出SOCO基准，引入对应类型分类法，在100个类别上提供超过100万对功能一致的关键点标注，并包含关键点语言描述以支持大视觉语言模型评估。

Result: (i)视觉基础骨干编码强语义结构但跨类别对应迁移差且仅部分捕获部件位置；(ii)LVLMs在文本提示定位上强于跨图像视觉匹配，显示语言定位与细粒度视觉对应之间存在差距；(iii)对应性能比分ImageNet分类更能预测分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计和3D检测等密集下游任务性能。

Conclusion: SOCO可作为评估视觉和多模态基础模型中结构化、部分级表示质量的基准，揭示了当前模型在部分理解上的局限性和未来改进方向。

Abstract: Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [83] [Linear Scaling Video VLMs for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31598)
*Cristobal Eyzaguirre,Jiajun Wu,Juan Carlos Niebles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StateKV是一种推理时方法，通过固定容量、基于重要性的循环状态携带跨帧上下文，将预训练的长视频VLM适配为线性时间视频预填充，在不微调或改变架构的情况下，在长视频基准测试中接近全自注意力性能，并优于滑动窗口等方法，同时降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频VLM编码器依赖时空自注意力，计算量和延迟随帧数呈二次增长；现有的效率方法（如激进丢帧/丢token或粗粒度注意力近似）虽提升了可扩展性但损失了准确性。

Method: 提出StateKV方法，在推理时采用固定容量、基于重要性的循环状态来携带跨帧上下文，实现线性时间视频预填充；同时保留第二个完整的逐帧缓存用于解码。

Result: 在三个长视频基准测试、七个模型（涵盖三个系列和多个规模）上，StateKV性能接近全自注意力，持续优于主流的滑动窗口/基于近期性的流式近似方法；同时减少了视频预填充的FLOPs计算成本，可在固定预算下运行更大模型以获得更强精度。

Conclusion: StateKV为可扩展的长视频理解提供了实用的一步，无需微调或架构改动即可实现效率与精度的良好平衡。

Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in long-horizon and streaming settings, yet most video encoders still rely on spatiotemporal self-attention, causing compute and latency to grow quadratically with the number of frames. Existing efficiency methods improve scalability but often lose accuracy relative to full self-attention, for example through aggressive frame/token dropping or coarse attention approximations. We introduce StateKV, an inference-time method that adapts pretrained long-video VLMs to linear-time video prefill by carrying cross-frame context in a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state, paired with a second full per-frame cache used for decoding. Across three long-video benchmarks and seven models spanning three families and multiple scales, StateKV remains close to full self-attention and consistently outperforms dominant sliding-window / recency-based streaming approximations, without fine-tuning or architectural changes. StateKV also reduces video-prefill cost measured FLOPs, enabling stronger accuracy at a fixed compute budget by running larger models. These results suggest a practical step toward scalable long-video understanding.

</details>


### [84] [Representation Forcing for Bottleneck-Free Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31604)
*Yuqing Wang,Zhijie Lin,Ceyuan Yang,Yang Zhao,Fei Xiao,Hao He,Qi Zhao,Zihan Ding,Fuyun Wang,Shuai Wang,Youliang Zhang,Haoqi Fan,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出表示强制（RF）技术，使统一多模态模型无需依赖预训练VAE，通过让解码器自回归预测视觉表示作为中间token，再引导像素扩散，实现了高效的无瓶颈端到端图像理解与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMMs）仍然依赖冻结的、单独预训练的VAE进行图像生成，这引入了结构瓶颈。直接移除VAE会导致质量下降，因为模型需要从原始像素同时学习高层结构和低层细节。

Method: 提出表示强制（Representation Forcing, RF）技术：强制解码器自回归地预测视觉表示作为中间token，这些token保留在上下文中，在同一骨干网络内引导像素扩散。将表示从感知输出转化为生成目标，消除外部生成潜空间的需求。

Result: 在图像生成任务上，基于像素空间的RF模型达到了与基于VAE的最先进统一模型相当的效果；在图像理解任务上，基于像素空间的RF模型通常优于基于VAE的变体。

Conclusion: RF技术有效消除了VAE带来的结构瓶颈，实现了端到端的无瓶颈统一多模态模型，同时提升了理解和生成两种能力。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to handle perception and generation in a single model. Yet existing UMMs still rely on a frozen, separately pretrained VAE for image generation, imposing a structural bottleneck. Naively removing it introduces a quality gap, as the model must learn both high-level structure and low-level details from raw pixels. In this paper, we propose Representation Forcing (RF), a technique that closes this gap by making representation prediction a native capability of the model. Concretely, RF forces the decoder to autoregressively predict visual representations as intermediate tokens before pixels; these tokens then stay in context to guide pixel diffusion within the same backbone. By turning representations from perception outputs into generation targets, RF eliminates the need for any external generative latent space. We find that RF benefits both understanding and generation. On image generation, our pixel-space model with RF matches state-of-the-art VAE-based unified models. On image understanding, pixel-space RF generally outperforms its VAE-based variant. Together, these results offer an effective step toward end-to-end, bottleneck-free UMMs.

</details>


### [85] [LVSA: Training-Free Sparse Attention for Long Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31057)
*Gael Glorian,Ioannis Lamprou,Zhen Zhang,Yujie Yuan,Hongsheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LVSA，一种无需训练的、模型无关的块稀疏注意力机制，用于长视频扩散Transformer。通过结合结构化窗口模式与旋转全局锚点，LVSA在保持生成质量的同时显著降低计算成本（最高3.33x加速），并支持在单GPU上生成长于训练视界的视频，同时提出了VQeval工具以更公平地评估视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集自注意力在长视频扩散推理中成为计算和质量的瓶颈：计算量随序列长度二次增长，且超出训练视界时模型输出趋于"冻结"的重复视频。现有方法要么成本过高（如需要重训练），要么无法在性能和质量目标上实现可扩展的兼顾。

Method: LVSA是一种训练无关、模型无关的块稀疏注意力机制，将结构化窗口模式与旋转全局锚点相结合，消除了导致长程时间伪影的固定网格偏置。结合FlashInfer内核实现高效推理。

Result: 在Wan 2.1 1.3B上6x视界实现3.17x加速，在Wan 2.1 14B上6x视界实现2.98x加速，在HunyuanVideo 1.5上1.5x视界实现3.33x加速。相比RIFLEx和UltraViCo分别提升2.41x和3.27x。在NPU上Wan 2.2 A14B和Wan 2.1 1.3B分别实现2.71x和3.24x加速。LVSA在训练视界长度下质量中性，在扩展长度下质量正向。

Conclusion: LVSA无需重新训练即可有效加速长视频扩散Transformer推理，支持更长的生成视界，且质量保持或提升。同时提出的VQeval工具可更公平地评估视频循环伪影，弥补了现有评估指标的不足。

Abstract: Dense self-attention is the compute and quality bottleneck of long-video diffusion inference: cost grows quadratically with the sequence length, and beyond the training horizon the model converges to near-static output, that is, "frozen" repetitive video. State of the art approaches are either too costly, e.g., they require retraining, or fail to satisfy both performance and quality objectives in a scalable manner. To this end, we introduce Long Video Sparse Attention (LVSA), a training-free model-agnostic block-sparse attention for video diffusion transformers that combines a structured window pattern with rotating global anchors, thus removing the fixed-grid bias which causes long-range temporal artifacts. LVSA, combined with a FlashInfer kernel, reduces compute up to 3.17x on Wan 2.1 1.3B at a 6x horizon, 2.98x on Wan 2.1 14B at a 6x horizon, and 3.33x on HunyuanVideo 1.5 at a 1.5x horizon, compared to dense attention. Beyond reducing compute, LVSA enables HunyuanVideo 1.5 generation at a 2x horizon, which is otherwise out-of-memory on a single GPU. Moreover, LVSA provides speedups up to 2.41x compared to RIFLEx and 3.27x compared to UltraViCo on Wan 2.1 1.3B. To demonstrate applicability across diverse platforms, we apply LVSA on NPUs and achieve speedups up to 2.71x on Wan 2.2 A14B and 3.24x on Wan 2.1 1.3B compared to dense attention. To evaluate quality in a fair way, we introduce VQeval, a tool properly scoring loopy video failures, which instead are rewarded in state of the art evaluators like VBench-Long. LVSA is quality-neutral for generation at training horizon length and quality-positive at extended lengths.

</details>


### [86] [Light Interaction: Training-Free Inference Acceleration for Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31158)
*Jiacheng Lu,Haoyi Zhu,Sipei Yi,Enze Xie,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Light Interaction是一种无需训练的推理加速框架，通过自适应上下文管理、去噪缓存加速和软硬件协同设计的3D块稀疏注意力，在交互式视频世界模型中实现高达2.59倍加速，同时保持视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型在生成长交互轨迹时面临高昂计算成本，包括上下文内存增长、二次注意力复杂度和重复去噪步骤，亟需高效的推理加速方法。

Method: 提出Light Interaction框架，核心思路是利用交互特性实现轨迹依赖的自适应计算：探索新区域时丢弃已检索空间记忆、根据局部潜在动态调整时间上下文、相机回归熟悉区域时重用早期模型输出。具体包含自适应上下文管理、去噪缓存加速和基于Triton内核的软硬件协同3D块稀疏注意力。

Result: 在HY-WorldPlay和Matrix-Game-3.0基准上，Light Interaction实现最高2.59倍加速，无需模型重训练，同时保持有竞争力的视觉生成质量。

Conclusion: 利用交互式世界模型中的自然操作特性实现自适应计算，可以有效降低推理成本，为实时游戏模拟、虚拟场景导航和具身AI训练等应用提供实用加速方案。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate video chunk by chunk in response to user-controlled camera movements, enabling applications such as real-time game simulation, virtual scene navigation, and embodied AI training. However, scaling to long interactive trajectories is prohibitively expensive due to growing context memory, quadratic attention complexity, and repeated denoising steps. We present Light Interaction, a training-free inference acceleration framework for interactive video world models. Our key insight is that interaction naturally enables trajectory-dependent adaptive computation: retrieved spatial memory can be discarded during novel exploration, temporal context can be adjusted according to local latent dynamics, and early-step model outputs can be reused when the camera revisits familiar regions. Based on this insight, Light Interaction combines adaptive context management, denoising cache acceleration, and hardware-software co-designed 3D block sparse attention with fused Triton kernels. Evaluated on HY-WorldPlay and Matrix-Game-3.0, Light Interaction achieves up to 2.59x speedup without model retraining while maintaining competitive visual quality.

</details>


### [87] [From Local Geometry to Global Pseudo Labeling for Robust Positive Unlabeled Learning under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31187)
*Firas Gabetni,Alexandre Rocchi Henry,Nacim Belkhir,Ziyi Liu,Gianni Franchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPUNA框架，利用正无标签（PU）学习在弱监督条件下检测协变量偏移，通过几何感知的局部流形结构逐步发现偏移数据，在无需完全标注的情况下达到了与全监督方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量偏移检测对构建可靠的视觉系统至关重要，但现有方法通常依赖全监督训练（需要来自原始分布和偏移分布的标注样本），这在实际中往往不可行。本文旨在探索如何利用更弱的监督（正无标签学习）来有效检测协变量偏移。

Method: 提出Spectral PU Neighborhood Annotation (SPUNA)框架，利用视觉特征的局部流形结构，通过几何感知的方式逐步发现偏移数据，解决经典PU方法在协变量偏移下分布重叠严重导致的噪声敏感和不稳定问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，SPUNA在PU设置下达到最先进水平，且与全监督方法性能相当。该方法在不同类型的偏移下均表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移检测可以在弱监督（PU学习）条件下有效实现，SPUNA通过几何感知的流形结构利用，克服了经典PU方法在分布重叠情况下的局限性，为实际场景中缺乏标注数据的偏移检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Detecting covariate shift is critical for building reliable vision systems. While most prior work focuses on improving robustness to shift, explicitly detecting covariate shift remains underexplored. Existing approaches typically rely on fully supervised training, requiring labeled examples from both original and shifted distributions, which is often impractical. In this paper, we show that covariate shift detection can be effectively addressed with weaker supervision using Positive Unlabeled (PU) learning. However, under covariate shift, in distribution and shifted data overlap significantly, making classical PU methods unstable and sensitive to noise. To overcome this challenge, we introduce Spectral PU Neighborhood Annotation (SPUNA), a geometry aware framework that progressively discovers shifted data by leveraging the local manifold structure of visual features. Extensive experiments show that SPUNA achieves state of the art performance in PU settings and remarkably matches the performances of fully supervised methods. Moreover, our approach transfers robustly across different types of shifts, demonstrating strong generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [88] [Latent Geometric Chords for Query-Efficient Decision-Based Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31219)
*Ei Hmue Khine,Yao Li,Jiebao Sun,Shengzhu Shi,Zhichang Guo,Boying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Geometric Chords (LGC)方法，通过曲率感知的几何搜索在压缩语义流形中导航决策边界，并引入残差对抗生成(RAG)机制以提升视觉保真度和搜索空间维度，在决策级黑盒对抗攻击中实现了SOTA性能(SSIM>0.99, LPIPS<0.01)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策级黑盒对抗攻击存在根本性局限：像素级攻击引入不自然的高频伪影；潜空间方法受限于低维流形的有限搜索空间和固有的重建缺陷。亟需一种能同时解决视觉保真度和搜索空间瓶颈的方法。

Method: 提出LGC方法，核心是在压缩语义流形上执行曲率感知的几何搜索以导航决策边界；同时引入残差对抗生成(RAG)机制，将语义扰动隔离为几何弦并叠加到原始图像上，解决重建缺陷并有效倍增搜索空间维度。

Result: LGC在5000次查询下SSIM超过0.99、LPIPS低于0.01；实现最小扰动幅度和SOTA视觉保真度；保持高攻击成功率，成功攻破对抗训练的鲁棒模型；展现出强大的跨数据集迁移性。

Conclusion: LGC有效克服了像素级和潜空间方法的双重局限，通过几何弦和残差生成机制实现了查询高效、高保真的决策级黑盒攻击，为对抗攻击领域提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: While decision-based black-box adversarial attacks present a severe security threat, current methodologies suffer from fundamental limitations. Pixel-wise attacks frequently introduce unnatural, high-frequency visual artifacts, while latent-space frameworks are confined by the limited search space of low-dimensional manifolds and inherent reconstruction flaws. To resolve these limitations, we propose Latent Geometric Chords (LGC) for Query-Efficient Decision-Based Adversarial Attacks alongside a variant, LGC-H. At its core, LGC navigates decision boundaries by executing a curvature-aware geometric search within a compressed semantic manifold. To guarantee high visual fidelity and circumvent dimensionality bottlenecks, we introduce a Residual-based Adversarial Generation (RAG) mechanism. RAG isolates semantic perturbations as geometric chords and superimposes them directly onto the original source image. RAG substantially resolves baseline reconstruction flaws and effectively doubles the permissible search space dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that LGC achieves robust cross-dataset transferability and substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, our method, LGC, minimizes perturbation magnitudes while achieving state-of-the-art visual fidelity--with a Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) exceeding 0.99 and a Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS) below 0.01 at 5000 queries--and sustaining high attack success rates under stringent perceptual constraints, successfully compromising adversarially trained robust models. The source code is available at: https://github.com/eihmuekhine/Latent-Geometric-Chords.

</details>

### [89] [ZAPS-DA: Zero-Phase Action Policy Smoothing with Decoupled Actor for Continuous Control in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30612)
*Faiq Shamass*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZAPS-DA通过解耦的监督学习分支从零相位滤波目标中蒸馏平滑动作策略，在不引入相位滞后和后处理的情况下显著降低强化学习策略的动作抖动（14-45x），同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习训练的连续控制策略常产生高频动作抖动，直接部署不可行；事后滤波引入相位滞后，嵌入平滑惩罚会与RL梯度耦合并混淆回归目标与过度平滑。

Method: 将主actor（由基础RL损失训练）与一个解耦的decoupled actor分离；后者通过监督学习模仿存放在回放缓冲区中的零相位滤波（Savitzky-Golay）目标动作，实现非因果滤波器的因果蒸馏；使用幅度匹配的MSE损失实现零超参数迁移。

Result: 在MetaDrive中转向抖动减少14-21x、油门抖动减少3-5x（p<0.0001），任务完成率无显著差异，奖励损失6.3%；在Webots自适应巡航中实现Pareto改进：奖励持平，转向抖动减少8-45x，任务失败率从2.0%降至0.7%。

Conclusion: ZAPS-DA是一种即插即用的框架，无需修改主actor训练流程、无需推理时滤波或动作历史输入，即可显著抑制动作抖动且几乎不引入相位滞后。

Abstract: Continuous control policies trained with off-policy reinforcement learning frequently exhibit high-frequency action jitter, rendering direct deployment on physical actuators impractical. Post-hoc filtering attenuates jitter but introduces phase lag; embedding smoothness penalties in the actor's loss couples them with the RL gradient and conflates reward regression with over-aggressive smoothing. We present ZAPS-DA, a framework that reduces action jitter at deployment with negligible phase lag and no post-processing. ZAPS-DA pairs an unmodified main actor (trained by the base RL loss) with a separate decoupled actor trained via supervised imitation of zero-phase filtered targets stored in the replay buffer. The deployed policy is the decoupled actor: a feed-forward map from the current observation to a smooth action, with no inference-time filter and no action-history input -- a mechanism we term causal distillation of a non-causal filter. A magnitude-matched MSE loss provides zero-hyperparameter portability across optimizer classes. Validated with Soft Actor-Critic and a Savitzky--Golay filter in two driving simulators using paired n=150 evaluation protocols: on MetaDrive, ZAPS-DA reduces steering jitter by 14--21x and throttle jitter by 3--5x (all $p < 10^{-4}$, Bonferroni-corrected) while matching task-completion (p=0.28 success, p=0.31 crash) at a 6.3% reward cost; on a custom Webots adaptive cruise control environment, the same SG configuration produces a Pareto improvement -- reward parity (p=0.121), 8--45x steering jitter reduction, and total task-failure rate reduced from 2.0% to 0.7%.

</details>


### [90] [Don't Fool Me Twice: Adapting to Adversity in the Wild with Experience-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31119)
*Navin Sriram Ravie,Andrew Jong,Krrish Jain,John Liu,Omar Alama,Bijo Sebastian,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"Don't Fool Me Twice"框架，让移动机器人在线学习干扰事件，通过语义分析归因异常行为，利用核回归和语义体素建模实现小样本异常表征与不确定性估计，支持跨 embodiments 的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大视觉语言模型（VLM）的常识危险预测难以捕捉与具体机器人交互和 embodiment 相关的对抗性因素，未结构化环境中无法预知所有危险。

Method: 三阶段流程：1) 观察干扰并描述其对机器人的影响；2) 结合视觉上下文查询VLM预测可能原因；3) 使用核回归对局部干扰进行高效小样本建模。同时采用语义体素中心建模估计认知不确定性，将交互驱动的干扰视为可学习的空间行为。

Result: 在仿真和实物平台上跨不同 embodiment 和对抗模式验证了四项假设的有效性。

Conclusion: 在线持续学习框架能使移动机器人通过语义归因从干扰中学习，提升对未知环境的预测与规划能力，尤其适用于 embodiment 相关的交互式对抗场景。

Abstract: In robotics, dangers and adversity modes are often embodiment-specific and relative to each agent. A frontier of autonomous mobile robotics is to enable agents to operate effectively in the wild in unseen unstructured environments. A significant challenge in unseen unstructured environments is that it may not be possible to predict all the dangers to the specific robot. Although recent work has used large foundation vision-language models (VLMs) to preemptively predict an exhaustive list of common-sense dangers, it remains difficult to capture possible interaction and embodiment-dependent adversities. We propose a continual learning framework for a mobile embodied agent to learn online from disturbances and attribute anomalous behaviours to causes through semantics, enabling better prediction and planning of the world in the future. Our framework, "Don't Fool Me Twice", first observes disturbances and describes their effects on the robot; this description is augmented with visual context to query a VLM to predict possible causes; the local disturbance is characterized using kernel regression, which allows for efficient, few-shot modeling of transient anomalies. We leverage semantic voxel-centric modeling to estimate epistemic uncertainty, enabling richer downstream recovery by treating interaction-driven disturbances as learnable spatial behaviors. We present four hypotheses and validate them in simulation and on hardware across embodiments and adversity modes.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Gait2Hip-60: A Unified Deep Learning Benchmark for Predicting Hip Muscle Forces and Joint Moments from Multi-Cadence Gait Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30374)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Ji Hou,Qing Sun,Xianzhi Gao,Bo Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于深度学习（LSTM、Transformer、Mamba）的框架，直接从下肢步态运动学预测髋部肌肉力和关节力矩。在60名健康成人数据上，Transformer表现最佳（肌肉力R²=0.819，力矩R²=0.862），并在股骨头坏死患者的零样本外推测试中保持中等预测能力（R²≈0.55），表明该方法可行但需进一步病理验证以提高泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于肌骨仿真的髋关节动力学参数估计方法虽信息丰富但耗时且难以应用于临床，亟需一种更高效、可直接从步态运动学数据预测髋部肌肉力和关节力矩的方法。

Method: 采集60名健康成人在三种节拍器引导步频下的步态数据，以10个双侧下肢关节角度为输入，OpenSim导出的髋部肌肉力和关节力矩为参考输出；在统一协议下训练并比较LSTM、Transformer、Mamba三种深度学习模型；将最优模型直接在9名股骨头坏死患者上进行零样本外推测试。

Result: 健康受试者中Transformer表现最优：肌肉力预测RMSE=1.33 N/kg、MAE=0.57 N/kg、R²=0.819；力矩预测RMSE=0.11 Nm/kg、MAE=0.07 Nm/kg、R²=0.862。零样本外推至ONFH患者时，Transformer保持中等预测能力：肌肉力R²=0.537，力矩R²=0.569。

Conclusion: 从步态运动学估计髋关节动力学参数是可行的，Transformer可作为强基线模型，但在临床广泛应用前需更广泛的病理验证和泛化性改进。

Abstract: Estimating hip muscle forces and joint moments during gait typically relies on musculoskeletal simulation, which is informative but time-consuming and difficult to apply in clinical settings. This study developed a deep learning framework to predict these hip dynamics parameters directly from lower-limb gait kinematics and compared three representative sequence models under a unified protocol. Gait data were collected from 60 healthy adults under three metronome-guided cadence conditions. Ten bilateral lower-limb joint angles were used as inputs, and OpenSim-derived hip muscle forces and hip joint moments were used as reference outputs. Three deep learning models of LSTM, Transformer, and Mamba were trained and evaluated using the same subject-level split, preprocessing pipeline, and metrics. The best model was then directly tested on an external cohort of 9 patients with osteonecrosis of the femoral head (ONFH) without retraining. In the healthy-subject benchmark, Transformer achieved the best subject-level mean performance for both hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.33 N/kg, MAE = 0.57 N/kg, R2 = 0.819) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.11 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.07 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.862), with similar advantages across walking cadences. In zero-shot external validation, Transformer retained moderate predictive ability in ONFH for hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.51 N/kg, MAE = 0.70 N/kg, R2 = 0.537) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.17 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.12 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.569). These findings support the feasibility of estimating hip dynamics from gait kinematics, identify Transformer as a strong baseline, and highlight the need for broader pathological validation and improved generalization before clinical application.

</details>


### [92] [When LLMs Learn to Be Consistently Wrong: A Multi-Model Study of Linear Representations of Synthetic Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30381)
*Vahideh Zolfaghari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多模型对比研究，发现通过LoRA微调可在早期层（1-3层）实现近乎完美的合成不诚实检测（AUC≥0.99，Pythia除外），且不诚实表征呈现领域不变性和早期固化特征，揭示了两种表征机制：表征坍塌（Pythia/Llama/Qwen）与高维保持（Gemma-2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗性对齐（模型保持正确内部表征但故意输出错误结果）是AI安全的核心挑战。虽然战略性欺骗是主要长期担忧，但合成不诚实（通过对错误答案的直接优化诱导）为研究习得性欺骗的表征基础提供了受控实验平台。

Method: 采用多模型范式，对五种Transformer模型（Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B）使用LoRA在相同问题分布上微调诚实与不诚实变体。使用线性探针（基于平均池化隐藏状态）检测合成不诚实，并比较逻辑回归与MLP探针性能。通过Fisher判别比、有效秩、质心几何、方向稳定性、跨域对齐和校准误差（ECE）进行机制分析。

Result: 线性探针在四种架构的1-3层实现近完美AUC（≥0.99），Pythia最高0.705。逻辑回归探针一致匹配或超越MLP探针。TruthfulQA训练的探针可零损失泛化至MMLU。深层表征对高斯噪声有强鲁棒性，Gemma-2稳定性最优。发现两种机制：Pythia/Llama/Qwen的维度坍塌与Gemma-2的高维保存。不诚实方向随层数加深而固化，1-4层可达最优校准（ECE<0.01，Pythia除外）。

Conclusion: 稳健、领域不变的不诚实表征可通过适度监督微调快速固化，对基于激活的监控方法具有重要启示。

Abstract: Deceptive alignment, in which models maintain accurate internal representations while deliberately producing false outputs, remains a central challenge in AI safety. While strategic deception is the primary long-term concern, synthetic dishonesty - induced via direct optimization on incorrect answers - provides a controlled testbed for studying the representational basis of learned deception. We introduce a multi-model paradigm in which honest and deceptive variants of five transformer models (Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B) are fine-tuned using LoRA on the same question distribution. Linear probes trained on mean-pooled hidden states detect synthetic dishonesty with near-perfect AUC (greater than or equal to 0.99) as early as layers 1-3 in four architectures, while Pythia-1.4B reaches a peak of 0.705. Logistic regression probes consistently match or outperform MLP probes, supporting the Linear Representation Hypothesis. Probes trained on TruthfulQA generalize with near-zero loss (Delta AUC approx. 0) to held-out MMLU subjects. Late-layer representations show strong robustness to Gaussian noise, with Gemma-2 models exhibiting exceptional stability. Mechanistic analysis of Fisher Discriminant Ratio, effective rank, centroid geometry, directional stability, cross-domain alignment, and calibration (ECE) reveals two regimes: representational collapse in Pythia/Llama/Qwen versus high-dimensional preservation in Gemma-2. Across all models, the dishonesty direction consolidates progressively in deeper layers, with optimal calibration (ECE less than 0.01 except Pythia) achievable in layers 1-4. These results demonstrate that robust, domain-invariant dishonesty representations can be rapidly entrenched via modest supervised fine-tuning, with implications for activation-based monitoring.

</details>


### [93] [LLMs Without Deep Neural Networks: New Architecture, Benefits and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30385)
*Vincent Granville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RBF网络的深度神经网络替代方案，该方案可以在单次迭代中以闭式解找到损失函数的全局最优解，无需传统DNN的繁琐训练步骤，并提供了案例研究和与类似方法的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为作者独立发现的深度神经网络替代方案提供验证，该方案基于RBF网络机制，旨在提高LLM上下文中的可解释性和准确性，同时消除耗时的训练步骤。

Method: 提出一种基于RBF网络的模型，与标准DNN不同，该模型以闭式解在一次迭代中找到损失函数的全局最优解，无需训练过程。

Result: 该模型不需要深度神经网络，能够在单次迭代中通过闭式解找到全局最优，消除了繁琐的训练步骤。

Conclusion: 该技术作为DNN的替代方案，在LLM上下文中具有更高的可解释性和准确性，且训练效率大幅提升。

Abstract: The purpose of this article is to provide validation to my deep neural network alternative in the context of LLMs. Very recently, there has been a significant interest by Chinese researchers in a model called RBF network, as a substitute to standard DNNs, with increased explainability and higher accuracy. It turns out that my new model, discovered independently, is based on the exact same machinery. But with a major twist: it does not need DNN as it finds the global optimum of the loss function in closed form, in one iteration, thus eliminating the tedious training step. Here I provide a high-level overview of my technology, with case study and comparison to similar methods.

</details>


### [94] [LongDS-Bench: On the Failure of Long-Horizon Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434)
*Kewei Xu,Xiaoben Lu,Shuofei Qiao,Zihan Ding,Haoming Xu,Lei Liang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongDS是一个用于评估长程、多轮数据分析能力的基准测试，包含68个任务（2225轮对话），覆盖地理科学、商业、教育等6个领域。实验表明，最佳模型平均准确率仅48.45%，从早期到后期回合性能下降近47个百分点，52%-69%的失败源于长程错误，且增加交互轮次并非关键瓶颈，核心在于维护正确的分析状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估孤立或短交互任务，无法测试智能体在长程多轮数据中跟踪演化分析上下文的能力，因此需要构建一个专门评估长程状态跟踪与维护的基准。

Method: 从真实Kaggle notebooks构建68个长程多轮数据分析任务（2225轮），围绕状态演化模式（反事实扰动、回滚、多状态组合等）设计，平均依赖跨度11.3轮。评估5个先进模型。

Result: 最佳模型平均准确率仅48.45%；从早期到后期回合性能下降近47个百分点；52%-69%的失败由长程错误导致；增加智能体步骤不一定提升性能。

Conclusion: 当前模型在长程数据分析中表现不佳，关键瓶颈在于维护正确的分析状态而非增加交互预算，LongDS可用于推动可靠长程智能体数据分析的研究。

Abstract: Real-world data analysis is inherently iterative, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate isolated or short interactive tasks, leaving agents' ability to track evolving analytical context over long horizons untested. We introduce LongDS, a benchmark for long-horizon, multi-turn data analysis where agents must maintain, update, restore, and compose evolving analytical states. LongDS comprises 68 tasks constructed from real-world Kaggle notebooks, spanning 2,225 turns across six domains including Geoscience, Business, and Education. Tasks are designed around state-evolution patterns (e.g., counterfactual perturbation, rollback, multi-state composition), with an average dependency span of 11.3 turns. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models, we find that the best model reaches only 48.45% average accuracy, performance drops nearly 47 points from early to late turns, and long-horizon errors account for 52%--69% of failures. Further analysis shows that additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance, suggesting that the key bottleneck is maintaining a correct analytical state rather than increasing interaction budget. We release LongDS to support research on reliable long-horizon agentic data analysis. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/DataMind.

</details>


### [95] [Calibrated Preference Learning: The Case of Label Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30447)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Viktor Bengs,Timo Kaufmann,Sebastian J. Vollmer,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化定义了概率化标签排序中的校准问题，构建了涵盖完整排序、子排序和top-k排序的校准层次体系，并通过理论证明和实验验证揭示了不同类型校准之间的关系及现有模型在校准方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准在分类和回归任务中已被广泛研究，但在概率化标签排序（预测标签集排序上的分布）中尚未被正式定义。简单地视排序为类别会忽略其结构性，无法捕捉成对和top-k预测等重要模态，因此需要为该任务建立正式的校准理论框架。

Method: 1) 形式化定义标签排序的校准概念；2) 建立涵盖完整排序、子排序和top-k排序的校准层次体系；3) 理论证明完整排序校准蕴含其他校准但反之不成立，且子排序与top-k校准不可比较；4) 在流行标签排序模型上进行实验评估；5) 将框架应用于RLHF奖励模型进行实证分析。

Result: 1) 完整排序校准蕴含子排序和top-k校准，但反之不成立；子排序校准与top-k校准之间不可比较。2) 流行的标签排序模型普遍校准不佳，子排序与top-k指标存在显著差异。3) RLHF奖励模型中，校准性与基准准确率强相关但并非完全一致，表明校准捕捉了超越top-1准确率的有意义质量维度。

Conclusion: 本文为标签排序校准奠定了理论基础，揭示了现有模型在校准方面的不足，并激励未来研究探索校准不足的下游影响及开发校准修正方法。

Abstract: Calibration, the alignment of predicted probabilities with true outcome frequencies, is essential for reliable decision-making. While extensively studied for classification and regression, calibration has not been formally addressed for probabilistic label ranking, where the goal is to predict a distribution over orderings of a label set. Naively treating rankings as classes ignores their structure and fails to capture important modalities such as pairwise and top-k predictions. We formalize calibration for label ranking and develop a hierarchy of notions covering full rankings, sub-rankings, and top-k rankings. We prove that full-rank calibration implies the others but not conversely, and sub-ranking and top-k calibration are incomparable. Empirically, we find popular label ranking models are often poorly calibrated, with substantial differences between sub-ranking and top-k metrics. Applying our framework to RLHF reward models, we find that calibration correlates strongly but not perfectly with benchmark accuracy, suggesting it captures a meaningful quality dimension beyond top-1 accuracy. These findings motivate future work on understanding the downstream effects of miscalibration and developing methods to correct it.

</details>


### [96] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有界行为不可区分性（bounded behavioral indistinguishability）作为黑盒LLM蒸馏的评估框架，并通过实验证明语义相似度不足以衡量蒸馏质量，需要对抗性和类别感知的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黑盒LLM蒸馏评估仅关注输出相似性或任务一致性，但这无法保证学生模型在行为上与教师模型不可区分。作者希望提出一个更严格的评估框架来捕捉行为层面的差距。

Method: 形式化定义了(ε,q,t,A)-行为不可区分性；在Qwen和Llama的师生对上进行实验，使用5000条提示的行为探测套件，比较基础学生与LoRA蒸馏学生，采用语义相似度、对抗鉴别器、成对类别分析及A/B交换一致性过滤等指标。

Result: LoRA蒸馏提升了语义相似度（Qwen: 0.788→0.862；Llama: 0.814→0.874），但对抗评估显示行为差异仍然存在；学习型鉴别器保持非零优势，风格/格式、鲁棒性、领域技术类提示存在人工痕迹；跨族Llama评判器下Qwen区分优势从0.158降至0.081；查询预算实验中，分歧引导采样未一致优于分层随机采样。

Conclusion: 语义保真度有用但不充分；黑盒LLM蒸馏需要有界、对抗性、类别感知的评估框架。

Abstract: Black-box LLM distillation is usually evaluated as an output-matching problem: a student is considered successful when its responses are semantically similar to, or task-consistent with, those of a teacher. However, output similarity does not imply that the student is behaviorally indistinguishable from the model it imitates. We introduce bounded behavioral indistinguishability, formalized as $(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability over an explicit prompt distribution, where $ε$ bounds distinguishing advantage, $q$ bounds oracle queries, $t$ bounds computation, and $\mathbb{A}$ denotes the adversary class.
  We instantiate this notion on Qwen and Llama teacher-student pairs using a controlled $5,000$-prompt behavioral probe suite. For each family, we compare the teacher with both the base student and the LoRA-distilled student, measuring whether distillation reduces distinguishability rather than merely improving similarity. LoRA raises semantic similarity from $0.788$ to $0.862$ for Qwen and from $0.814$ to $0.874$ for Llama. Yet adversarial evaluation reveals remaining behavioral differences: learned discriminators retain nonzero advantage, and pairwise category analysis shows artifacts concentrated in style/format, robustness, and domain-technical prompts. A pairwise teacher-identification adversary confirms this trend. With a different-family Llama judge and A/B-swap consistency filtering, Qwen distinguishing advantage drops from $0.158$ for the base student to $0.081$ after LoRA distillation. Query-budget experiments show that disagreement-guided acquisition does not consistently outperform stratified random sampling, indicating that coverage and diversity remain strong baselines. Our results show that semantic fidelity is useful but insufficient: black-box LLM distillation requires bounded, adversarial, and category-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [97] [VeriGate: Verifier-Gated Step-Level Supervision for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30451)
*Aakriti Agrawal,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriGate是一种基于验证器门控的GRPO扩展方法，通过在验证器奖励退化时引入过程监督、将PRM步骤分数转化为未来累积奖励、以及生成归一化的token级优势信号，解决了GRPO中奖励稀疏、梯度失效和信用分配不足的问题，在1.5B和7B模型上分别提升约20%和12%的推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在基于验证器的结果奖励训练推理模型时存在监督稀疏问题：当所有采样轨迹获得相同的验证器奖励时，组相对优势归零导致学习停滞；同时仅靠结果奖励无法提供步骤级的信用分配，限制了探索效率和学习鲁棒性。

Method: VeriGate采用三方面设计：(1) 验证器门控机制：当验证器奖励能产生有意义的偏好时由验证器主导，否则启用过程监督；(2) 将PRM步骤分数转化为未来累积奖励，实现延续感知的信用分配；(3) 将累积奖励转换为组归一化的token级优势信号，在保留丰富梯度的同时降低奖励破解风险。

Result: 在MATH数据集上使用Qwen2.5-Instruct 1.5B和7B模型训练，在六个推理基准上评估，1.5B模型平均准确率提升约20%，7B模型提升约12%；显著减少零梯度故障和奖励破解行为，推理质量相比结果级GRPO和PRM-as-outcome基线均有提升。

Conclusion: VeriGate通过验证器门控的步骤级GRPO有效解决了结果奖励稀疏问题，在提升推理性能的同时保持了对抗奖励破解的鲁棒性，为训练推理模型提供了更优的框架。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is an effective recipe for training reasoning models with verifier-based outcome rewards, but its supervision is sparse: when all sampled trajectories for a prompt receive the same verifier reward, the group-relative advantage collapses to zero and learning stalls. Outcome-only rewards also provide no step-level credit assignment, limiting exploration and making it harder to learn robust reasoning. We present VeriGate (Verifier-Gated Step-Level GRPO), a verifier-gated extension of GRPO that addresses these limitations with three design choices. First, VeriGate keeps the verifier in charge whenever verifier rewards induce a meaningful preference among sampled trajectories, and uses process supervision only when verifier rewards are degenerate. Second, instead of collapsing Process Reward Model (PRM) step scores into a single trajectory reward, VeriGate converts them into future-cumulated rewards to assign continuation-aware credit. Third, VeriGate transforms these rewards into group-normalized token-level advantages, restoring informative gradients and fine-grained credit assignment while remaining less susceptible to reward hacking than methods that optimize aggregated PRM scores. Empirically, training on MATH with 1.5B and 7B Qwen2.5-Instruct models and evaluating on six reasoning benchmarks, VeriGate improves average accuracy by about 20% and 12% for 1.5B and 7B models respectively, substantially reduces zero-gradient failures, decreases reward-hacking behavior, and improves reasoning quality relative to outcome-only GRPO and PRM-as-outcome baselines.

</details>


### [98] [A Unified Framework for Gradient Aggregation in Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30452)
*Zeou Hu,Kelvin Ho,Yaoliang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多目标优化中梯度聚合的统一框架，建立了收敛到帕累托驻点的（最优）速率，提出了充分对齐条件作为收敛的充分条件，并通过原始优化视角统一了现有算法并设计了新变体（如capped MGDA），在对抗联邦学习中验证了其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度多目标优化方法动机各异、分析方式不一，缺乏统一的收敛理论框架，阻碍了方法间的理论关系理解和新算法设计。

Method: 1. 提出充分对齐条件（sufficient alignment condition），证明在梯度凸包中选择非冲突方向是收敛的充分条件；2. 通过对偶锥投影确保可行性；3. 提出梯度聚合的原始优化视角，统一解释现有算法；4. 基于CVaR提出capped MGDA变体。

Result: 建立了多目标优化梯度聚合的统一收敛理论，证明了（最优）收敛速率，提出了capped MGDA算法并在对抗联邦学习中展示了鲁棒性，通过合成问题和实际基准实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 该统一框架不仅澄清了现有MOO方法间的理论关系，还为新算法设计提供了原则性指导，capped MGDA的提出验证了框架的实用性。

Abstract: Many machine learning problems involve multiple inherent trade-offs that are best addressed by gradient-based multi-objective optimization (MOO) algorithms. Existing methods are often proposed with various motivations, analyzed case by case, and differ algorithmically in how the component gradients are aggregated at each step. In this work, we develop a unifying framework for gradient aggregation in MOO, establishing (optimal) rates of convergence to Pareto stationarity, the standard measure of performance in MOO. Central to our analysis is a sufficient alignment condition, from which we derive a theorem showing that non-conflicting directions, when chosen within the convex hull of gradients, form a fundamental sufficient condition for convergence. We further show that feasibility can be ensured through projection onto the dual cone, broadening the scope of methods that admit convergence guarantees. In parallel, we present a primal optimization perspective of gradient aggregation that encompasses established algorithms, clarifies their theoretical relationships, and enables the design of new variants. As an illustration, we introduce capped MGDA, derived from a CVaR-based formulation, and demonstrate its robustness in adversarial federated learning. Finally, we validate our theory through experiments on synthetic problems and practical benchmarks.

</details>


### [99] [DisjunctiveNet: Neural Symbolic Learning via Differentiable Convexified Optimization Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30456)
*Shraman Pal,Can Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一端到端框架，通过在神经网络中嵌入可微分的层次凸松弛优化层，实现对输入相关的混合整数线性约束的硬性满足，在真实数据集上达到了完美的规则遵守和强 predictive 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学与工程中的学习任务常面临稀疏数据集，纯数据驱动方法效果有限；同时存在丰富的领域知识（物理定律、操作要求、专家启发式），通常以逻辑命题和线性不等式规则表达。现有神经符号方法存在软惩罚近似、架构设计假设规则独立于输入、或依赖推理时不可微后处理等问题，无法在端到端框架中硬性满足输入相关的混合整数规则。

Method: 将规则表示为析取约束，应用层次凸松弛获得凸包络公式，将可处理的线性约束嵌入为可微优化层，从而实现精确的规则满足。

Result: 在真实数据集上实现了完美的规则满足（hard constraint satisfaction）和强预测性能。

Conclusion: 提出统一端到端框架，将输入相关的混合整数线性约束硬性嵌入神经网络，通过层次凸松弛解决了非凸性问题，实验证明了有效性和规则零违反的可行性。

Abstract: Many learning tasks in science and engineering are characterized by sparse datasets, which limits the effectiveness of purely data-driven approaches. At the same time, these problems are often accompanied by rich domain knowledge derived from physical laws, operational requirements, and expert heuristics. Such knowledge is frequently expressed as rules involving logical propositions and linear inequalities. Existing neuro-symbolic methods typically enforce these rules approximately through soft penalties, assume input-independent rules when designing specialized architectures, or rely on non-differentiable post-processing at inference time to achieve hard constraint satisfaction. While recent advances in differentiable optimization layers enable end-to-end feasibility enforcement within neural networks, extending these approaches to logical or mixed-integer rules remains challenging due to inherent nonconvexity. In this work, we propose a unified end-to-end framework for enforcing hard, input-dependent mixed integer linear constraints within neural networks. Our approach represents rules as disjunctive constraints and applies hierarchical convex relaxations to obtain convex hull formulations. These relaxations yield tractable linear constraints that can be embedded as differentiable optimization layers while enabling exact rule satisfaction. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework on real-world datasets, achieving perfect rule satisfaction and strong predictive performance.

</details>


### [100] [Scalable Constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via State Augmentation and Consensus for Separable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30461)
*Santiago Amaya-Corredor,Miguel Calvo-Fullana,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种分布式约束多智能体强化学习方法，通过邻居间对偶变量共识实现全局约束满足，在保持线性可扩展性的同时避免了独立学习的不可行解问题，实验表明该方法可扩展到数千个智能体，而集中式训练分散执行方法仅能处理数十个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有可分离动力学但需满足全局资源约束的多智能体系统中，独立学习无法产生可行解，因为各智能体无法确定对集体约束的适当个体贡献；现有集中式训练分散执行方法复杂度随智能体数量呈二次增长，扩展性受限。

Method: 将状态增广策略学习与对偶变量上的分布式共识相结合：每个智能体学习一个单增广策略（以局部状态和编码约束反馈的对偶变量为条件），在执行阶段通过局部邻居通信达成对偶变量共识，在温和连通性假设下证明乘子间的共识误差有界，约束违反随图连通性和共识轮次减少。

Result: 在智能电网需求响应实验中，无共识时智能体仅能通过无限期推迟需求来满足电网容量约束（退化非解），而有共识时智能体收敛到共享对偶变量并同时满足电网约束和需求满足；该方法可扩展到数千个智能体，而CTDE基线仅为数十个。

Conclusion: 轻量级邻居间对偶乘子共识足以实现全局协调的约束执行，同时保持独立训练的可扩展性；共识协调对可行性至关重要。

Abstract: We present a distributed approach for constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) that combines state-augmented policy learning with distributed consensus over dual variables. Our method targets systems where agents have separable dynamics but must coordinate to satisfy global resource constraints, a setting in which, as we demonstrate empirically, independent learning fails to produce feasible solutions because agents cannot determine appropriate individual contributions toward collective constraint satisfaction. The key technical contribution is showing that lightweight neighbor-to-neighbor consensus over Lagrange multipliers suffices for globally coordinated constraint enforcement while preserving the scalability of independent training. Each agent learns a single augmented policy offline, conditioned on both its local state and a dual variable encoding constraint feedback. During execution, agents reach agreement on this dual variable through local communication alone. We prove that under mild connectivity assumptions, the consensus error among agents' multipliers is bounded, and show that this translates to a bounded constraint violation that decreases with graph connectivity and the number of consensus rounds. Unlike centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) approaches, whose complexity grows at least quadratically with agent count, our method scales linearly in both training and execution. Experiments on smart grid demand response demonstrate that consensus coordination is \emph{essential for feasibility}: without it, agents satisfy grid capacity constraints only by indefinitely postponing demand, a degenerate non-solution. With consensus, agents converge to a shared dual variable and satisfy both grid constraints and demand fulfillment, scaling to thousands of agents while CTDE baselines are limited to dozens.

</details>


### [101] [idSCD: Identifying Training Datasets through Semantic Correlation Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30462)
*Andrada Gobeaja,Ionut Hodoroaga,Elena Burceanu,Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语义相关性描述符（SCD）的白盒方法，利用模型在训练过程中从数据集学到的虚假相关（语义指纹）进行数据集级成员推断，在NLI、情感分类和医学文本分类三个场景中平均性能优于RMIA、Attack-P、LiRA等基线方法，最大相对提升超过60% ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集成员推断方法依赖行为或分布证据（如置信度、损失、生成样本等），本文探索能否通过模型学到的语义相关结构（数据集特有的虚假相关）来识别数据集是否参与训练。

Method: 提出语义相关性描述符（SCD）捕获模型学到的语义相关结构；设计基于SCD的成员分数，仅需目标模型的SCD和独立目标数据集的SCD即可判定成员身份，无需留出法模型。

Result: 在留一数据集诊断中SCD完美分离匹配与非匹配数据集对；在NLI、情感分类、医学文本分类三个场景中，SCD分类器平均性能最高、标准差最低，优于RMIA、Attack-P、LiRA和SIF基线，最大相对ROC-AUC提升超60%。

Conclusion: 数据集成员身份可通过模型内部的语义相关结构进行追溯，语义分离度越大的数据集组别优势越显著。

Abstract: Can a dataset be recognized from the spurious correlations it induces during training? We argue that datasets leave dataset-specific traces in a model's learned semantic correlation structure: incidental regularities that are predictive within a dataset, but not causal for the underlying task, can be internalized during training. We use this insight to study dataset-level membership inference, moving beyond existing methods that rely on behavioral or distributional evidence such as confidence scores, losses, margins, generated samples, or query responses. We introduce a white-box semantic fingerprinting approach based on semantic correlation descriptors (SCDs), which capture the semantic correlation structure learned by a model and make it comparable across dataset mixtures. In a controlled leave-one-dataset-out diagnostic, SCDs recover dataset-specific changes and perfectly separate matching from non-matching dataset pairs. We then propose a practical SCD-based membership score that tests whether a target dataset is part of a model's training mixture using only the model's SCD and the target dataset's standalone SCD, without requiring leave-one-dataset-out models. Across three diverse experimental settings, with dataset groups for natural language inference, emotion classification, and medical text classification, we test both the advantages and limitations of SCD-based membership inference with different degrees of semantic separation and keyword support between dataset splits. On average, the classifier based on this score achieves the highest performance and the lowest std, outperforming black-box baselines RMIA, Attack-P, and LiRA, as well as the white-box SIF baseline. These results show that dataset membership can be traced through internal semantic correlations, with the largest relative gain exceeding 60% in ROC-AUC when dataset groups expose distinct semantic particularities.

</details>


### [102] [Can Subgraph Explanations Be Weaponized to Steal Graph Neural Networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30470)
*Ojas Nimase,Jiate Li,Yue Zhao,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个针对严格黑盒图分类的模型提取攻击方法，利用可解释性接口的输出（离散标签和二值解释掩码）通过蒙特卡洛边缘敏感性估计和解释子图搜索来窃取模型，揭示了可解释AI的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图机器学习即服务（GMLaaS）平台为实现监管透明度要求而部署可解释性接口，但这种透明度为模型提取攻击创造了可利用的漏洞。现有攻击方法通常依赖概率分数、梯度或置信度值，而严格黑盒场景中攻击者仅能观测到离散类标签和二值解释掩码，亟需针对该约束的攻击方法。

Method: (1) 利用模型解释输出来引导蒙特卡洛边缘敏感性估计，向决策边界逼近，并利用Hoeffding不等式提供估计精度的浓度保证；(2) 利用解释子图有效缩小边界搜索空间。

Result: 在多个领域的基准图数据集上进行的大量实验表明，该方法在可比基线上具有优越性。

Conclusion: 可解释性接口会创建可利用的攻击面，该发现可为防御机制和可解释AI授权的政策框架提供参考。

Abstract: Graph Machine Learning as a Service (GMLaaS) platforms increasingly implement explainability interfaces to meet regulatory transparency requirements. However, this transparency creates exploitable vulnerabilities for model extraction attacks. We present the first model extraction attack specifically designed for graph classification under strict black-box constraints where the attacker observes only discrete class labels and binary explanation masks (no probability scores, gradients, or confidence values). Our method (1) uses model explanation outputs to guide Monte Carlo edge sensitivity estimation toward decision boundaries, with Hoeffding concentration guarantees on estimation accuracy and (2) exploits explanation subgraphs to efficiently narrow the boundary search space. Extensive experiments on benchmark graph datasets across multiple domains demonstrate our method's superiority over comparable baselines. These findings demonstrate that such explainability interfaces create exploitable attack surfaces, informing both defensive mechanisms and policy frameworks for explainable AI mandates. The implementation code is provided in https://github.com/LabRAI/XSTEAL/.

</details>


### [103] [Universal Multiclass Transductive Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30479)
*Steve Hanneke,Hongao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了通用转导在线分类问题（标签空间可能无界），刻画了可学习性，发现可学习类的最优错误率只有两种：有界或对数增长。引入了新的组合结构"LCLL树"及无差异性质来刻画可学习性，并将结果推广到不可知情形和已知实例序列随机过程的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在转导在线学习设定下，实例序列预先已知但标签未知，且标签空间可能无界，现有理论对这类场景的可学习性刻画尚不充分，需要建立完整的可学习性理论并揭示最优错误率。

Method: 引入了一种新的组合结构——"层级约束的利特尔通-利特尔通树（LCLL树）"，并结合无差异性质（indifference property）来刻画可学习性。通过分析不同概念类的结构特征，推导出最优错误率只能是常数或对数增长。

Result: 发现可学习的概念类的最优错误率只有两种可能：有界（常数）或对数增长；LCLL树与无差异性质联合刻画了可学习性的充要条件；该结果可推广到不可知情形和已知实例序列随机过程的情形。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了通用转导在线分类的可学习性，揭示了其错误率只有两种可能的速率，并通过新组合工具建立了统一的刻画框架。这不仅深化了对转导在线学习的理解，也为后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider the problem of universal transductive online classification with a possibly unbounded label space. This setting considers online learning, with the sequence of instances (without labels) known to the learner in advance. We say a concept class $\mathcal{H}$ is learnable if there is a learning algorithm $\mathcal{A}$, such that for every realizable sequence, the number of mistakes made by $\mathcal{A}$ grows at most sublinearly with the number of predictions. We characterize the learnability of this setting and show that there are only two possible optimal rates for the learnable classes: either bounded or increasing logarithmically. We introduce a new combinatorial structure, called ``Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL) tree'', which, along with the indifference property, characterizes the learnability. We also extend the learnability result to the agnostic case and the case where only the stochastic process that generates the instance sequence is known.

</details>


### [104] [Discovering a Zeta Map Algorithm on Dyck Paths via Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30482)
*Xiaoyu Huang,Blake Jackson,Kyu-Hwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机械可解释性分析一个用于Dyck路径zeta映射的小型transformer模型，发现了基于层级（level-based）的机制，并将其翻译为显式的组合算法——scaffolding map，证明了该算法与zeta映射等价（除标签反转约定外）。这是一个通过机械可解释性将模型行为转化为可验证人类数学发现的受控案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数学发现中使用机器学习时，期望的输出往往不是预测本身，而是可独立验证的显式构造。本文希望通过分析小模型学习经典双射（zeta映射）的内部计算机制，探索能否将模型行为翻译为精确、可验证的组合算法。

Method: 训练一个故意小型化的单层单头编码器-解码器Transformer学习Dyck路径的zeta映射；使用机械可解释性工具（解码器交叉注意力分析、线性探针、因果干预）分析模型内部计算；从分析信号中提取组合结构，提出scaffolding map算法；最后证明该算法与zeta映射等价。

Result: 发现模型采用层级机制：编码器表示使路径层级线性可访问，解码器以结构化方式选择和遍历输入位置。翻译出的scaffolding map是一个以峰为中心的遍历算法，并证明它与zeta映射一致（仅标签反转约定不同）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个AI辅助数学发现的受控范例，展示了机械可解释性如何将模型学到的行为转化为人类可验证的精确组合算法，为机器学习在数学发现中的应用提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly used in mathematical discovery, but in mathematics the desired output is often not a prediction itself, but an explicit construction that can be checked independently. We study this setting through the zeta map on Dyck paths, a classical bijection in the combinatorics of the q,t-Catalan numbers. We train a deliberately small one-layer, one-head encoder-decoder transformer on this map and analyze its learned computation using mechanistic interpretability tools, including decoder cross-attention analysis, linear probing, and causal intervention. The analysis reveals a level-based mechanism: encoder representations make path levels linearly accessible, while the decoder selects and traverses input positions in a structured way. Translating these signals into combinatorics leads to the scaffolding map, an explicit peak-centered traversal algorithm for Dyck paths. We prove that this algorithm agrees with the zeta map, modulo a reversal convention in the labeling. This gives a controlled example of AI-assisted mathematical discovery in which mechanistic interpretability turns model behavior into a precise, human-verifiable combinatorial algorithm.

</details>


### [105] [Graph-Conditioned Mixture of Graph Neural Network Experts for Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30486)
*Amirhossein Ghaffari,Saeid Sheikhi,Ekaterina Gilman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GC-MoE提出了一种图条件混合专家框架，通过为每个节点分配个性化的冻结预训练时空GNN专家组合，仅训练轻量路由模块（约17K参数）即可在四个交通预测基准上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器图中的不同节点（如道路段）在功能类别、结构和交通行为上存在差异，单一骨干架构无法适应这种异质性，因此需要节点级别的专家专业化。

Method: 提出GC-MoE框架：1) 基于图拓扑和最近交通输入窗口，为每个节点分配个性化冻结专家组合；2) 结合冻结预训练时空GNN专家与输入感知、空间上下文化的路由模块；3) 仅训练轻量路由模块（~17K参数），专家权重（1.5M）冻结；4) 可选的有界图条件输出精炼层；5) 使用节点自适应ST-LoRA作为消融诊断。

Result: 在PEMS04、PEMS07、METR-LA、PEMS-BAY四个标准基准上，GC-MoE相对于零参数集成基线提升了MAE指标，同时RMSE和MAPE具有竞争力，仅训练约17K参数（专家权重1.5M冻结）。

Conclusion: 图条件混合专家机制能有效利用节点异质性提升时空预测性能，且参数高效，验证了节点级专家专业化的有效性。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal forecasting on sensor graphs is commonly tackled with a single backbone architecture applied uniformly across all nodes, although graph regions can exhibit different dynamics. Road segments differ in functional class, structure, and traffic behavior, suggesting that node-wise expert specialization can be useful. We propose GC-MoE, a graph-conditioned mixture of experts framework that assigns each node a personalized combination of frozen forecasting experts based on graph topology and the recent traffic input window. GC-MoE combines frozen pretrained spatio-temporal GNN experts with an input-aware, spatially contextualized router while training only a lightweight routing module. We also study a bounded graph-conditioned output refinement layer as an optional extension and include node-adaptive ST-LoRA adapters only as an ablation diagnostic. Across four standard benchmarks (PEMS04, PEMS07, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY), GC-MoE improves MAE over a zero-parameter ensemble baseline, with competitive RMSE and MAPE, while training only ~17K parameters on top of 1.5M frozen expert weights. The implementation is available at https://github.com/Ahghaffari/gc_moe.

</details>


### [106] [MAAT: Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30514)
*Suryash Yagnik,Shubham Gaur,Saksham Thakur,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有机器遗忘评估存在严重结构偏差：因果类"为什么"问题占比极低（<0.06%-1.3%），导致方法在因果知识上的失败被掩盖。作者提出5WBENCH平衡基准（5类各1000样本）和MAAT方法，首次同时实现Why类因果知识的高遗忘与高保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘评估存在结构性偏差，Why型（因果/关系知识）问题在现有基准中占比极低（CounterFact<0.06%, ZSRE<0.6%, TOFU/MUSE/WMDP-Cyber<1.3%），导致在因果知识上失败的方法在聚合指标上仍可高分，且这种失败无法通过非平衡评估检测。

Method: 1) 5WBENCH: 平衡5维基准（Who/What/When/Where/Why各1000样本），首次量化因果遗忘失败。2) MAAT: 三阶段框架，作用于LoRA适配器权重，包含梯度投影上升、SVD秩维剪枝、任务向量消减、混合KL-隐状态保留修复。

Result: 现有基线无法同时在Why型问题上实现高遗忘与高保留：激进遗忘破坏保留知识，保守方法无法遗忘因果事实。MAAT首次在Why型因果知识上同时实现高遗忘与高保留，达到遗忘-保留帕累托前沿的新操作点。

Conclusion: Why型遗忘困难源于多跳推理链（44% vs ≤2%）和40.1令牌平均答案跨度的梯度稀释。MAAT证明了通过多阶段适配器操作克服这一挑战的可行性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning evaluation is structurally skewed: Why-type questions, which probe causal and relational knowledge, comprise less than 0.06% of CounterFact, 0.6% of ZSRE, and less than 1.3% of TOFU, MUSE, and WMDP-Cyber. This near-zero representation means that methods that fail on causal knowledge can score highly in aggregate, and this failure is undetectable without balanced evaluation. We present 5WBENCH, a balanced 5,000-sample benchmark with 1,000 examples per 5W category (Who, What, When, Where, Why), making causal unlearning failures quantifiable for the first time. Using 5WBENCH, we show that no existing baseline simultaneously achieves high forgetting and high retention on Why-type questions: aggressive forgetting degrades retained knowledge, while conservative methods fail to forget causal facts. Why-type difficulty stems from multi-hop reasoning chains (44% of Why entries vs. less than or equal to 2% for others) and gradient dilution over 40.1-token answer spans. We present MAAT (Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning), a three-phase framework operating on LoRA adapter weights, combining gradient-projected ascent, SVD rank-dimension pruning, task vector negation, and hybrid KL-hidden-state retain repair. MAAT is the first method to simultaneously achieve high forgetting and high retention on Why-type causal knowledge, reaching a new operating point on the forget-retain Pareto frontier. We make our code publicly available.

</details>


### [107] [Revisiting Padded Transformer Expressivity: Which Architectural Choices Matter and Which Don't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30523)
*Anej Svete,William Merrill,Ryan Cotterell,Ashish Sabharwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文系统研究了带填充（padded）Transformer在布尔电路复杂度类中的表达能力，证明了在实用假设下其表达性对注意力类型、模型宽度和均匀性条件具有鲁棒性，主要影响因素是数值精度和模型深度。具体结论包括：多项式填充的常数精度Transformer等价于L-uniform AC⁰，增长精度可达L-uniform TC⁰；循环（looping）机制可使常数精度Transformer达到FO-uniform AC^d，增长精度达到FO-uniform TC^d；宽度或精度超过对数级不会提升表达性；这些结论对softmax和平均硬注意力均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过布尔电路描述Transformer的计算能力，但缺乏精确刻画且对建模选择敏感。填充Transformer作为一种有效工具，通过提供多项式空间进行自适应并行计算，建立起与电路类的等价关系。然而，已有研究仅涉及有限的填充Transformer理想化模型，对注意力类型、模型宽度和均匀性条件变化下这些等价关系的鲁棒性尚不清楚。本文旨在系统地探究这些因素对Transformer表达性的影响。

Method: 采用理论分析方法，在填充Transformer框架下考虑不同数值精度（常数精度与增长精度）、不同注意力类型（softmax与平均硬注意力）、不同模型宽度以及不同均匀性条件（L-uniform与FO-uniform），同时引入循环（looping）机制来模拟顺序处理。通过这些维度的系统变化，证明Transformer与不同电路复杂度类（AC⁰、TC⁰、AC^d、TC^d）之间的等价关系。

Result: （1）多项式填充的常数精度Transformer等价于L-uniform AC⁰，增长精度等价于L-uniform TC⁰，且与宽度无关；（2）循环机制下，log^d N步循环的常数精度Transformer达到FO-uniform AC^d，增长精度达到FO-uniform TC^d；（3）宽度或精度超过对数级不会进一步增加表达性；（4）所有结论对softmax和平均硬注意力两种类型均成立。

Conclusion: 填充Transformer的表达性对注意力类型、模型宽度和均匀性条件具有令人惊讶的鲁棒性，数值精度和模型深度才是影响表达性的主要因素。该研究填补了填充Transformer在电路复杂度类中精确刻画的理论空白，为理解Transformer的计算极限提供了更完整的理论框架。

Abstract: Recent work describes what transformers can and cannot compute through connections to boolean circuits, but existing results lack exact characterizations and are sensitive to modeling choices. Padded transformers -- to whose input filler symbols such as ``...'' are appended -- emerge as a useful gadget for establishing equivalences to circuit classes by providing polynomial space for adaptive parallel computation. However, only a limited set of padded transformer idealizations has been studied, leaving open how robustly these equivalences hold under changes to attention type, model width, and uniformity. We find that, under practical assumptions, padded transformers are surprisingly robust to all of these, and identify numeric precision and model depth as the main factors affecting expressivity. Concretely, we prove that polynomially padded $\text{L-uniform}$ constant-precision transformers are equivalent to $\text{L-uniform AC}^0$, while growing-precision ones achieve $\text{L-uniform TC}^0$ regardless of width. Furthermore, looping enables sequential processing analogous to circuits: $\log^d N$-looped constant-precision transformers reach $\text{FO-uniform AC}^d$, and growing-precision ones reach $\text{FO-uniform TC}^d$. Interestingly, growing width or precision beyond logarithmic does not increase expressivity, and all our results hold for both softmax and average hard attention transformers.

</details>


### [108] [Representation Collapse in Sequential Post-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30524)
*Yichen Liu,Mingyu Chen,Hao Wang,Xiaoran Xu,Chenxi Lin,Rui Zhang,Yutong Zhou,Yuxin Yang,Jiarui Wu,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在多阶段后训练过程中，内部表示是否会逐渐退化为低秩、各向异性、同质的特征空间，并验证这种表示集中化会降低后续可塑性、泛化能力和校准性能，同时提出轻量级干预方法以保持未来可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型现在通过多阶段后训练链（而非单次指令微调）进行适配，但多阶段顺序训练对内部表示结构的影响尚不清楚。本文旨在探究这种顺序后训练是否会导致表示逐渐压缩为低秩、各向异性、同质的特征空间，以及这种压缩是否影响模型后续的学习能力。

Method: 定义了一套测量指标（隐藏状态、logits、token轨迹、LoRA更新），在受控阶段顺序下分析监督微调、偏好优化、安全/拒答调优、数学和代码专业化、长思维链调优等多种后训练阶段。评估轻量级干预方法包括：混合领域回放、特征刷新、表示多样性正则化、LoRA更新去相关。

Result: 表示过度集中不仅仅是几何上的偶然现象，它会预测后续适应阶段中可塑性降低、域外泛化能力减弱以及校准性能变差。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级干预方法可以在不牺牲后训练行为收益的前提下，保留未来的可学习性。

Abstract: Large language models are now adapted through chains of post-training stages rather than through a single instruction-tuning pass. This paper studies whether such sequential post-training gradually compresses internal representations into low-rank, anisotropic, and homogeneous feature spaces. We define a measurement suite for hidden states, logits, token trajectories, and LoRA updates, and we use it to analyze supervised fine-tuning, preference optimization, safety/refusal tuning, math and code specialization, and long chain-of-thought tuning under controlled stage orderings. The central hypothesis is that excessive representation concentration is not merely a geometric curiosity: it predicts reduced plasticity during later adaptation, weaker out-of-domain generalization, and poorer calibration. We further evaluate lightweight interventions, including mixed-domain replay, feature refresh, representation diversity regularization, and LoRA update decorrelation, as ways to preserve future learnability without giving up the behavioral gains of post-training.

</details>


### [109] [Measuring, Localizing, and Ablating Alignment Signatures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30526)
*Aniket Anand,Janvijay Singh,Zhewei Sun,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Nick Feamster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对齐语言模型中的AI风格特征，发现后训练会放大这种风格，并提出PASTA方法通过激活消融来降低AI检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐语言模型常表现出可识别的AI风格，但后训练与这种风格及内部表示之间的联系尚不清楚，作者旨在探索后训练是否引入或放大了AI风格规律性，以及这些规律性是否有局部的神经内部特征。

Method: 对比人类文本、基座模型生成和对齐模型生成（匹配人类前缀）；提出PASTA方法，通过对齐-基座残差对比估计后训练对齐特征，在解码时消融对应方向。

Result: 对齐生成比基座生成的人类语料亲和度更低、AI检测率更高；PASTA在11个对齐模型和6个AI检测器上降低了大多数对齐模型的检测率，且效果跨检测器迁移，不被随机方向复现；PASTA生成文本保持相关性和连贯性且风格更多样。

Conclusion: 后训练的AI风格效应是可测量、可定位的，并可通过激活消融进行因果检验。

Abstract: Aligned language models often exhibit a recognizable AI-like style, yet its connection to post-training and internal representations remains poorly understood. In this work, we study whether post-training introduces or amplifies AI-like stylistic regularities and whether these regularities have a localized internal signature. To this end, we compare human text, base-model generations, and aligned-model generations under matched human-source prefixes. Aligned generations show lower human-corpus affinity and higher AI-detection rates than base generations, suggesting that post-training shifts generated text away from human-corpus style and toward detector-visible AI-like text. We then introduce PASTA (Post-training Alignment Signature Targeted Ablation), a training-free method that estimates a post-training alignment signature from aligned-base residual contrasts and ablates the corresponding direction during decoding. Across 11 aligned models and 6 AI detectors, PASTA lowers the detection rate for most aligned models; this effect transfers well across detectors and is not reproduced by random directions. Qualitative analysis suggests that PASTA generations remain relevant and coherent while exhibiting greater stylistic variation. Together, these results show that AI-like stylistic effects of post-training can be measured, localized, and causally tested through activation ablation.

</details>


### [110] [The Long-Term Effects of Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30537)
*Yuxin Yang,Aoxiong Zeng,Xiangquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM多阶段微调中的数据选择问题，发现短视的选择策略（只优化当前阶段）会导致后续学习变慢、遗忘增加等问题，提出了长视距感知选择（LHAS）目标函数来缓解这一矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法大多关注当前阶段的即时效用，忽略了多阶段微调中当前最优选择可能损害模型未来适应性的问题，作者希望从长视距视角重新审视数据选择策略。

Method: 在统一的多阶段协议下比较了随机、基于损失、基于梯度、基于多样性、基于质量和效用-多样性等代表性选择族，形式化了"短视选择"行为并进行了局部理论分析，提出了LHAS目标函数（结合即时效用、覆盖度、未来代理迁移和反集中项）。

Result: 短视选择器会出现排名逆转现象：提升当前阶段性能但减慢后续学习并增加遗忘；LHAS目标函数能缓解这一问题。

Conclusion: 数据选择应被视为塑造模型学习轨迹的训练干预，而非仅作为局部数据效率机制；评估时需考虑未来适应速度、遗忘、能力不平衡和分布外鲁棒性等长期指标。

Abstract: Data selection is increasingly used to reduce the cost of large language model (LLM) fine-tuning, with recent methods prioritizing samples by current utility, diversity, quality, or influence. This paper studies a different question: when fine-tuning occurs over multiple stages, can selection strategies that look optimal now make the model less adaptable later? We introduce a long-horizon view of LLM data selection in which a selector is evaluated not only by immediate task performance, but also by future adaptation speed, forgetting, capability imbalance, and out-of-distribution robustness.
