We compare representative random, loss-based, gradient-based, diversity-based, quality-based, and utility-diversity selection families under a unified multi-stage protocol. Through controlled experiments designed to instantiate this protocol, we show how short-term selectors can exhibit rank reversal: they improve the current stage while slowing subsequent learning and increasing forgetting. We formalize this behavior as \emph{myopic selection}, provide a simple local analysis of why it can occur, and propose a diagnostic Long-Horizon Aware Selection (LHAS) objective that augments immediate utility with coverage, future-proxy transfer, and anti-concentration terms. The study argues that data selection should be evaluated as a training intervention that shapes the model's learning trajectory, rather than only as a local data-efficiency mechanism.

</details>


### [111] [DisasterLex: An Expert Concept-to-Schema Knowledge Graph for Geospatial Reasoning in Disaster Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30538)
*Yiming Xiao,Ankit Basu,Kai Yin,Sahil Vartak,Christian Swords,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DisasterLex 是一个知识图谱中介的文本到SQL框架，通过专家知识图谱（EKG）将用户查询与灾害领域的异构地理空间表格连接起来，在75个查询测试集上比四个最先进的基线方法提升1.4倍到2.75倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到SQL方法在灾害领域迁移性差，因为该领域查询涉及异构地理空间模式并需要对因果关系的推理，而传统方法无法有效处理这些复杂操作。

Method: 提出DisasterLex框架，在用户查询和数据库之间插入一个包含107个概念、117条因果边和52个概念到模式链接的专家知识图谱（EKG），通过四个阶段（实体识别、域路由、因果规划、SQL生成）逐步限制传给模型的模式范围。

Result: 在7个基础模型上，DisasterLex以1.65到3.56（满分5.0）的绝对分数，比LightRAG、HippoRAG 2、ReFoRCE、CHESS等基线方法提升1.4x到2.75x。错误分析表明基线失败集中在路由和多表SQL组合操作上。

Conclusion: 知识图谱中介的方法能有效解决灾害领域文本到SQL中的模式路由和复杂SQL组合问题，为灾害响应中的结构化数据查询提供了显著优于现有方法的解决方案。

Abstract: Disasters are inevitable and increasingly costly, and effective response depends on querying structured tabular data: precise, information-dense records of hazard, exposure, vulnerability, and lifeline infrastructure that underpin disaster management. Current text-to-SQL methods enable natural-language access to such tables but transfer poorly to the disaster domain, where queries span heterogeneous geospatial schemas and require reasoning over causal relations. We introduce DisasterLex, a knowledge-graph-mediated framework that inserts an Expert Knowledge Graph (EKG) of curated concepts and typed causal edges between the user query and the database, bridged to schema by concept-to-table links. The orchestration runs four stages (identifying query entities, routing to the operational domain, planning over causal edges, and grounding the SQL), restricting the schema passed to the model at each step. We instantiate it on a disaster-analytics database (36 geospatial tables, 150 columns) with an EKG of 107 concepts, 117 causal edges, and 52 concept-to-schema links, evaluated on a 75-query test set. On all seven base models spanning proprietary and open-weight families, DisasterLex beats four state-of-the-art baselines (LightRAG, HippoRAG 2, ReFoRCE, CHESS) by 1.4x to 2.75x, with absolute scores of 1.65 to 3.56 (of 5.0). Error analysis shows baseline failures cluster in routing and multi-table SQL composition, the operations our orchestration explicitly addresses. Code, data, and the EKG artifact are available at https://github.com/YimingXiao98/DisasterLex and on Zenodo at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20388029.

</details>


### [112] [SubsurfaceGen: Procedural Generation of Field-Scale Earth Models and Seismic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30541)
*Joseph Stitt,Pratik Rathore,Madeleine Udell,Ching-Yao Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SubsurfaceGen——一个GPU加速的3D速度模型和地震数据生成器，并发布包含4,276个2D速度切片、5秒波场和8秒炮记录的数据集，覆盖6种地质环境，用于推动基于机器学习的全波形反演研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FWI机器学习训练数据（如Marmousi、SEAM、OpenFWI）在空间/时间范围、地质多样性和物理真实性方面存在不足，无法满足现场尺度需求。

Method: 开发SubsurfaceGen GPU加速生成器，构建42个真实尺度3D速度模型（10 km×10 km×6.19 km，10 m分辨率），从中提取2D切片、波场和炮记录数据，涵盖六种地质环境。

Result: 生成了4,276个2D速度切片、5秒波场和8秒炮记录数据集；在波场预测（神经算子）和端到端速度反演（编码器-解码器）任务上评估，发现现场尺度下的失效模式。

Conclusion: SubsurfaceGen及其数据集能够揭示现场尺度ML-FWI的失效模式，对推动基于机器学习的全波形反演具有重要价值。

Abstract: Full waveform inversion (FWI) is the gold standard for subsurface imaging, with applications from carbon sequestration to energy and mineral exploration to earthquake hazard assessment. Machine learning approaches to FWI need field-scale, geologically diverse, and physically realistic training data, but existing resources such as Marmousi, SEAM, and OpenFWI fall short on spatial extent, temporal extent, geological diversity, and physical realism. We address these limitations with SubsurfaceGen, a GPU-accelerated generator for 3D velocity models and seismic data. Along with SubsurfaceGen, we release a paired dataset of 4,276 2D velocity slices, 5 s wavefields, and 8 s shot gathers drawn from 42 realistic, field-scale 3D velocity models, each spanning 10 km x 10 km laterally and 6.19 km deep at 10 m resolution. The dataset spans six geological settings -- four built with SubsurfaceGen and two drawn from prior sources -- relevant for carbon sequestration and hydrocarbon exploration. We use this dataset to evaluate neural operators on wavefield prediction and encoder-decoders on end-to-end velocity inversion, holding out one geological setting for out-of-distribution testing. These experiments surface failure modes at field-scale and demonstrate how SubsurfaceGen and the associated dataset can impact ML-based FWI.

</details>


### [113] [Early Prediction of Future Behavioral Strategy from Process Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30550)
*Robert Kasumba,Dennis Barbour,Chien-Ju Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出过程级潜变量模型（PLVM），通过跨任务的部分行为轨迹（process traces）来预测个体在新任务中的策略行为。在PowerWash Simulator游戏数据中，PLVM利用两个清洁任务的早期轨迹，成功预测了玩家在消防站关卡中的策略类型（Zone Planner vs Zone Hopper）。模拟实验表明，当源任务揭示共享潜过程的不同维度时，跨任务融合有助于提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应系统常需从有限证据中做出针对特定任务的决策（如预测学习者如何解题、玩家进入新关卡时的行为模式、人类-AI协作中推断伙伴是否会坚持计划或切换目标）。这些决策依赖于个体层面的倾向性（person-level tendencies），但标准行为证据难以推断这些倾向。聚合结果摘要（如分数、完成率）虽简洁但会混淆不同行为过程，而过程级轨迹虽更精细却容易将稳定个体倾向与任务特定布局纠缠在一起。因此，研究如何在早期利用跨任务的部分过程轨迹推断可迁移的个体层面结构。

Method: 提出过程级潜变量模型（Process-Level Latent Variable Model, PLVM），该模型首先编码任务特定的行为轨迹，然后将其融合为共享的个体层面潜表示，用于跨任务预测。在PowerWash Simulator自然主义游戏遥测数据集上，使用两个清洁任务的早期部分轨迹，预测玩家在消防站关卡中表现出的局部持续型（Zone Planner）与频繁切换型（Zone Hopper）行为。此外，通过控制已知潜类型的模拟实验验证方法的有效性。

Result: PLVM在PowerWash Simulator数据上成功利用两个源任务的早期部分轨迹预测了目标任务中的玩家策略类型。模拟实验表明，当源任务能够揭示共享潜过程的不同维度时，跨任务融合能够提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 过程级跨任务建模能够在观察足够目标任务行为不可行的情况下，支持对目标任务策略的早期预测。这表明利用部分源任务过程轨迹进行跨任务行为推断是一种有前景的路径。

Abstract: Adaptive systems often need to make task-specific decisions about people from limited evidence: a tutor may need to anticipate how a learner will approach a new problem, a game may need to adapt when a player enters a new level, and a human-AI system may need to infer whether a partner will persist with a plan or switch goals. These decisions depend on person-level tendencies that shape how people solve related tasks, but such tendencies are difficult to infer from standard behavioral evidence. One approach is to use aggregate outcome summaries, such as scores, completion rates, or productivity; these summaries are compact and available across tasks, but can collapse distinct behavioral processes into similar outcomes. Another approach is to use process-level traces, which record how behavior unfolds; however, process modeling within one task can entangle stable person-level tendencies with task-specific layout and affordances. In this work, we study early cross-task behavioral inference: whether partial source-task process traces can reveal transferable person-level structure that predicts strategy in a held-out target task. We introduce a Process-Level Latent Variable Model (PLVM), which encodes task-specific traces and fuses them into a shared person-level latent representation for cross-task prediction. In PowerWash Simulator, a naturalistic telemetry dataset of human gameplay, PLVM uses partial traces from two cleaning tasks to predict locally persistent Zone Planner behavior versus frequent Zone Hopper behavior in the held-out Fire Station level. Controlled simulations with known latent types show that cross-task fusion helps when source tasks reveal complementary dimensions of a shared latent process. These results suggest that process-level cross-task modeling can support early prediction of target-task strategy when observing sufficient target-task behavior is impractical.

</details>


### [114] [The Fast Mixing Mechanism for Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30600)
*Omri Lev,Moshe Shenfeld,Vishwak Srinivasan,Katrina Ligett,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于快速变换的差分隐私 sketching 机制，在保持强隐私保证的同时实现了与经典快速 sketching 方法相当的运行时间，并将其应用于 DP 线性回归，首次实现了 DP 普通最小二乘的快速方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯 sketching 的 DP 线性回归方法虽然具有强效用保证，但通常无法在运行时间上超越经典 DP 方法，缺乏快速高效的 DP 优化压缩工具。

Method: 提出基于快速变换（如 Hadamard 矩阵）的新型 DP sketching 机制，理论上证明其隐私保证在有利条件下与高斯 sketch 仅差常数因子，并将其与最新的 sketch-based DP 线性回归方法结合。

Result: 新机制在特定情况下匹配了经典快速 sketching 的运行时间，并达到了最先进的隐私保证。结合该机制的 DP 线性回归算法获得了强效用和更优运行时间，据我们所知是首个 DP 普通最小二乘的快速方法。

Conclusion: 基于快速变换的 DP sketching 机制能够兼顾隐私、效用和效率，为大规模 DP 优化问题提供了一种实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Randomized sketching is a central tool for compressing large-scale optimization problems while preserving accuracy. In particular, sketches that are based on structured matrices, such as the Hadamard matrix, can be applied efficiently and often yield solutions that approximate those of the original problem at much lower computational cost. In differential privacy (DP), Gaussian sketching has been used to solve DP linear regression, beginning with \citet{sheffet2017differentially, sheffet2019old} and later refined by \citet{lev2025gaussianmix, lev2026near}. However, although these methods achieve strong utility guarantees, they usually do not improve runtime over classical DP approaches. In this work, we introduce a new DP sketching mechanism based on fast transforms, which, in certain cases, matches the runtime of classical fast sketching methods. We prove state-of-the-art privacy guarantees for this mechanism and show that, in favorable regimes, they match those of the Gaussian sketch up to a constant factor. As an application, we combine this mechanism with recent sketch-based methods for DP linear regression to obtain a new algorithm with strong utility and improved runtime. We establish privacy and accuracy guarantees for this algorithm, yielding, to the best of our knowledge, the first fast method for DP ordinary least squares.

</details>


### [115] [Score Broadcast and Decorrelation: A General Framework for Broadcast-Based Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30638)
*Mustafa Uzun,Mete Erdogan,Cengiz Pehlevan,Alper T. Erdogan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Score Broadcast and Decorrelation (SBD)框架，基于输出分数与隐藏层激活之间的正交性原理，将广播式信用分配推广到通用可微损失函数族（包括交叉熵、Bregman散度、 proper scoring rules及指数族负对数似然）。该框架为三因子学习规则提供了理论依据，并通过分数向量扩展技术丰富了广播信号。实验表明SBD显著优于现有广播方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Error Broadcast and Decorrelation (EBD)框架从均方误差(MSE)设置推广到通用可微损失函数族，为生物可信的广播式信用分配（无需权重传输）提供统一的理论基础。

Method: 1) 提出输出分数（损失对最终层输出的梯度）与隐藏层激活之间的正交性原理；2) 该原理统一了多种损失函数族下的广播式信用分配；3) 推导交叉熵情形，刻画可容许损失类；4) 引入分数向量扩展技术以丰富广播信号。

Result: CIFAR-10和Tiny ImageNet上的实验表明，SBD显著优于现有广播方法，分数向量扩展带来了进一步的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作识别了损失分数作为广播信号，为正交性理论和神经科学中的三因子学习规则提供了理论依据，并展示了分数向量扩展如何丰富目标函数的去相关方向。

Abstract: We introduce Score Broadcast and Decorrelation (SBD), a principled framework for broadcast-based credit assignment for general families of differentiable losses. Error broadcast is a biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation that sends output information to hidden layers without weight transport. The Error Broadcast and Decorrelation (EBD) framework, recently introduced for the mean-squared-error (MSE) setting, grounded this mechanism in the stochastic orthogonality of optimal estimators, under which the optimal residual is orthogonal to functions of the input. We generalize that foundation by introducing an orthogonality principle between the output score (the gradient of loss with respect to the final-layer output) and hidden-layer activations, which holds whenever the optimal score has conditional mean zero. This single principle unifies broadcast-based credit assignment across the standard differentiable-loss families, including cross-entropy, Bregman divergences, proper scoring rules, and exponential-family negative log-likelihoods. The framework supplies a theoretical grounding for the three-factor learning rule under general losses, with the neuromodulatory factor derived as the broadcast loss score. We derive the cross-entropy case explicitly, characterize the admissible loss class, and introduce a score vector expansion technique that enriches the broadcast signal while preserving the orthogonality framework. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and Tiny ImageNet show that SBD substantially improves over existing broadcast approaches, with score vector expansion delivering further gains. Overall, this work identifies the loss score as the signal to broadcast, supplies the orthogonality theory and theoretical grounding for the three-factor learning rule from neuroscience, and shows how score vector expansion enriches the decorrelation directions of the resulting objective.

</details>


### [116] [Diffusion Models Preferentially Memorize Prototypical Examples or: Why Does My Diffusion Model Love Slop?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30642)
*Marta Aparicio Rodriguez,Anastasia Borovykh,Grigorios A. Pavliotis,Daniel J. Korchinski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过随机层次模型（RHM）训练扩散模型，发现模型优先记忆训练数据中的常见子串而非稀有样本，且即使数据完全去重仍然存在记忆现象。长尾数据集（含更多非典型样本）会延迟记忆发生，而高层次生产规则中的长尾分布会放大该效应。研究还识别出部分记忆的中间状态，其中常见子串被优先学习并在生成时过度产生，若在此阶段停止训练，模型会产生“回归均值”的平淡输出（即“slop”）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型存在记忆训练数据的固有限制，这可能导致法律纠纷并削弱创造性多样性。理解哪些样本被部分或完全记忆、以及在何种条件下发生记忆，是重要的开放问题。作者特别探究了“非典型或稀有样本是否会被优先记忆”这一具体问题。

Method: 使用随机层次模型（RHM）的生产规则生成字符串，训练扩散模型。通过控制数据集中子串的常见性、数据去重程度以及数据集的长尾特性（引入非典型样本），系统性地观察模型的记忆行为。在训练过程中监控模型对常见子串与稀有子串的学习与生成模式。

Result: （1）模型优先记忆由常见子串组成的样本，而非稀有样本；（2）即使训练数据中每个样本完全唯一（数据点级别去重），模型仍会记忆常见子串模式，说明去重不提供有意义的隐私保障；（3）长尾数据集（含更多非典型样本）会延迟记忆的发生，且高层次生产规则中的长尾分布放大此效应；（4）存在部分记忆的中间状态，其中常见子串被优先学习并在生成时过度产生，若提前停止训练，模型输出呈现“回归均值”的平淡特性（slop）。

Conclusion: 数据集多样性，特别是高层次抽象层面的多样性，在抑制记忆方面起重要作用。部分记忆中间状态的存在表明，训练过早停止会导致模型生成缺乏多样性的平庸输出。研究为理解生成模型记忆机制的规律（优先记忆常见模式而非稀有样本）提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Generative models have a persistent limitation: their tendency to memorize training data can create legal liabilities and erode creative diversity. Understanding which samples are memorized in whole or in part, and under what conditions, therefore remains an important open problem. Here we answer the question "Are atypical or rare samples memorized first?" in the negative. We train diffusion models on strings generated according to the production rules of the Random Hierarchy Model (RHM), and find that samples composed of common substrings are preferentially memorized. This holds true even if the training data consists of entirely unique samples, indicating that deduplication at the data point level does not provide a meaningful privacy guarantee. Correspondingly we predict, then observe, delayed memorization for fat-tailed datasets (i.e., those with more atypical samples). This effect is amplified when fat-tails are introduced into high-level production rules. These together suggest that dataset diversity, particularly at higher levels of abstraction, plays an important role in staving off memorization. Finally, we identify an intermediate regime of partial memorization in which common substrings are learned first and subsequently overproduced during generation. If training is stopped in this regime, models will exhibit the reversion-to-the-mean blandness often derided as "slop".

</details>


### [117] [LARK: Learnability-Grounded Trajectory Selection for Efficient Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30651)
*Tianrun Yu,Kaixiang Zhao,Chih-Chun Chen,Amanda Hughes,Taylor W. Killian,Fenglong Ma,Weitong Zhang,Porter Jenkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LARK提出了一种基于可学习性的推理轨迹选择方法，通过理论驱动的可学习性因子和χ²正则化策略，在保证分布覆盖的同时选择学生模型高效学习的轨迹，在多个模型和推理任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择方法依赖启发式规则（如轨迹质量或模型置信度），忽略了轨迹对学生模型是否可学习这一关键因素。

Method: 提出可学习性因子ρ表征学生模型训练损失下降速率；设计可学习性代理指标进行高效估计；引入χ²正则化选择策略平衡可学习性与分布覆盖度，并提供理论保证。

Result: 在多个基础模型和推理任务上，LARK一致优于现有数据选择基线；诊断分析表明LARK分数可预测下游训练效用，且所选轨迹能加速监督微调损失下降。

Conclusion: LARK通过将可学习性纳入轨迹选择，实现了更高效且泛化性更好的推理蒸馏，代码已开源。

Abstract: We study trajectory selection for reasoning distillation, where teacher-generated reasoning trajectories are selectively used as supervision for a student model. Existing methods rely on heuristics such as trajectory quality or model confidence, but they often overlook whether a trajectory is learnable by the student. In this paper, we present LARK, a learnability-grounded method for reasoning trajectory selection. LARK selects trajectories that the student can learn efficiently while preserving the generalization of the full training distribution. At the core of LARK is a learnability factor $ρ$, which characterizes the rate at which the student's training loss decreases. To estimate this rate efficiently and maintain generalization, we introduce a learnability proxy and a $χ^2$-regularized selection policy that balances learnability and distributional coverage, both with strong theoretical guarantees on their estimation error. Empirically, LARK consistently outperforms data selection baselines across multiple base models and reasoning tasks. Diagnostic analyses show that the LARK score predicts downstream training utility and that LARK-selected trajectories induce faster supervised fine-tuning loss reduction. Our code is available at https://github.com/Tianrun-Yu/LARK.

</details>


### [118] [Bridging the Gap Between Natural Language and Market Dynamics via High-Dimensional Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30652)
*Yujin Jeong,Noelle Jung,Brian Y. C. Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用高维FinBERT嵌入替代传统标量情感分数，在Transformer预测架构中进行金融预测。在FNSPID数据集上对比多种嵌入策略，发现Siamese网络优化的嵌入在预测短期股价变动上优于标量基线和原始嵌入方法，而注意力机制因金融数据信噪比低而效果不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态金融预测依赖标量情感分数，无法捕捉财经新闻中的细微语义信息，存在信息丢失问题。

Method: 采用FinBERT高维嵌入替代离散极性评分，在Transformer预测架构中对比了原始嵌入、注意力加权聚合和Siamese网络三种嵌入策略。

Result: Siamese优化嵌入优于标量基线和原始嵌入；注意力机制因金融数据信噪比低而表现不佳。

Conclusion: 保留高维叙事上下文能提升短期股价预测的准确性。

Abstract: Traditional multi-modal financial forecasting often relies on scalar sentiment scores, which fail to capture the nuances of financial news. To address this information loss, this paper explores high-dimensional representation learning by replacing discrete polarity ratings with dense FinBERT embeddings within a Transformer-based forecasting architecture. We benchmarked various embedding strategies on the FNSPID dataset, including raw embeddings, attention-weighted aggregation, and a custom Siamese network. While the attention-based mechanism struggled with the low signal-to-noise ratio typical of financial data, the integration of Siamese-optimized embeddings outperformed both the scalar baseline and raw embedding approaches, demonstrating that preserving high-dimensional narrative context yields improved predictive accuracy for short-term stock price movements.

</details>


### [119] [Learning to Perceive the World Through Control: Empowerment-Based Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30656)
*Mahsa Bastankhah,Sophie Broderick,Benjamin Eysenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在强化学习中，通过 empowerment 目标学习仅捕获控制相关特征的表示，发现 empowerment 智能体能够诱导出两种互补的、对控制无关特征不变的表示（前向和后向），并强调通过交互而非被动数据学习表示的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际强化学习环境中观测维度远高于控制所需的变量维度，因此需要探索能否学习仅捕获控制相关特征的表示，以提升样本效率和泛化能力。

Method: 通过 empowerment 目标（最大化智能体对环境的影响）进行研究，分析 empowerment 智能体诱导出的前向（forward）和后向（backward）两种表示，并检验它们对控制无关特征的不变性。

Result: empowerment 智能体诱导的前向和后向两种表示都捕获了状态的互补方面，且均对控制无关特征具有不变性；empowerment 最大化使智能体学习到隐式的、以控制为中心的世界模型。

Conclusion: 通过交互（而非被动数据集）学习表示至关重要，因为旨在最大化控制的交互对于学习有用的不变性性质是必要的，这一观点与因果学习文献紧密契合。

Abstract: In many practical reinforcement learning environments, observations are far higher-dimensional than the variables that matter for control. In this work, we ask: can we learn representations that capture only control-relevant features of the environment? We study this question through the empowerment objective, which maximizes an agent's influence over the environment and is widely used for unsupervised skill learning. We show that empowerment agents induce two distinct representations -- forward and backward -- that capture complementary aspects of the state, and both of which are invariant to control-irrelevant features. Thus, empowerment maximization leads agents to learn an implicit, control-centric model of the world. Our analysis highlights the importance of learning representations through interaction rather than from passive datasets: interaction aimed at maximizing control is essential for learning useful invariance properties, a perspective that aligns closely with the causal learning literature.

</details>


### [120] [Diversity Matters: Revisiting Test-Time Compute in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30713)
*Yijie Tong,Yifan Hou,Shaobo Cui,Antoine Bosselut,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了测试时计算策略在视觉语言模型中的应用，发现特征启发式方法和多数投票在单模型场景下效果有限，原因是预测缺乏多样性。为此提出基于熵的测试时计算（ETTC）方法，通过预测熵选择最自信的预测，在多模型集成中优于多数投票和最佳单模型，且小模型可以协同增强大模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时计算策略虽在大型语言模型中有效提升了推理能力，但在视觉语言模型中的应用和收益尚未被充分探索，因此需要进行系统性研究。

Method: 提出基于熵的测试时计算（ETTC）方法，利用预测熵选择最自信的预测结果；在单模型场景下退化为多数投票，在多模型集成中利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型。

Result: ETTC在温和假设下理论上优于多数投票，实验上持续超越投票策略和最佳单模型；小模型可以协同增强大模型，实现标准策略无法达成的集成增益。

Conclusion: 测试时计算在单模型VLM中受限于预测多样性不足而效果有限，但通过熵驱动的多模型集成策略可以显著提升性能，且不同规模模型间存在协同增效潜力。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) strategies have emerged as a lightweight approach to boost reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, their application and benefits for vision-language models (VLMs) remain underexplored. We present a systematic study of TTC across seven VLMs and six benchmarks, specifically analyzing feature-based scoring and majority voting methods. We find that feature heuristics fail and voting yields only modest gains in single-model settings. We theoretically show that this limitation stems from a lack of prediction diversity: when outputs are highly correlated, voting provides little benefit. In contrast, multi-model ensembles offer richer diversity, yet standard majority voting fails to account for varying model capabilities. To address this, we propose Entropy-based TTC (ETTC), which selects the most confident prediction based on predictive entropy. Our method reduces to majority voting in the single-model case, but in model ensembles, it leverages confidence disparities to prioritize stronger models. We prove that ETTC outperforms majority voting under mild assumptions and empirically demonstrate that it consistently surpasses both voting and the best individual model. Crucially, our results show that smaller models can synergistically enhance larger ones, unlocking ensembling gains not achievable with standard strategies.

</details>


### [121] [Kalimati Vegetable Price Index Forecasting with a Momentum Corrected Online Stacking Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30720)
*Sahaj Raj Malla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了Kalimati蔬菜价格指数（KVPI），一种基于逆波动率加权的复合指数，结合64个因果有效特征和动量校正在线堆叠集成模型，在尼泊尔农产品价格预测中实现了MAPE 0.68%、R² 0.845的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴经济体农产品价格预测因高波动性、频繁的供应中断和强烈的文化需求影响而极具挑战性，现有方法在噪声环境下表现不佳。

Method: 构建KVPI指数（逆波动率加权复合135种日度大宗商品），设计64个因果有效特征（包括节日超前滞后效应、滚动统计量、日历变量），评估14种模型（统计、树集成、深度学习、混合、Transformer），提出动量校正在线堆叠集成模型。

Result: 树集成模型表现稳健；动量校正在线堆叠集成在90天预测窗口取得RMSE 1.771、MAPE 0.68%、R² 0.845的最优结果。

Conclusion: 开源管道为尼泊尔及类似市场的政策制定者和供应链参与者提供了实用的价格预测工具，有助于加强粮食安全。

Abstract: Forecasting agricultural commodity prices in emerging economies is difficult due to high volatility, frequent supply disruptions, and strong cultural influences on demand. This study introduces the Kalimati Vegetable Price Index (KVPI), a new inverse-volatility weighted composite index that aggregates 135 daily wholesale commodities from Kathmandu over ten years (2013-2023). By creating a stable macro-level signal, the KVPI reduces the noise inherent in modelling individual crops. A rich set of 64 causally valid features was developed, including festival lead-lag effects, rolling statistics, and calendar variables. Fourteen forecasting models spanning statistical, tree-based, deep learning, hybrid, and transformer architectures were rigorously evaluated across short (7-day), medium (14- and 30-day), and long-term (90-day) horizons. Tree-based ensembles proved notably robust, while classical statistical models and complex transformers struggled with the noisy dataset. The proposed Momentum-Corrected Online Stacking Ensemble achieved the strongest performance, yielding a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 1.771, an exceptionally low Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) of 0.68%, and explaining 84.5% of the variance (R-squared = 0.845) at the 90-day horizon. This open-source pipeline provides policymakers and supply chain actors in Nepal and similar markets with a practical, reliable tool for anticipating price movements and strengthening food security.

</details>


### [122] [OrcaRouter: A Production-Oriented LLM Router with Hybrid Offline-Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30736)
*Zhenghua Bao,Fengya Tian,Chris Zhang,Zhenjun Chen,Xile Ma,Yi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OrcaRouter是一个面向生产环境的LLM路由器，结合LinUCB上下文赌博机和混合离线-在线学习协议，根据输入请求动态选择最优模型，在RouterArena排行榜上以72.08分位列第二，每千次查询成本1美元，准确率75.54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型快速发展带来多样化能力和推理成本差异，实际部署中需要解决"给定请求应由哪个模型处理"这一核心问题，以平衡性能与成本。

Method: 采用LinUCB上下文赌博机，结合词法和句子嵌入特征作为上下文表示；提出混合离线-在线学习协议：离线阶段通过全信息反馈评估每个候选模型，构建奖励矩阵并拟合岭回归器；在线部署时从离线参数初始化，可选地继续从赌博机反馈中学习，仅更新所选模型的臂参数。

Result: 在RouterArena排行榜（截至2026年5月20日）上，OrcaRouter-Adaptive以72.08分的竞技场分数排名第二，每千次查询成本1美元下达到75.54%的准确率。

Conclusion: OrcaRouter通过混合学习策略有效平衡了LLM路由中的探索-利用困境，在成本和性能之间取得了实用的折衷，适合生产环境部署。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models, each with distinct capabilities and inference costs, raises a practical deployment question: given an incoming request, which model should handle it? We present OrcaRouter, a production-oriented LLM router that combines a LinUCB-based contextual bandit over lexical and sentence-embedding features with a hybrid offline-online learning protocol. Offline, OrcaRouter obtains full-information feedback by evaluating each candidate model on a curated set of routing prompts, yielding a reward matrix used to fit one ridge regressor per arm. At deployment time, it initializes from these parameters and can optionally continue learning from bandit feedback, updating only the selected model's arm after observing its reward. At the time of our RouterArena submission (May 20, 2026), OrcaRouter-Adaptive ranked second on the public RouterArena leaderboard with an arena score of 72.08, achieving 75.54% accuracy at a cost of USD 1.00 per 1,000 queries.

</details>


### [123] [Chain-of-Thought and Compressed Looped Transformers: A Memory-Budget Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30757)
*Haozhou Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了循环Transformer（压缩隐状态循环与完整序列状态循环）和思维链（CoT）提示在测试时推理中的记忆机制差异。核心发现：压缩循环的记忆受限于循环状态大小，即使增加计算步数也无法弥补，在标准复杂度假设下无法解决P完全问题；而完整序列状态循环和CoT因携带更大记忆空间，能处理更复杂的推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究测试时推理中持久可变记忆（persistent mutable memory）这一核心资源的作用，明确不同记忆机制（压缩循环、完整序列循环、CoT草稿板）在推理能力上的根本差异。

Method: 理论分析（复杂度理论论证）+ 实验验证（指针追踪、联想召回等任务），对比三种记忆机制在相同模型规模下随计算步数增加的推理能力变化。

Result: 压缩循环即使增加步数也无法突破状态大小的推理瓶颈；完整序列状态循环因携带每个输入位置的状态，接近CoT的记忆丰富程度；CoT通过生成显式token形成可增长的草稿板，具有最强的空间推理能力。

Conclusion: 持久可变记忆的容量是测试时推理的关键资源，不同的记忆机制决定了推理能力的上限，这为设计更高效的推理模型提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting and looped Transformers both give a fixed model more test-time computation, but they differ in what they remember. Chain-of-thought stores intermediate state in generated tokens that remain in the context, whereas a looped Transformer carries state through recurrent hidden activations. We argue that this persistent mutable memory is a central resource for test-time reasoning.
  We compare three memory regimes, the compressed latent loop, the full sequence-state loop, and the chain-of-thought scratchpad. Our main result shows that a compressed loop is limited by the size of its recurrent state. Running the loop longer adds computation but does not by itself create a growing scratchpad, so a loop with a small recurrent state remains a small-space reasoner even when run for many steps. Under a standard complexity assumption, such loops cannot decide problems that are P-complete under logspace reductions, whereas polynomial-length chain-of-thought can.
  The separation is specific to compressed loops, as full sequence-state loops carry state at every input position and live in a memory-rich regime closer to explicit scratchpads. Controlled pointer-chasing and associative-recall sweeps illustrate this memory-budget view, with performance sensitive to whether the persistent-state budget matches the task's working-memory demand.

</details>


### [124] [Efficient and Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion Framework for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30776)
*Ha Manh Bui,Metod Jazbec,Eric Nalisnick,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DUAL框架，利用扩散模型先验知识、拉普拉斯近似和距离状态转移检测，在离线到在线强化学习中实现高效微调与不确定性量化，提升在线期望回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线到在线强化学习面临离线与在线分布转移导致的性能下降问题，现有工作通过扩散模型采样轨迹进行微调来缓解，但仍需更高效的不确定性感知方法来平衡探索与利用。

Method: 1) 离线阶段：利用扩散模型先验知识蒸馏快速采样扩散actor策略和转移模型；2) 在线阶段：采用拉普拉斯近似进行不确定性量化，结合距离状态转移偏移检测，改善探索与利用的平衡。

Result: 在多种设置和环境中，DUAL相比现有O2O-RL基线方法显著提升了在线期望回报。

Conclusion: 拉普拉斯近似的actor损失可作为认知不确定性的原则性估计代理，不确定性感知框架有效提升了离线到在线强化学习的样本效率和最终性能。

Abstract: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning (O2O-RL) leverages an offline, pre-trained policy to minimize costly online interactions. Although data-efficient, O2O-RL is susceptible to shifts between offline and online distributions. Existing work aims to mitigate the harm of this shift by finetuning the policy on trajectory data sampled from a diffusion model. Inspired by this line of work, we propose DUAL: an efficient \textbf{D}iffusion \textbf{U}ncertainty-\textbf{A}ware framework for offline-to-online reinforcement \textbf{L}earning. DUAL utilizes the prior knowledge of the diffusion model to distill a fast-sampling diffusion actor policy and transition model in the offline phase. DUAL also employs a Laplace approximation and distance transition-state-shift detection, thereby using uncertainty quantification to improve exploration versus exploitation in the online phase. We formally show that our actor loss with the Laplace approximation provides a proxy for a principled estimate of epistemic uncertainty. Empirically, DUAL improves the online expected return over O2O-RL baselines across multiple settings and environments.

</details>


### [125] [AbstainGNN: Teaching Graph Neural Networks to Abstain for Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30786)
*Xixun Lin,Zhiheng Zhou,Zhengyin Zhang,Yancheng Chen,Shuai Zhang,Ge Zhang,Shichao Zhu,Lixin Zou,Chuan Zhou,Peng Zhang,Shirui Pan,Yanan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AbstainGNN提出了一种基于理论驱动的图分类拒绝框架，允许GNN在不确定时拒绝预测而非强行决策，并通过PAC-Bayesian泛化理论刻画了分类误差与拒绝成本的权衡，采用两阶段训练策略，实验表明在相同拒绝率下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN在面对高不确定性或未知条件时被迫做出预测，导致不可靠的决策，在安全关键场景中产生严重影响，因此需要让GNN具备拒绝不确定预测的能力。

Method: 提出AbstainGNN框架：1) 显式建模预测函数和弃权函数，利用图结构信息；2) 从PAC-Bayesian泛化角度理论刻画分类误差与拒绝成本的权衡，导出统一学习目标；3) 设计两阶段训练策略：预测函数热启动 + 弃权函数校准。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，AbstainGNN在相同拒绝率下实现了优于现有拒绝方法的分类性能。

Conclusion: AbstainGNN通过理论驱动的图分类拒绝框架有效提升了GNN在不确定场景下的决策可靠性，为安全关键应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Graph classification is a core task in graph data mining with widespread real-world applications. Recent advances in graph neural networks (GNNs) have led to substantial performance improvements for graph classification. However, existing GNNs are typically forced to make predictions even under high uncertainty or unknown conditions, resulting in unreliable decisions that can severely impact downstream tasks, particularly in safety-critical scenarios. To address this critical limitation, we propose AbstainGNN, a novel and theory-driven framework for graph classification with abstention, which enables GNNs to reject uncertain predictions instead of producing incorrect decisions. Specifically, AbstainGNN explicitly models both the predictive function and the abstention function, allowing for effective utilization of graph structural information. Moreover, unlike existing heuristic abstention methods, we theoretically characterize the trade-off between classification errors and rejection costs from a PAC-Bayesian generalization perspective, and derive a unified learning objective for model optimization. Guided by this theoretical insight, we further develop an efficient two-stage training strategy consisting of predictive function warm-start and abstention function calibration. Extensive experiments on five benchmark datasets show that AbstainGNN outperforms existing abstention methods, achieving superior classification performance under the same rejection rates.

</details>


### [126] [Smaller Models are Natural Explorers for Policy-Level Diversity in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30789)
*Yiming Ren,Yiran Xu,Zicheng Lin,Chufan Shi,Yukang Chen,Dingdong Wang,Tianhe Wu,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang,Yu Qiao,Ruihang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S2L-PO框架，利用小模型天然的高策略多样性作为探索器来训练大模型，通过渐进退火策略从小模型 rollout 过渡到大模型自采样，在数学推理任务上显著提升性能（如AIME 24上+8.8%）并降低推理计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO方法主要通过增加token级随机性来提升多样性，但这会引入逐歩噪声并导致轨迹不连贯。作者发现同一模型族中较小模型具有更高的策略级多样性（pass@k更高），这种多样性具有时间相关性、逻辑一致性，能为梯度估计提供结构化探索信号，因此希望利用这一特性来改进GRPO。

Method: 提出S2L-PO框架：1）使用固定的小模型作为自然探索器为大模型生成多样化的rollout轨迹；2）设计渐进退火策略，在训练过程中从小模型的离线rollout逐步过渡到大模型自身的采样，平衡探索与利用，避免小模型容量限制导致的中期性能下降。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上取得显著提升，例如在AIME 24上使用1.7B探索器引导8B模型获得+8.8%的准确率提升，同时减少了rollout的计算量。

Conclusion: S2L-PO通过利用小模型的天然策略多样性替代token级噪声注入，结合渐进退火策略，实现了更快的收敛速度和更高的性能上限，为GRPO中的多样性增强提供了新范式。

Abstract: We identify a new dimension for enhancing rollout diversity in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for LLMs. While GRPO relies on diverse rollouts, prevailing strategies primarily increase diversity by injecting more token-level randomness, which may introduce step-wise noise and lead to incoherent trajectories. We uncover that smaller models within the same model family inherently exhibit higher policy-level diversity, indicated by their superior pass@k relative to larger counterparts as sample counts increase. Unlike token-level noise, this diversity is temporally correlated, preserves logical consistency, and provides structured exploration signals for gradient estimation. We thus propose S2L-PO (Small-to-Large Policy Optimization), a framework that leverages fixed small models as natural explorers to train larger models. To balance exploration and exploitation, we design a progressive annealing strategy that transitions from offline small-model rollouts to the large learner's own sampling. This shift elegantly avoids mid-training performance drops caused by the small model's capacity limits, achieving faster convergence and unlocking a higher performance ceiling. S2L-PO improves accuracy on diverse mathematical reasoning benchmarks (e.g., +8.8% on AIME 24 using a 1.7B explorer to guide the 8B model) while reducing rollout compute.

</details>


### [127] [Conformal Reliability: A New Evaluation Metric for Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30807)
*Yachen Gao,Xinwei Sun,Yikai Wang,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang,Jianfeng Feng,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于共形预测的可靠性评分（reliability score），用于评估条件生成模型在最坏情况下的性能，并设计了CReL框架来高效计算该指标，在图像到文本和文本到图像任务上验证了其有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标通常只评估单次输出，未能捕捉生成过程中的变异性或潜在风险，缺乏考虑模型固有不确定性的可靠性指标。

Method: 基于共形预测（conformal prediction）定义可靠性评分，衡量预测集在预设置信水平下的最差性能；提出CReL框架，包含两个核心能力：(i) 构建具有期望覆盖率的预测集；(ii) 在构建的预测集内精确优化可靠性评分。

Result: 提供了覆盖率的理论保证；实验表明该方法比现有方法能产生信息量更丰富的预测集；在合成数据及图像到文本、文本到图像任务上验证了指标的可解释性及计算框架的有效性。

Conclusion: CReL框架能够有效计算条件生成模型的可靠性评分，为评估生成模型的不确定性和风险提供了新视角。

Abstract: Conditional generative models have recently achieved remarkable success in various applications. However, a suitable metric for evaluating the reliability of these models, which takes into account their inherent uncertainty, is still lacking. Existing metrics, which typically assess a single output, may fail to capture the variability or potential risks in generation. In this paper, we propose a novel evaluation metric called reliability score based on conformal prediction, which measures the worst-case performance within the prediction set at a pre-specified confidence level. However, computing this score is challenging due to the high-dimensional nature of the output space and the nonconvexity of both the metric function and the prediction set. To efficiently compute this score, we introduce Conformal ReLiability (CReL), a framework that can (i) construct the prediction set with desired coverage; and (ii) accurately optimize the reliability score within the constructed prediction set. We provide theoretical results on coverage and demonstrate empirically that our method produces more informative prediction sets than existing approaches. Experiments on synthetic data and the image-to-text and text-to-image tasks further demonstrate the interpretability of our new metric, and the validity and effectiveness of our computational framework. Source code can be found at https://ggc29.github.io/CReL/.

</details>


### [128] [IRIS: time-structured manifold projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30810)
*Brian Ondov,Chia-Hsuan Chang,Weipeng Zhou,Xingjian Zhang,Xueqing Peng,Yutong Xie,Huan He,Qiaozhu Mei,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRIS是一种新的流形学习算法，能够在布局中同时融入时间顺序和流形拓扑结构，用于可视化动态生物医学数据（如scRNA-seq、比较宏基因组学和文献数据）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物医学数据（如细胞×基因矩阵）越来越多地以时间序列形式生成，但现有的流形学习算法（如t-SNE和UMAP）无法在布局中纳入时间顺序，从而模糊了细胞类型或其他类别的动态变化过程。

Method: 提出IRIS算法，一种新的流形学习方法，能够在布局中同时按照时间顺序和流形拓扑结构进行结构化排列。

Result: IRIS能够可视化多种动态生物医学数据，包括单细胞RNA测序（scRNA-seq）、比较宏基因组学和文献数据。

Conclusion: IRIS通过引入时间顺序维度，弥补了传统流形学习算法在动态数据可视化方面的不足，能够更清晰地展示生物过程的动态变化。

Abstract: High-dimensional biomedical data, such as cell-by-gene matrices, are increasingly generated temporally. However, Manifold Learning algorithms, like t-SNE and UMAP, cannot incorporate time-ordering in their layouts, obfuscating the dynamics of cell types or other classes. As a solution, we present IRIS, a new Manifold Learning algorithm that structures layouts both chronologically and by manifold topology. IRIS can visualize a wide range of dynamic biomedical data, including scRNA-seq, comparative metagenomics, and literature.

</details>


### [129] [Non-destructive Identification of Oyster Species is possible from Hyperspectral Images with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30811)
*Ethan Kane Waters,Max Wingfield,Aiden Mellor,Paul Stewart,Iman Tahmasbian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用高光谱成像（950-2515nm）结合PLS-DA和CNN模型，成功实现了对黑唇岩牡蛎和悉尼岩牡蛎的非破坏性快速鉴别，PLS-DA模型达到100%的分类准确率，并揭示了两种牡蛎在元素组成和微观结构上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牡蛎物种鉴别对商业养殖开发和供应链溯源至关重要，而传统DNA分析方法具有破坏性且耗时，因此需要一种快速、非破坏性的鉴别方法。

Method: 采集156只活体黑唇岩牡蛎和悉尼岩牡蛎的左、右壳瓣的高光谱图像（950-2515nm），分别采用PLS-DA和CNN模型进行蒙特卡洛交叉验证分类；同时利用电子显微镜测量壳瓣表面和截面的元素及矿物组成，并进行透射率分析。

Result: PLS-DA模型对左、右壳瓣的分类中位数测试集准确率均达100%，优于CNN的83%（左壳）和96%（右壳）。右壳瓣中BL有4层结构而SR仅有2层；BL外层富含碳，SR外层富含氧，差异可能与几丁质和糖蛋白的相对丰度或组成有关。透射率分析表明光可通过壳瓣及边缘传播。

Conclusion: 高光谱成像结合PLS-DA是一种有效的快速、非破坏性牡蛎物种鉴别方法，为海产品供应链溯源提供了新的技术手段。

Abstract: Differentiating between oyster species is important for developing new commercial oyster species suited to production systems and is critical for traceability in seafood supply chains. Common methods, such as DNA profiling, are destructive and time consuming. The possibility of using hyperspectral imaging (HSI) for discriminating between Black-Lip rock (BL) and Sydney rock (SR) oysters was investigated. Live BL and SR samples (N = 156) were scanned with a HSI camera (950-2515nm). Partial Least Square Discriminant Analysis and Convolutional Neural Networks were trained with Monte Carlo Cross Validation to distinguish BL and SR oysters from the spectral reflectance of their left and rights valves. The PLS-DA model successfully distinguished between the species from both the left and right valves with a median test set classification accuracy of 100%, out performing the CNN with 83% and 96% respectively. Elemental and mineralogical composition in the surface and cross-section of oyster valves were measured with electron microscopy. Analysis of the right valve revealed a greater number of layers in BL compared to SR (4 vs 2). The concentrations of carbon and oxygen varied in the outer layer of the right valves, with BL being rich in carbon and SR being rich in oxygen. The variation in carbon and oxygen concentrations observed between BL and SR right valves may reflect differences in the relative abundance or composition of chitin and glycoproteins. This is supported by model-derived wavelength importance corresponding to vibrational modes of functional groups characteristic of these compounds. Transmittance analysis revealed that light was transmitted through the valves, around the valve edges, indicating that the spectral signatures may have been influenced by the other valve or the meat. Ultimately, the findings highlight an effective rapid, non-destructive methodology for oyster species.

</details>


### [130] [Learning Permutation-invariant Macroscopic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30812)
*Zhichao Han,Mengyi Chen,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种置换不变的自编码器框架，用于从无序微观粒子系统中学习低维宏观动力学，通过重建质量分布而非逐样本重建，并在多个物理系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动方法假设微观自由度具有固定顺序，但粒子系统等微观状态本质上是无序的，现有方法无法处理置换不变性，需要一种能够学习置换不变潜在表示的框架。

Method: 采用置换不变的编码器，解码器重建以观测点为中心的质量分布（而非逐样本重建），联合学习宏观动力学和潜在状态。

Result: 在相互作用粒子系统的能量动力学、Lennard-Jones流体的混合动力学、以及聚合物在拉伸力场中的运动视频数据等多个微观场景中验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的置换不变自编码器框架能够有效处理无序微观系统，学习有意义的宏观动力学表征。

Abstract: Accurately modeling the macroscopic dynamics of high-dimensional microscopic systems is of broad interest across the sciences. Many data-driven approaches learn a low-dimensional latent state through an autoencoder trained for pointwise input reconstruction. These methods typically assume a fixed ordering of microscopic degrees of freedom in the input. However, in many settings, such as particle systems, the microscopic state is inherently unordered. This motivates an autoencoder framework that learns permutation-invariant latent representations. To this end, we adopt a permutation-invariant encoder and design the decoder to reconstruct the mass distribution centered at the observed points rather than per-sample reconstruction. We then jointly learn the macroscopic dynamics of the observables together with the latent states. We demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method across a range of microscopic settings, including learning the energy dynamics in interacting particle systems, predicting mixing dynamics in Lennard-Jones fluids, and modeling the stretching dynamics from video data of polymers moving in an elongational force field.

</details>


### [131] [Unlearning in Diffusion Models: A Unified Framework with KL Divergence and Likelihood Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30825)
*Shervin Khalafi,Alejandro Ribeiro,Dongsheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于约束优化的扩散模型遗忘框架，使用KL散度和似然约束实现遗忘与保留之间的最优权衡，并证明了强对偶性，实验表明在概念遗忘和数据遗忘任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型遗忘面临两个根本冲突的目标：移除不想要的数据或概念，同时保持预训练模型的效用。现有方法缺乏 principled 的理论框架来平衡这一权衡。

Method: 提出三种约束优化问题：基于反向KL散度、前向KL散度和似然约束。将遗忘建模为在满足与遗忘分布显式分离约束的条件下，最小化与预训练模型的偏差。尽管KL约束非凸，证明了所有三个问题的强对偶性，从而可以显式刻画最优解并开发原-对偶算法。

Result: KL约束方法在保留-遗忘权衡上优于基于权重的基线方法；基于似然的方法在遗忘效果上匹配基线，同时更好地保留未被遗忘的概念。

Conclusion: 约束优化框架为扩散模型遗忘提供了理论支撑，实现了更好的遗忘与保留权衡，其中基于似然的第三种公式创新且有效。

Abstract: Unlearning in diffusion models aims to remove undesirable data or concepts while preserving the utility of pretrained models -- two fundamentally conflicting objectives. We propose a principled constrained optimization framework that formulates unlearning as minimizing the deviation from a pretrained model, subject to explicit separation constraints from the unlearning distributions. Specifically, we formulate three constrained optimization problems based on reverse and forward KL divergences, and likelihood constraints. The first two generalize existing approaches for concept and data unlearning, while the third offers a novel and natural formulation for unlearning. Despite the nonconvexity of the KL constraints, we establish strong duality for all three problems, enabling us to explicitly characterize their optimal solutions as unlearning targets and develop primal-dual algorithms for each formulation. Experimental results demonstrate that our KL-constrained approach achieves superior retention-unlearning tradeoffs compared to weight-based baselines for concept and data unlearning, and that our likelihood-based approach matches unlearning effectiveness while better preserving retained concepts compared to baselines.

</details>


### [132] [Cross-Layer Subspace Coupling for LLM Compression: A Unifying Framework and Its Empirical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30836)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，虽然统一优化框架（如SVD LLM和Basis Sharing的联合优化）在权重重建误差上可提升高达46%，但在实际任务中（困惑度、准确率）反而显著劣于逐层SVD LLM。原因在于Transformer残差流在前向传播中实际上解耦了相邻层，使得逐层最优性比跨层联合优化更重要。结论指出权重空间重建是跨层压缩的 flawed 目标，未来应聚焦于逐层激活重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于SVD的大语言模型压缩方法（如SVD LLM和Basis Sharing）能否通过统一优化框架进一步提升性能。

Method: 将SVD LLM和Basis Sharing统一为同一优化问题，在Pythia模型上进行数学证明和实验验证，并通过机制分析解释失败原因。

Result: 统一方法在权重重建误差上降低最多46%，但在困惑度和准确率等下游指标上显著劣于标准逐层SVD LLM。

Conclusion: 权重空间重建不适合跨层压缩目标，应转而采用逐层激活重建的方法。

Abstract: Recent SVD based compression methods for large language models like SVD LLM and Basis Sharing can be unified under one optimization problem. While mathematical proofs and tests on Pythia models show this unified approach improves weight reconstruction error by up to 46% percent it fails in practical tasks. Downstream metrics like perplexity and accuracy severely degrade compared to standard per layer SVD LLM. The authors explain this failure mechanistically. Although the bundle method mathematically couples adjacent layers the transformer residual stream actually decouples them during forward passes. Thus per layer optimality matters more than joint cross layer optimization. The paper concludes that weight space reconstruction is a flawed objective for cross layer compression and future methods must focus on per layer activation reconstruction instead.

</details>


### [133] [CoMem: Context Management with A Decoupled Long-Context Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30842)
*Yuwei Zhang,Chengyu Dong,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Hejie Cui,Hongye Jin,Xinyang Zhang,Hamed Bonab,Colin Lockard,Jianshu Chen,Zhenyu Shi,Jingbo Shang,Xian Li,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMem 提出了一种解耦记忆管理与主体工作流的异步框架，通过 k-step-off 异步流水线将记忆模型的摘要生成与主体推理并行执行，结合奖励驱动的训练策略，在 SWE-Bench-Verified 上实现了 1.4 倍延迟提升，同时保持了大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上下文管理通过迭代摘要解决长时域任务，但额外摘要token的解码开销严重影响了端到端响应延迟。现有耦合架构无法并行化记忆压缩与主体推理，导致部署时延瓶颈。

Method: 提出 CoMem 框架：(1) 解耦记忆管理与主体工作流，允许并行执行；(2) k-step-off 异步流水线，将记忆模型的摘要过程与主体推理重叠，掩盖上下文处理延迟；(3) 奖励驱动的训练策略，使记忆模型在异步设置下捕获足够统计量以支持主体决策。

Result: 在 SWE-Bench-Verified 上，CoMem 相比传统长上下文方案实现了 1.4 倍延迟提升，同时保留了大部分性能。延迟增益随系统吞吐量增加而显著提升。

Conclusion: CoMem 提供了更优的效率-效果权衡，为独立优化主体推理和记忆压缩提供了模块化路径。

Abstract: Context management enables agentic models to solve long-horizon tasks through iterative summarization of previous interaction histories. However, this process typically incurs substantial decoding overhead for the extra summarization tokens, which significantly affect the end-to-end response latency at deployment. In this paper, we introduce CoMem, a novel framework that decouples memory management from the primary agent workflow, enabling these processes to execute in parallel. We propose a $k$-step-off asynchronous pipeline that overlaps the memory model's summarization with the agent's inference, effectively masking the latency of context processing. To ensure robustness under this asynchronous setting, we introduce a reward-driven training strategy that aligns the memory model to capture sufficient statistics for the agent's decision-making. Theoretical analysis confirms that CoMem offers a superior efficiency-effectiveness trade-off compared to coupled architectures. Our extensive experimental results on SWE-Bench-Verified show that CoMem provides 1.4x latency improvements upon vanilla long-context solutions while preserving most of the performance. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these latency gains scale favorably with increased system throughput, offering a modular path forward for the independent optimization of agent reasoning and memory compression.

</details>


### [134] [A Lecture Note on Offline RL and IRL, Part II: Foundations of Inverse Reinforcement Learning and Dynamic Discrete Choice Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30843)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了结构计量经济学中的动态离散选择（DDC）模型与机器学习中的熵正则化逆强化学习（IRL）模型是等价的，并系统梳理了两大领域中的经典识别方法、计算范式及其局限性，最后介绍了基于经验风险最小化的梯度估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正向强化学习中奖励是已知的，而逆向强化学习（IRL）旨在从专家离线数据中恢复其优化的奖励函数。两个独立发展的社区——结构计量经济学（DDC）和机器学习（IRL）——实际上在研究同一个概率模型，但缺乏统一的认识和比较。

Method: 首先证明DDC与熵正则化IRL的模型等价性；然后梳理经典识别结果（Magnac & Thesmar）及四种计算范式：Rust嵌套不动点算法、Hotz-Miller条件选择概率方法、Adusumilli & Eckardt的两种时序差分方法（线性半梯度TD和近似值迭代）；接着分析现代ML/IRL方法：对抗IRL、占据匹配、IQ-Learn、离线ML-IRL，阐明其实际目标函数和识别能力；最后介绍Kang等人的经验风险最小化框架，提供离线IRL/DDC的梯度估计器。

Result: 建立了DDC与熵正则化IRL的严格等价关系；系统揭示了各计算范式的局限性——维数灾难、转移核估计困难、"致命三元组"（函数近似、自举、离策略三者结合导致发散）、投影不动点偏差；阐明了各现代IRL方法的实际识别内容与局限。

Conclusion: DDC和IRL本质上解决的是同一个问题，两社区的方法可以互相借鉴；离线环境下的奖励识别需要谨慎对待各方法的假设和偏差来源；Kang等人的经验风险最小化框架为离线IRL/DDC提供了一种统一且实用的梯度估计途径。

Abstract: In the forward reinforcement-learning problem, the reward is fixed and known; the learner is asked to find a good policy or value function. Here we turn the question around. Given offline data generated by an expert, can we recover the reward the expert was optimizing? This is the inverse reinforcement learning problem, and remarkably, two communities, structural econometricians studying dynamic discrete choice (DDC) and machine learners studying entropy-regularized IRL, have been working on exactly the same probabilistic model under different names. We begin by proving their equivalence. We then develop the classical identification result of Magnac and Thesmar and the classical computational paradigms that grew out of it: Rust's nested fixed-point algorithm, the conditional-choice-probability approach of Hotz and Miller, and the two temporal-difference approaches of Adusumilli and Eckardt: linear semi-gradient TD and approximate value iteration. Each route has its limits: dimensionality, transition-kernel estimation, the deadly triad, or projected fixed-point bias. We then walk through the modern ML/IRL strand: adversarial IRL, occupancy matching, IQ-Learn, and offline ML-IRL, deriving each method's actual objective and stating precisely what it does and does not identify. We close with the empirical-risk-minimization framework of Kang et al., which yields a gradient-based estimator for offline IRL/DDC.

</details>


### [135] [ForecastCompass: Guiding Agentic Forecasting with Adaptive Factor Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30858)
*Yurui Chang,Yongkang Du,Yuanpu Cao,Jinghui Chen,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoCo是一个基于自适应因子记忆的智能体预测框架，通过分层预测任务分类、因子记忆和推理记忆组件，以及回忆性分析驱动的记忆修订机制，使智能体能够积累可迁移的预测知识，在Prophet Arena和FutureX数据集上显著提升了概率预测准确性和校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的智能体预测面临证据不完备、时间有限等挑战，现有智能体记忆方法未能专门针对预测任务设计，通常存储通用交互/反思/事实关联，缺乏对可复用预测因子或校准知识的显式表征。

Method: 提出ForecastCompass (FoCo)框架：(1) 构建分层预测任务分类学以组织预测经验；(2) 设计因子记忆（捕获可复用预测维度）和推理记忆（编码概率更新/不确定性处理/校准原则）两个互补组件；(3) 利用回忆性分析作为学习信号，通过言语化记忆修订程序迭代更新记忆。

Result: 在Prophet Arena和FutureX数据集上，使用GPT-5-mini和Gemini-2.5-Flash进行实验，FoCo在概率预测准确性和校准性方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: FoCo通过结构化记忆设计有效提升了智能体在动态环境中的预测能力，验证了将可迁移预测知识显式编码到记忆中的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic forecasting is important for decision-making in dynamic environments, but it remains challenging because agents must reason from incomplete, time-limited evidence and produce calibrated probabilities before outcomes are resolved. Memory provides a natural mechanism for transferring experience from resolved forecasts to future prediction tasks. However, existing agent-memory methods are not tailored to forecasting, as they typically store past interactions, reflections, or factual associations without explicitly representing reusable predictive factors or calibration knowledge. We propose ForecastCompass (FoCo), an adaptive factor-based memory framework for agentic forecasting. FoCo organizes forecasting experience with a hierarchical forecasting-task taxonomy, enabling retrieval task-relevant forecasting knowledge. It maintains two complementary memory components: factor memory, which captures reusable predictive dimensions, and reasoning memory, which encodes probability updating, uncertainty handling, and calibration principles. Using retrospective analyses as learning signals, FoCo iteratively revises memory through a verbalized memory-revision procedure, enabling the agent to accumulate transferable forecasting knowledge over time. Experiments on Prophet Arena and FutureX with GPT-5-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash show that FoCo improves both probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


### [136] [GlucoFM: A Dual-Stream Foundation Model for Continuous Glucose Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30865)
*Zechen Li,Keerthana Natarajan,Weizhi Zhang,Menglian Zhou,Simon A. Lee,Yuwei Zhang,Maxwell A. Xu,Zeinab Esmaeilpour,Flora D. Salim,Mark Malhotra,Lindsey Sunden,Shwetak Patel,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoFM 是一个轻量级 CGM 基础模型，通过将不规则的血糖记录对齐到24小时时间网格，并将血糖动态分解为慢速生理状态流和瞬态事件流，在七个临床预测任务中显著优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列和 CGM 基础模型将血糖轨迹编码为纠缠的单流序列，未能明确建模血糖动态中不同时间尺度的结构化特征，亟需一种能够显式解耦血糖信号中慢速生理状态与快速瞬态事件的方法。

Method: GlucoFM 首先将不规则 CGM 记录对齐到24小时时间网格并保留观测掩码，然后将血糖动态分解为慢速生理状态流和瞬态事件流；采用双重预训练目标：掩码上下文潜在预测（融合日表征）和时间动态预测（状态与事件流）。

Result: 在四个不同队列和七个临床预测任务中，GlucoFM 线性探测平均 PR-AUC 提升 4.1 个点；在所有糖尿病风险和 β 细胞功能障碍任务以及 4 个胰岛素抵抗任务中的 3 个取得最优 PR-AUC；跨数据集迁移和少样本适应性能最佳，多日聚合预测表现一致提升。

Conclusion: 生理学感知的解耦方法作为一种有效的归纳偏置，能够显著提升 CGM 表征学习的迁移能力，GlucoFM 在核心代谢结局预测上优势最为突出。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides a dense view of daily metabolic physiology, yet existing generic time-series and CGM-specific foundation models often encode glucose traces as entangled single-stream sequences, leaving the distinct temporal structure of glycemic dynamics only implicitly modeled. We present GlucoFM, a lightweight CGM foundation model that aligns irregular recordings to a 24-hour chronological grid, preserves observation masks, and decomposes glucose dynamics into slow physiological state and transient event streams, capturing low-frequency glycemic baselines and short-term deviations that may reflect acute physiological responses or sensor artifacts. GlucoFM is pretrained on 109,066 hours of unlabeled CGM recordings from 477 subjects with two complementary objectives: masked contextual latent prediction over fused daily representations and temporal dynamics prediction over state and event streams. Across four diverse cohorts and seven clinical prediction tasks, GlucoFM achieves the strongest subject-disjoint linear-probing performance among evaluated baselines, improving average PR-AUC by 4.1 points over the best CGM-specific foundation model. Its gains are most pronounced on core metabolic outcomes, leading PR-AUC on all diabetes-risk and $β$-cell dysfunction tasks and on 3 of 4 insulin-resistance tasks. GlucoFM also achieves the best overall cross-dataset transfer performance and strong few-shot adaptation among evaluated methods, and consistent gains when aggregating multiple days for subject-level prediction, highlighting physiology-aware decomposition as an effective inductive bias for transferable CGM representation learning.

</details>


### [137] [Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873)
*Jabin Koo,Hoyoung Kim,Minwoo Jang,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedVPA-GP框架，通过联邦混合先验和正交损失解决联邦学习中变分偏好对齐的后验坍塌问题，实现隐私保护下的个性化偏好解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习框架在对齐大语言模型时使用单一奖励模型，平均化了用户间冲突的偏好（如有用性与无害性）。变分偏好学习虽能实现个性化，但在去中心化场景中面临局部数据稀疏和异构性导致的后验坍塌问题。

Method: 提出FedVPA-GP框架：1) 联邦混合先验（Federated Mixture Prior），让客户端利用全局聚合分布作为动态先验以稳定变分推断；2) 正交损失（Orthogonal Loss），在潜空间中显式强制偏好原型分离。

Result: 在HH-RLHF数据集上，FedVPA-GP显著优于单一奖励模型基线，成功解耦了冲突的用户意图，并实现了动态偏好切换。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了联邦学习中个性化偏好对齐的后验坍塌问题，在保护隐私的前提下实现了多样化偏好的解耦，为联邦大语言模型对齐提供了新方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>


### [138] [Automating Formal Verification with Reinforcement Learning and Recursive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30914)
*Max Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何通过来自验证器环境的可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)和验证器引导的推理时搜索来提升大语言模型生成经过验证的程序和证明的能力。在Dafny中，使用GRPO将验证通过率从2.2%提升至58.1%，但发现了"规约破解"问题；经筛选后多轮RLVR将验证通过率从9.7%提升至31.1%。在Lean中，将证明生成建模为对分解子目标的结构化搜索，在VeriCoding上将通过率从46.2%提升至69.2%，并在VERINA数据集中解决了7个之前未解决的任务。同时引入了仓库规模的Lean基准测试Dalek-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化形式化验证仍然是大语言模型的挑战，因为面向证明助手和验证感知语言的数据稀缺，且正确性取决于满足精确的机器可检查规约而非生成合理的代码。本文旨在研究如何利用验证器环境改善LLM生成经过验证的程序和证明。

Method: 1) 在Dafny上使用GRPO及相关变体进行RLVR训练，将生成的候选程序组装为完整程序并用编译器和验证器结果评分；2) 发现并解决"规约破解"问题后，在精炼基准上进行多轮RLVR；3) 在Lean中开发验证器引导的推理脚手架，将证明生成建模为对分解子目标的结构化搜索，结合验证器反馈、诊断和修复；4) 引入仓库规模的Lean基准测试Dalek-Bench。

Result: Dafny RLVR初始实验将验证奖励从2.2%提升至58.1%（但存在规约破解）；经筛选后多轮RLVR将验证通过率从9.7%提升至31.1%。Lean脚手架在VeriCoding上将通过率从46.2%提升至69.2%，在VERINA上解决了7个之前未解决的任务。Dalek-Bench上的初步结果仍然较弱。

Conclusion: 验证器环境结合RLVR和推理时搜索能显著提升LLM生成已验证程序和证明的能力，但仍面临规约破解、长尾困难任务等挑战，未来需要更强的进度评估和任务特定的工具使用策略。

Abstract: Automated formal verification remains challenging for large language models because data for proof assistants and verification-aware languages is scarce, and correctness depends on satisfying precise machine-checkable specifications rather than producing plausible code. This thesis studies how verifier environments can improve LLM generation of verified programs and proofs through reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) and verifier-guided inference-time search. First, we train open-source models in Dafny with RLVR using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related variants, assembling generated candidates into complete programs and scoring them with compiler and verifier outcomes. Initial experiments on an APPS-derived Dafny dataset increased verified reward from 2.2% to 58.1%, but revealed specification hacking, where models exploit weak formal specifications instead of implementing the intended solutions. After filtering underspecified and vulnerable tasks, multi-turn RLVR on the refined benchmark improves the verified pass rate from 9.7% to 31.1%. Second, we develop a verifier-guided inference scaffold in Lean that treats proof generation as structured search over decomposed subgoals, verifier feedback, diagnostics, and repair. With a fixed base model, the full scaffold with proof reviser improves pass rate on an initial VeriCoding pilot set from 46.2% under direct repair to 69.2%. On the larger VERINA dataset, whole-task decomposition plus proof reviser solves 7 of 42 previously unsolved tasks. We also introduce Dalek-Bench, a repository-scale Lean benchmark derived from the Rust $\texttt{curve25519-dalek}$ verification project; preliminary results remain weak, indicating that stronger progress evaluation and task-specific tool-use policies are still needed.

</details>


### [139] [UniRTL: Unifying Code and Graph for Robust RTL Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31040)
*Yi Liu,Hongji Zhang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniRTL提出了一种多模态预训练框架，通过联合利用RTL代码和控制数据流图（CDFG）来学习统一的RTL表示，在性能预测和代码检索任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常仅依赖单一数据模态（RTL代码或图表示），导致学到的表示表达能力和泛化能力有限。RTL的CDFG提供了完整的结构信息，而代码模态编码了语义和功能信息，融合这些互补模态对于全面理解RTL设计至关重要。

Method: 提出UniRTL多模态预训练框架：(1) 通过互掩码建模实现代码与CDFG的细粒度对齐；(2) 采用分层训练策略，包括预训练的图感知分词器、文本（功能摘要）与代码的阶段性对齐，以及最终图集成。

Result: 在性能预测和代码检索两项下游任务的多个设置下，UniRTL一致优于先前方法，展现出更强的鲁棒性和更强大的表示能力。

Conclusion: UniRTL通过多模态融合策略为硬件设计自动化提供了更鲁棒、更强大的基础表示方法，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Developing effective representations for register transfer level (RTL) designs is crucial for accelerating the hardware design workflow. Existing approaches, however, typically rely on a single data modality, either the RTL code or its associated graph-based representation, limiting the expressiveness and generalization ability of the learned representations. For RTL, the control data flow graph (CDFG) offers a comprehensive structural representation that preserves complete information, while the code modality explicitly encodes semantic and functional information. We argue that integrating these complementary modalities is essential for a thorough understanding of RTL designs. To this end, we propose UniRTL, a multimodal pretraining framework that learns unified RTL representations by jointly leveraging code and CDFG. UniRTL achieves fine-grained alignment between code and graph through mutual masked modeling and employs a hierarchical training strategy that incorporates a pretrained graph-aware tokenizer and staged alignment of text (i.e., functional summary) and code prior to graph integration. We evaluate UniRTL on two downstream tasks, performance prediction and code retrieval, under multiple settings. Experimental results show that UniRTL consistently outperforms prior methods, establishing it as a more robust and powerful foundation for advancing hardware design automation.

</details>


### [140] [The Challenges of Using Reinforcement Learning for Controlling Industrial Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31044)
*Tobias Lademann,Théo Vincent,Jan Peters,Matthias Weigold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了强化学习在真实工业能源系统（热力供暖网络）中部署的挑战，发现尽管在仿真中表现良好，但实际部署时存在显著的性能差距，仅能实现运行稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在工业能源系统优化控制中的研究大多局限于仿真环境，缺乏对真实部署挑战的系统性分析。

Method: 将任务形式化为马尔可夫决策过程，沿形式化描述的结构系统分析了部分可观测性、动作空间设计、奖励设计以及仿真到现实的差距等挑战，并在真实工业热力供暖网络中进行部署验证。

Result: 强化学习在实际部署中实现了运行稳定性，但相比仿真环境存在显著的性能差距。

Conclusion: 真实工业能源系统中部署强化学习面临诸多挑战，实际性能与仿真之间存在显著差距，需要进一步研究缩小这一差距的方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has shown promising results for optimizing the control of industrial energy systems, yet most existing studies remain limited to the application in simulation environments. We investigate the challenges of deploying reinforcement learning in a real-world industrial energy system, considering a thermal heating network as a use case. We formulate the task as a Markov Decision Process and systematically analyze the associated challenges along the structure of the formal description, including partial observability, action space design, reward design, and the simulation-to-reality gap. The challenges are grounded in an existing real-world deployment, where reinforcement learning achieves operational stability but shows a significant performance gap compared to simulation.

</details>


### [141] [Learning to Solve and Optimize by Evolving Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31049)
*Veronika Semmelrock,Benedetta Strizzolo,Francesco Zuccato,Gerhard Friedrich,Patrick Rodler,Konstantin Schekotihin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHECKMATE工具，通过代码演化自动生成算法，仅需形式化规范（what）而无需专家指定求解过程（how），在工业配置与调度问题上超越现有求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解决大规模组合优化问题需要专家进行问题形式化、设计专用求解器和启发式规则，即不仅需要指定"是什么"（what），还需要指定"如何做"（how）。这带来了高昂的人力成本和领域知识依赖。

Method: 提出CHECKMATE工具，利用代码演化实现算法自动生成。形式化规范确保解的正确性并支持系统性能评估，自然语言描述引导演化过程。该方法仅依赖"what"（问题规范），无需人工设计"how"（求解过程）。

Result: 在配置与调度两个工业领域的选定问题上，演化生成的算法在所有测试案例中均一致优于最先进的求解器。

Conclusion: 形式化方法在引导代码演化以自动解决复杂现实问题方面具有巨大潜力，标志着从需要专家指定求解过程到仅需问题规范的范式转变。

Abstract: Combinatorial and optimization problems are fundamental to many industrial AI applications. Solving large-scale real-world instances of such problems typically requires careful problem formalization, specialized solvers, and expert-designed heuristics. Thus, experts need to specify not only what solutions are, but also how they are derived. By introducing the tool CHECKMATE, we show that algorithm generation via code evolution represents a paradigm shift by eliminating the need to formulate the how. CHECKMATE solely relies on the what. Specifically, a formal specification ensures solutions' correctness and enables systematic performance evaluation of the generated programs, while a natural language description guides the evolutionary process. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated on selected problems from two industrial domains: configuration and scheduling. In all cases, the evolved algorithms consistently outperform state-of-the-art solvers. This underscores the potential of formal methods in guiding code evolution for automatically solving complex real-world problems.

</details>


### [142] [Best-Arm Identification-Based Trust Region Selection for Bayesian Optimization on Multimodal Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31050)
*Nobuo Namura,Sho Takemori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轨迹感知的贝叶斯优化框架，通过集成最优臂识别与信任区域方法，在多模态优化问题中加速收敛到全局最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯过程贝叶斯优化在复杂多模态或高维问题上性能下降，信任区域方法虽有所缓解但区域选择效率有待提升。

Method: 将信任区域贝叶斯优化中的区域选择建模为多臂老虎机问题，利用最优臂识别（BAI）推断多个局部优化器的优化轨迹并预测其最终性能，逐步淘汰次优候选。

Result: 在温和假设下理论上证明了所提方法比传统BO更快收敛到全局最优，并在合成和真实基准实验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: BAI引导的信任区域BO能有效处理多模态优化问题，提升收敛速度与全局搜索能力。

Abstract: Gaussian process-based Bayesian optimization (BO) is a popular approach for expensive black-box optimization, but its performance often degrades on complex multimodal or high-dimensional problems. Trust region-based BO mitigates this issue by focusing on local regions, and recent studies suggest that selecting an effective region can be formulated as a multi-armed bandit problem. We propose a trajectory-aware framework that integrates best-arm identification (BAI) with trust region-based BO to efficiently solve multimodal optimization problems. Our method extrapolates the optimization trajectories of multiple locally initialized optimizers to predict their final performance and progressively eliminates suboptimal candidates via BAI. We theoretically show that the proposed BAI-guided BO converges faster to the global optimum than conventional BO under mild assumptions, and demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [143] [STEP: Learning STructured Embeddings for Progressive Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31061)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督对比学习方法，用于渐进时间序列的可解释表示学习。通过将观测映射到两个固定正交原型向量锚定的低维流形上，利用极坐标(θ, r)分别追踪状态进展（如健康→失效）和识别运行模式，无需代理标签。在工业退化、机器人任务和神经活动等多个领域验证了端状态预测、多步预测和可解释相位分离能力，性能匹配或超越黑盒方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有渐进时间序列的表示学习方法缺乏可解释性，无法直观揭示底层状态的演化机制和运行模式，且通常依赖代理标签。

Method: 采用自监督对比目标学习低维潜在空间，该空间几何结构本身即为解释：每个观测点位于两个固定正交原型向量锚定的流形上，轨迹形成跨流形的路径。利用潜在向量的极坐标(θ, r)构成"潜在罗盘"：θ追踪状态进展（如健康→失效），r识别活跃模式（如运行条件），无需代理标签。

Result: 在工业退化、机器人任务和神经活动等多种领域验证了三项关键能力：(1)端状态预测，(2)多步预测，(3)可解释相位分离。性能匹配或超越黑盒方法，且潜在罗盘坐标上的简单线性回归器即可与深度架构竞争，证明底层状态以几何可访问形式编码。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持与黑盒方法相当性能的同时，提供了对底层机制的透明性，证明了可解释几何表示在渐进时间序列分析中的有效性。

Abstract: We present a novel method for learning interpretable representations of progressive time series, that is, data capturing irreversible state transitions such as degradation or task completion. Our approach uses a self-supervised contrastive objective to learn a low-dimensional latent space whose geometry is itself the interpretation: each observation becomes a point on a manifold anchored between two fixed orthogonal prototype vectors, and a trajectory becomes a path across that manifold. From this structure we read a latent compass, the polar coordinates (θ, r) of the latent vector, in which θ tracks the progression of the underlying state (e.g., from healthy to failed) and r identifies the active mode (e.g., the operating condition), without any proxy labels. We evaluate the approach against the state of the art on diverse domains, including industrial degradation, robotic tasks, and neural activity, validating three key capabilities: (1) end-state prediction, (2) multi-step forecasting, and (3) interpretable phase separation. Our method matches or improves over black-box counterparts on all of these while providing transparency about the underlying mechanisms. A simple linear regressor on top of the latent compass coordinates is competitive with deep architectures, direct quantitative evidence that the underlying state is encoded in a geometrically accessible form.

</details>


### [144] [Learning to Bid in FCR Markets: A Best-of-Both-Worlds Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31070)
*Marius Potfer,Cheng Wan,Pierre Gruet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对欧洲频率包含备用（FCR）市场的竞价问题，将多国FCR清算问题重新表述为针对内生对手报价向量的重复多单元统一价格拍卖，并适配了一种“两全其美”组合半强盗算法，该算法在随机环境下实现对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境下实现O(√T)遗憾。合成实验验证了理论缩放，历史回测表明该方法在稳定产品上表现优异，而EXP3基线在强非平稳性下更安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲FCR市场中，灵活性提供商面临信息不透明（竞争对手报价隐藏、仅能观察部分市场反馈如清算价格和中标量）的竞价挑战，现有方法缺乏理论保障和实际有效性。

Method: 将多国FCR清算问题重述为针对内生对手报价向量的重复多单元统一价格拍卖，转化为在线学习问题，适配了一种Best-of-Both-Worlds组合半强盗算法，仅需标准市场反馈（清算价格和中标量）即可实现。

Result: 算法在随机环境下实现对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境下实现O(√T)遗憾；合成实验验证了期望的缩放行为；历史欧洲FCR数据回测显示该方法在稳定产品上表现优异，EXP3基线在强非平稳性下更安全。

Conclusion: 基于学习的FCR市场竞价在理论上具有坚实基础，实践中当学习规则与产品层面的市场稳定性相匹配时具有实际可用性。

Abstract: Bidding in the European Frequency Containment Reserve (FCR) market is challenging for flexibility providers because competing offers are hidden and bidders observe only partial feedback form the market, such as, clearing price and awarded quantity. For a participant active in a single country, we show that the multi-country FCR clearing problem can be recast as a repeated multi-unit uniform-price auction against an endogenous vector of opposing bids. This reformulation yields an online learning problem and allows us to adapt a Best-of-Both-Worlds combinatorial semi-bandit algorithm implementable from this standard market feedback. The resulting bidder achieves logarithmic pseudo-regret in stochastic environments and $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret in adversarial ones. Synthetic experiments confirm the expected scaling, and backtests on historical European FCR data show competitive performance in practice: the method performs especially well on stable products, while EXP3-type baselines can be safer under stronger non-stationarity. Overall, the results show that learning-based bidding in FCR markets is theoretically grounded and practically useful when the learning rule matches product-level market stability.

</details>


### [145] [Riemannian Diffusion Models on General Manifolds via Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31106)
*Gyeonghoon Ko,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）直接求解流形上的热方程来近似热核，从而将黎曼扩散模型推广到任意显式指定的流形上，无需依赖封闭形式的热核表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼扩散模型需要采样和微分流形热核，但除少数高对称流形外，热核鲜有封闭形式，限制了模型在一般流形上的应用。

Method: 给定显式流形后，选择坐标系并推导对应的热（Fokker-Planck）方程及短时渐近近似，然后训练PINN学习对数热核，从而同时实现前向加噪（热核采样）和条件评分估计（用于去噪评分匹配）。

Result: 在S²、SO(3)、SPD(n)以及置换商点云等多样流形上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PINN近似热核为将黎曼扩散模型推广到更广泛流形提供了一种通用且实用的途径。

Abstract: Riemannian diffusion models generalize score-based generative modeling to manifold-supported data via stochastic diffusion equations on the manifold. However, training requires sampling from and differentiating the manifold heat kernel, which is rarely available in closed form beyond a few highly symmetric manifolds. We propose a general approach that approximates the heat kernel by directly solving the manifold heat equation with a physics-informed neural network (PINN). Given an explicit manifold specification, we choose a coordinate system, derive the corresponding heat (Fokker--Planck) equation and a short-time asymptotic approximation, and then train a PINN to learn the log heat kernel. The resulting surrogate enables both forward noising (heat-kernel sampling) and conditional-score evaluation for denoising score matching. We demonstrate the method on diverse manifolds including $S^2$, $SO(3)$, $\mathrm{SPD}(n)$, and permutation-quotiented point clouds.

</details>


### [146] [Subspace-Decomposed JEPAs: Disentangling Progression and Content in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31111)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SD-JEPA将JEPA潜空间正交分解为低维进度子空间（余弦三元组损失）和高维内容子空间（SIGReg），实现训练时反坍缩力在正交坐标上的加性叠加，在控制基准上超越LeWM基线，且1D角度进度坐标具备场景感知罗盘能力，可定位语义事件并区分意外与意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA学习紧凑潜世界模型，但潜变量中没有任何单一坐标编码任务进度，缺乏显式的进度表征结构。

Method: 将JEPA潜空间正交分解为低维进度子空间（余弦三元组损失塑造）和高维内容子空间（SIGReg正则化），证明两种反坍缩力作用于不相交坐标，实现加性组合而非竞争。

Result: SD-JEPA在多数控制基准上超越LeWM基线；1D角度进度坐标的|Δθ_t|在40个保留立方体场景中定位语义事件的pooled AUROC提升最高+0.18（97.5%每集胜率）；8维进度子空间（占潜空间4.2%）解释72-95%任务进度方差。

Conclusion: 潜空间正交分解实现进度与内容的解耦表征，1D角度坐标既是场景感知罗盘，又能在扰动下重定位到语义正确的任务阶段，区分预测误差标量无法区分的"意外"与"意义"。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models by predicting future embeddings, but no single coordinate of the latent is designated to encode task progression. We carve the JEPA latent into two orthogonal subspaces with disjoint roles: a low-dimensional progression subspace shaped by a cosine-margin triplet loss, and a high-dimensional content subspace regularised by the existing SIGReg objective of LeWM. We prove that the two anti-collapse forces act on disjoint coordinates, so they compose additively rather than competing on the same dimensions. Our method, SD-JEPA improves over the LeWM baseline on the majority of its control benchmarks at matched compute, and outperforms the strongest non-LeWM JEPA baseline on Push-T; a subspace-ablation falsifier confirms the split is the load-bearing ingredient. Beyond planning, the resulting 1-D angular progression coordinate functions as a scene-aware compass on the latent. It advances with task progress, regresses when the agent backtracks, and under controlled perturbations both spikes and relocalises to a semantically appropriate new task-phase sector, separating the moment of surprise from its meaning in a way that prediction-error scalars cannot. Three quantitative tests back this up: $|Δθ_t|$ outperforms the standard latent-prediction-error surprise at localising semantic events on 40 held-out cube episodes by up to +0.18 pooled AUROC (97.5% per-episode win rate at $\pm 1$-step tolerance); a within-episode linear probe across all four environments (40 episodes per env) shows the 8-dimensional progression subspace (4.2% of the latent) explains 72-95% of task-progress variance..

</details>


### [147] [Scalable Bayesian Inference for Nonlinear Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31127)
*Tim Weiland,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯过程先验的数值守恒方法，用于非线性守恒定律的不确定性量化，在正向模拟中继承了经典求解器的精度并提供了结构化不确定性，在逆问题中能在数秒内恢复非参数源场的后验分布，优于需要数分钟且精度较低的神经基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性守恒定律在实际应用中常受稀疏或噪声测量等不确定性因素影响，导致反问题不适定，传统数值方法和深度学习方法均难以恰当处理不确定性。

Method: 将经典数值方法框架重新构建为高斯过程先验下的贝叶斯推断，利用稀疏近似技术扩展到大规模正向和逆问题，实现数值守恒的不确定性感知模拟。

Result: 正向模拟继承经典求解器精度并提供结构化不确定性量化；逆问题可在数秒内恢复非参数源场的后验分布，性能远超需要数分钟且仅能提供点估计的神经基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在不牺牲数值守恒性的前提下，有效解决了非线性守恒定律中不确定性量化的难题，在大规模正向和逆问题中兼具精度与效率优势。

Abstract: Nonlinear conservation laws are at the heart of many of the most important dynamical systems in science and engineering. In practical applications, such systems are often subject to various sources of uncertainty, e.g. due to sparse or noisy measurements. Inferring physical quantities and fields of interest then becomes an ill-posed problem which both classical numerical methods and modern deep learning-based methods struggle to treat appropriately. Recent work has framed classical numerical methods as Bayesian inference under Gaussian process priors, resulting in a physics-aware treatment of uncertainties. Following this line of work, we develop a novel numerically conservative method for uncertainty-aware simulations of nonlinear conservation laws. We use recent sparse approximation techniques to scale up to large-scale forward and inverse problems. For forward simulation, we inherit the accuracy of classical solvers while providing structured uncertainty quantification. On inverse problems, we recover posteriors over nonparametric source fields in seconds -- outperforming neural baselines that take minutes to produce a less accurate point estimate.

</details>


### [148] [Generalizing Multi-Scale Time-Series Modeling with a Single Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31129)
*Cheonwoo Lee,Dooho Lee,Doyun Choi,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SiGMA，一种基于可学习离散高斯（LDG）核的统一多尺度时间序列预测架构，解决了现有方法依赖固定离散尺度的局限性，在长期和短期预测任务上均取得最优性能，同时训练速度提升高达5.3倍，内存消耗降低高达3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测中的多尺度建模方法缺乏理论基础，且存在依赖固定和离散尺度的根本性局限，亟需一种更具原则性的统一框架来克服这一问题。

Method: 将现有尺度化方法统一为尺度算子族，并在此基础上提出SiGMA架构，核心是可学习离散高斯（LDG）核，该核基于尺度空间理论实现距离感知的尺度化，支持连续可学习的多尺度表示。

Result: 在长期和短期预测基准上全面优于所有多尺度基线模型；在16个长期预测设置中取得13个最佳结果；训练速度提升最高5.3倍，内存消耗降低最高3.8倍。

Conclusion: SiGMA通过可学习的连续尺度化机制，在预测精度和计算效率上均显著超越现有方法，为多尺度时间序列建模提供了更加原则化且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-scale modeling has emerged as an effective design principle for time-series forecasting by capturing temporal dynamics at multiple resolutions. As no principled foundation has been established in the literature, we unify existing scaling methods into a scaling operator family, revealing a fundamental limitation of existing approaches: reliance on fixed and discrete scaling. To address this limitation, we propose SiGMA (Single Generalized Multi-scale Architecture), which enables distance-aware scaling via the learnable discrete Gaussian (LDG) kernel grounded in scale-space theory.
