We evaluate SiGMA comprehensively on long- and short-term forecasting benchmarks against state-of-the-art multi-scale baselines. SiGMA outperforms all competitors on both tasks, especially achieving the best performance in 13 out of 16 long-term evaluation settings. Beyond accuracy, SiGMA significantly improves training speed by up to 5.3 times and reduces memory consumption by up to 3.8 times over the strongest competitors. Code is available at https://github.com/cheonwoolee/SiGMA.

</details>


### [149] [Learning Hyperspherical Time-Frequency Representations for Time-Series Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31155)
*Willian T. Lunardi,Samridha Shrestha,Martin Andreoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超球面嵌入的时序列分布外（OOD）检测方法，通过von Mises-Fisher（vMF）似然目标学习类别条件结构，结合时域和频域编码器构建联合嵌入空间，在UCR和UEA数据集上使用k-NN和马氏距离进行OOD检测，效果优于对比学习和后处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序列数据的OOD检测相较于视觉和语言领域研究不足，缺乏如何利用监督式时序列表示进行可靠分布外检测的原则性理解。

Method: 将时序列OOD检测形式化为超球面嵌入的表示学习，利用von Mises-Fisher（vMF）似然目标在单位球面上诱导类别条件结构；通过时域和频域专用编码器结合输入信号的时频视图，集成到联合嵌入空间中；使用基于距离的评分（k-NN和马氏距离）进行OOD检测。

Result: 在UCR和UEA时序列档案的跨数据集协议下，使用k-NN和马氏距离评分均一致优于强对比学习和后处理基线方法。

Conclusion: 超球面嵌入结合时频域联合表示能有效提升时序列OOD检测性能，为该领域提供了有原则的解决方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection for time-series data remains comparatively underexplored compared to vision and language, with a limited principled understanding of how supervised time-series representations can be leveraged for reliable detection under distributional shifts. This work formulates time-series OOD detection as representation learning with hyperspherical embeddings, where class-conditional structure is induced by a von Mises-Fisher (vMF) likelihood-based objective on the unit sphere. The learned representation combines time- and frequency-domain views of the input signal via domain-specific encoders, integrating them into a joint embedding space for OOD detection. Detection uses distance-based scores over the learned embeddings, including k-nearest neighbors (k-NN) and Mahalanobis scores. We evaluate the approach at scale on the complete UCR and UEA time-series archives under a cross-dataset protocol. Empirical results show consistent improvements under both k-NN and Mahalanobis scoring over strong contrastive learning and post-hoc baselines in the same setting. Code is available at https://github.com/tiiuae/hypertf-time-series-ood.

</details>


### [150] [TabCausal: Pretraining Across Causal Environments for Tabular Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31156)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabCausal，一种通过广泛因果预训练（涵盖多种图先验、结构机制、噪声模型等）构建的因果发现基础模型，采用动态任务构建策略从观测和混合干预数据中学习可迁移的结构知识，在大规模合成基准和语义因果环境基准上均优于多种基线方法，尤其擅长处理干预证据下的结构恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现基础模型性能有限，难以持续匹配经典方法，关键瓶颈在于因果预训练任务的构建方式不够有效。

Method: 提出TabCausal，采用数据驱动的因果预训练方式，覆盖多样化的图先验、结构机制、噪声模型、维度、样本量和干预机制，并通过动态任务构建策略组合这些因果环境，形成多样化的发现任务，从而学习可迁移的结构学习能力。

Result: 在大规模合成基准上，TabCausal的宏平均性能优于多种因果发现基线方法。在引入的协议引导且LLM审核的语义因果环境基准上，TabCausal在合成和语义环境中均展现出鲁棒的结构恢复能力，尤其在干预证据下表现突出。

Conclusion: 广泛的因果预训练是实现可迁移的摊销因果发现的关键要素，TabCausal验证了这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to recover directed causal relations from observational and interventional data, providing a basis for mechanistic understanding and reliable decision-making. Causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) seek to amortize this problem by mapping a dataset directly to a causal graph in a single forward pass, avoiding per-dataset testing, search, or optimization. However, existing CDFMs remain limited, often failing to consistently match strong classical methods, and we find that a key bottleneck is how causal pretraining tasks are constructed. Based on this observation, we propose TabCausal, a data-driven CDFM trained with broad causal pretraining over diverse graph priors, structural mechanisms, noise models, dimensions, sample sizes, and intervention regimes. A dynamic task construction strategy composes these causal environments into varied discovery tasks, enabling more transferable structural learning from observational and mixed-interventional data. On large-scale synthetic benchmarks, TabCausal achieves better macro-averaged performance than a diverse set of causal discovery baselines. To further bridge abstract synthetic generators and realistic causal reasoning scenarios, we introduce a protocol-guided and LLM-audited semantic causal environment benchmark, where domain-grounded SCMs generate interpretable observational and interventional datasets for out-of-distribution analysis. Across both synthetic and semantic environments, TabCausal demonstrates robust structure recovery, especially under interventional evidence, highlighting broad causal pretraining as a key ingredient for transferable amortized causal discovery.

</details>


### [151] [Trust-Region Behavior Blending for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31159)
*Daniil Plyusov,Alexey Gorbatovski,Alexey Malakhov,Nikita Balagansky,Boris Shaposhnikov,Daria Korotyshova,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出信任域行为混合（TRB）方法，通过将学生早期 rollout 策略替换为教师最近的行为策略（在KL信任域内）进行预热，然后退火至纯学生 rollout，解决了在线策略蒸馏中早期 rollout 质量差的问题，在数学推理蒸馏任务上取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中，学生早期的 rollout 质量较差，导致教师监督建立在薄弱或低质量的前缀上，影响蒸馏效果。

Method: 提出信任域行为混合（TRB）预热方法：在学生为中心的KL信任域内，将早期 rollout 策略替换为最接近教师的行为策略，同时保持每个前缀的反向KL OPD损失不变；然后逐步将KL预算退火至零，使训练在预热后恢复为纯学生 rollout。

Result: 在两个数学推理蒸馏设定中，TRB 在所有对比方法中取得了最强的平均性能。

Conclusion: TRB 作为一种简单有效的预热方法，通过信任域行为混合缓解了在线策略蒸馏中早期 rollout 质量差的问题，在数学推理任务上优于现有方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on prefixes sampled from its own policy while matching a stronger teacher. This addresses the prefix mismatch of offline distillation, but early student rollouts can still be poor, placing teacher supervision on weak or low-quality prefixes. We propose Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB), a warmup method that replaces the early rollout policy with the closest-to-teacher behavior policy inside a student-centered KL trust region, while keeping the per-prefix reverse-KL OPD loss unchanged. The KL budget is annealed to zero, so training returns to pure student rollouts after warmup. Across two math-reasoning distillation settings, TRB attains the strongest average among the compared methods.

</details>


### [152] [Convergence of Two-Timescale Markovian Stochastic Approximations with Applications in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31172)
*Vagul Mahadevan,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双时间尺度随机逼近算法在马尔可夫噪声下的稳定性和收敛性，无需投影算子或紧致空间假设。关键创新是用慢时间尺度参数的运行最大值来控制快时间尺度参数，并首次证明了带资格迹的TDC算法在离策略线性函数逼近下的几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前双时间尺度SA算法的稳定性和收敛性仅在i.i.d.噪声下被证明，而在强化学习中更实际的马尔可夫噪声设置下缺乏理论保证。

Method: 提出用慢时间尺度参数的运行最大值（running max）而非当前值来控制快时间尺度参数，从而在马尔可夫噪声下建立稳定性和收敛性。

Result: 首次建立了双时间尺度SA在马尔可夫噪声下的稳定性和收敛性，无需投影算子且噪声无需紧致空间假设；作为应用，首次证明了带资格迹的TDC算法在离策略线性函数逼近下的几乎必然收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作为强化学习中更实际的马尔可夫噪声设置下的双时间尺度算法提供了理论基础，特别是为TDC等算法提供了严格的收敛保证。

Abstract: This work studies the convergence of two-timescale stochastic approximations (SA), a class of iterative algorithms that update two sets of parameters in fast and slow timescales respectively. Notable examples of two-timescale SA in reinforcement learning (RL) include temporal difference learning with gradient correction (TDC) and actor-critic methods. Previously, the stability (i.e., boundedness) and convergence of two-timescale SA were only established under i.i.d. noise. This work instead establishes the stability and convergence of two-timescale SA under Markovian noise, a setup that is more realistic in RL. Notably, we do not need to use any projection operator and the noise does not need to live in a compact space. Our key technical novelty is to control the fast timescale parameter with the running max of the slow timescale parameter, instead of with the current slow timescale parameter, as most prior works do. As a key application, we establish the first almost sure convergence of TDC with eligibility traces under off-policy learning with linear function approximation.

</details>


### [153] [Retriever Portfolios: A Principled Approach to Adaptive RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31176)
*Miltiadis Stouras,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Silvio Lattanzi,Ola Svensson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自动选择少量多样化检索器组合（Portfolio）的方法，以覆盖异构查询分布，并通过预期最优-k目标实现近似最优的组合构建，在多个QA基准上超越单检索器和朴素多检索器基线，同时相比推理时调参方法，固定组合能实现并行检索、更低延迟与更优成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统使用单一检索器和固定超参数，无法有效应对从简单事实查询到复杂多跳推理的高度异构查询分布。

Method: 从大量候选检索器中自动选择少量多样化子集（Portfolio），形式化为基于查询分布的期望最优-k目标，提出高效且具有近似最优保证的组合构建算法及路由流水线。

Result: 在多个QA基准上，该组合与路由方法在检索指标和答案质量上均优于单检索器及朴素多检索器基线；与推理时超参数调优相比，固定组合在实现相当或更好精度的同时，显著降低延迟和令牌成本。

Conclusion: 学习到的检索器组合与路由策略可以有效覆盖异构查询分布，兼顾检索性能与推理效率，为RAG系统提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rely on a single retriever and a single set of hyperparameters, despite facing highly heterogeneous queries that range from simple factoid questions to complex multi-hop reasoning. We propose a method that automatically selects a small, diverse subset of retrievers (a portfolio) from a large pool of candidates, to cover different regions of the target query distribution. We formalize this setting via an expected best-of-$k$ objective over the query distribution and show that it admits an efficient portfolio construction algorithm with near-optimal guarantees. Across multiple QA benchmarks, our learned portfolios and router pipeline consistently outperform single-retriever and naive multi-retriever baselines on both retrieval metrics and answer quality. In addition, compared to inference-time hyperparameter tuning approaches, fixed portfolios enable parallel retrieval and LLM calls, achieving comparable (and sometimes better) accuracy with substantially lower latency and token cost.

</details>


### [154] [How well does Classification Accuracy capture Concept Drift Detection Quality? An overview of Concept Drift Detection evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31186)
*Joanna Komorniczak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对数据流概念漂移检测领域缺乏统一评估框架的问题，系统研究了8种漂移检测质量指标与分类性能之间的关系，利用7种合成数据流生成工具并考虑漂移动态因素，旨在识别最具信息量的评估指标集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前概念漂移检测研究缺乏统一的评估框架，现有研究常依赖分类质量指标来评估漂移检测，但这些指标受多种因素影响，无法可靠反映检测质量，亟需建立更科学的评估方法。

Method: 研究分析了8种漂移检测质量指标与分类器性能之间的关系，使用7种合成非平稳数据流生成工具进行实验，并将漂移动态作为影响因素纳入分析。

Result: 识别出最具信息量的漂移检测质量指标集，并深入理解了不同指标在评估漂移检测方法时的表现特征。

Conclusion: 提出了更可靠的漂移检测评估指标体系，为建立统一的概念漂移检测评估框架提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Data streams are nowadays among the most frequently analyzed data structures, with the concept drift posing a major challenge encountered by processing systems. Despite the proposition of numerous solutions to counteract the accuracy degeneration due to concept drift, the scientific community has not yet established a unified framework for evaluating the concept drift detection task. Existing research often relies on classification quality metrics, but these can be affected by multiple factors and may not reliably reflect drift detection quality. In this work, we present an in-depth overview of the relationship between metrics for quantifying drift detection quality and classification performance in synthetic nonstationary data streams. The proposed research studies eight drift detection quality metrics in relation to the classifier's performance across seven synthetic data stream generation tools, additionally considering drift dynamics as a factor. The studies aim to identify the most informative set of drift detection quality metrics and provide a deep understanding of the method's evaluation.

</details>


### [155] [FlagGAM: Rule-Based Generalized Additive Modeling for Explainable Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31189)
*Zijie Zhao,Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlagGAM是一个基于规则基函数的预测框架，通过分离特征级规则构建与预测过程，在保留稀疏规则矩阵的同时支持混合类型任务，在透明性、准确性和鲁棒性之间取得了实用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的表格预测任务需要模型同时满足准确性、透明性和对不完美输入的鲁棒性，现有方法难以兼顾这些需求。

Method: 提出FlagGAM框架：1) Flag核心模块将数值和类别变量转换为稀疏、可读的单变量基函数（阈值标志、类别级标志、尾偏差基、类别阶梯函数）；2) 默认加性头将这些基函数组合为受限GAM风格预测器；3) 保留稀疏规则矩阵而非压缩为计数摘要，支持混合类型分类/回归、特征级加权和灵活预测头。

Result: 默认FlagGAM在透明加性模式下接近EBM；在混合类型回归上显著优于岭回归；在缺失和噪声扰动下AUROC下降小于常见基线；灵活头进一步提升精度，接近强树基线。

Conclusion: FlagGAM为需要竞争性准确率、可沟通规则和对不完美输入鲁棒性的表格预测场景提供了一种实用的中间方案。

Abstract: Tabular prediction in high-stakes domains requires models that are accurate, transparent, and robust to imperfect inputs. We propose FlagGAM, a rule-defined basis framework that separates feature-level rule construction from prediction. A Flag Core Module converts numerical and categorical variables into sparse, human-readable univariate bases, including threshold flags, category-level flags, tail-deviation bases, and categorical step functions; a default additive head then combines these bases as a restricted GAM-style predictor. Rather than reducing triggered rules to compact count summaries, FlagGAM retains a sparse rule-basis matrix that supports mixed-type classification and regression, feature-specific weighting, and optional flexible prediction heads. Across tabular benchmarks, default FlagGAM remains close to EBM in transparent additive mode, improves substantially over ridge regression on mixed-type regression, and shows smaller AUROC degradation than common baselines under missing and noisy perturbations. Flexible heads further improve accuracy and approach strong tree-based baselines, with the caveat that the resulting model should be interpreted as a rule-basis representation followed by a nonlinear predictor rather than as a fully additive GAM. Overall, FlagGAM provides a practical middle ground for tabular settings that require competitive accuracy, communicable rules, and robustness to imperfect inputs.

</details>


### [156] [Student Capacity Moderates Knowledge Distillation Effectiveness: A Systematic Study Across ResNet Teacher-Student Pairs on CIFAR-10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31191)
*Umut Onur Yasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了ResNet在CIFAR-10图像分类中师生网络容量关系对知识蒸馏效果的影响，发现学生容量是关键调节因素、实现正确性对特征蒸馏至关重要、以及适配输入分辨率是蒸馏有效的前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究师生网络之间的容量关系如何影响知识蒸馏的有效性，并在可控可重复的条件下系统比较Logit-KD与Feature-KD在不同师生对上的表现差异。

Method: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，使用ResNet构建三个师生对（R50->R18, R34->R18, R50->R34），采用Logit-KD和Feature-KD两种蒸馏方法，在3个随机种子下进行实验并报告均值±标准差，同时对比实现正确性（梯度裁剪bug修正前后）和输入分辨率适配（修正ResNet主干网络）的影响。

Result: 1) 学生容量是蒸馏增益的关键调节因素：R34学生比R18学生从KD中获益更多，R50->R34 Feature-KD取得最大增益+0.30pp，而R34->R18 Feature-KD只有+0.18pp，R34->R18 Logit-KD增益为0.00pp。2) 实现正确性影响巨大：梯度裁剪bug修正后，Feature-KD在三个师生对中的两个达到或超过Logit-KD性能，R50->R34从95.25%基线提升至95.55%。3) 输入分辨率适配是蒸馏前提：修正32x32输入的ResNet主干网络使教师准确率提升超过5pp，比任何蒸馏增益高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 学生容量比师生准确率差距更能预测蒸馏收益；Feature-KD的实现细节（如梯度裁剪范围）会显著影响其与Logit-KD的比较结论；适配输入分辨率的架构设计是有效蒸馏的绝对前提条件。

Abstract: We investigate how teacher-student capacity relationships modulate knowledge distillation (KD) effectiveness in ResNet-based image classification on CIFAR-10. Across three teacher-student pairs -- R50->R18, R34->R18, and R50->R34 -- we compare Logit-KD and Feature-KD under controlled, reproducible conditions (3 seeds, mean+/-std reported throughout). We report three main findings. First, student capacity is a key moderating factor in distillation gain: R34 students benefit substantially more from KD than R18 students even when teacher-student accuracy gaps are comparable, with the strongest gain of +0.30pp observed for R50->R34 Feature-KD versus +0.18pp for R34->R18 Feature-KD and +0.00pp for R34->R18 Logit-KD. Second, implementation correctness critically affects Feature-KD: a gradient clipping bug that excluded projection layers suppressed Feature-KD performance and produced misleading comparisons with Logit-KD. After correction, Feature-KD matches or outperforms Logit-KD in two of three pairs, reaching 95.55% on R50->R34 against a baseline of 95.25%. Third, input-resolution-aware architecture is a prerequisite for effective distillation: correcting the ResNet stem for 32x32 inputs raises teacher accuracy by over 5pp -- an order of magnitude larger than any KD gain. All code and results are available at github.com/umutonuryasar/kd-capacity-gap.

</details>


### [157] [Geometry-based Schrödinger Bridges for Trustworthy Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31193)
*Jiayu Xiong,Jing Wang,Qi Zhang,Wanlong Wang,Jun Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于几何的多模态融合方法（GMF），利用扩散薛定谔桥传输的平方初始速度作为独立于模型预测的可靠性信号，有效检测低质量数据（噪声、缺失、冲突），打破传统置信度方法的循环依赖，显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信融合方法依赖模型自身预测置信度来判断数据质量，形成循环依赖：当模型自信但错误时，这些方法无法检测到错误。需要一种独立于预测的可靠性评估机制。

Method: 提出GMF框架，不依赖预测结果，而是通过测量输入在潜空间中所需的传输校正量来评估可靠性。具体实现使用扩散薛定谔桥传输（Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge）结合Rectified Flow，以平方初始速度作为高效的学习校正分数。有效数据具有低平方速度量级，而噪声、不完整或冲突数据需要更强的传输校正。

Result: 实验表明，GMF在严重传感器噪声和语义冲突场景下，相比基于置信度的基线方法显著提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于几何的可靠性信号可作为独立于分类器的"法官"，有效标记不可靠输入，即使分类器被欺骗也能正常工作，打破了传统方法的循环依赖问题。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal systems must be robust against low-quality data, such as sensor noise, incomplete multimodal data and conflicting inputs. However, existing trustworthy fusion methods rely on the model's own prediction confidence to judge data quality. This creates a circular dependency: when a model is confident but wrong, these methods fail to detect the error. To break this loop, we propose Geometry-based Multimodal Fusion (GMF). Instead of relying on predictions, we evaluate reliability by measuring how much transport correction the input needs in latent space. We implement Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge transport with Rectified Flow, where the squared initial velocity gives an efficient learned correction score. Valid data has low squared velocity magnitude, while noisy, incomplete data or conflicting data requires stronger transport correction. This geometry-based reliability signal acts as an independent judge, effectively flagging unreliable inputs even when the classifier is fooled. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GMF significantly improves robustness against severe sensor noise and semantic conflicts compared to confidence-based baselines.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Additive Decompositions: Interpretability Through Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31200)
*Jinyang Liu,Munir Eberhardt Hiabu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tensor Separation Learning (TSL)，一种通过秩-1乘积和逐步贪婪过程学习的回归模型，避免了加法模型在强交互下的信号抵消和外推问题，并保证了可视化对拟合组件的忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释机器学习方法（如GAM、SHAP、functional ANOVA）依赖加法表示，在存在强交互作用时会出现信号抵消和超出支持范围的外推问题，导致信息损失。

Method: 提出TSL模型，学习一组单变量特征函数的秩-1乘积之和，采用逐步贪婪过程配合正交重构进行训练。该模型可通过一阶偏依赖函数完全重构（相差常数因子），保证可视化与拟合组件的对应关系。

Result: 为具有有界混合p阶偏导数的函数建立了逼近率保证，并在回归基准测试中证明TSL可与黑盒模型竞争。

Conclusion: TSL通过分离性约束避免了加法投影的信息损失，在保持可解释性的同时达到了与黑盒模型竞争的性能，是一种忠实且准确的可解释机器学习方法。

Abstract: Interpretable machine learning requires models that are accurate and structurally faithful to the data.Existing explainability methods rely heavily on additive representations (e.g., Generalized Additive Models (GAMs), SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), functional ANOVA), which can suffer from signal cancellation and off-support extrapolation in the presence of strong interactions. We propose Tensor Separation Learning (TSL), a regression model that learns a sum of rank-1 products of univariate per-feature functions via a stagewise greedy procedure with orthogonal refitting. By enforcing separability, TSL avoids the information loss inherent in additive projections caused by marginalizing higher-order interactions. The learned TSL model can be fully reconstructed from first-order partial dependence functions, up to constant factors. This stage-wise correspondence ensures that the resulting visualizations are faithful to the fitted components. We establish approximation-rate guarantees for functions with bounded mixed $p$-th order partial derivatives and demonstrate that TSL competes with black-box models on regression benchmarks.

</details>


### [159] [Fixed-Point Masked Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31215)
*Andrea Miele,Yiming Qin,Alba Carballo-Castro,Justin Deschenaux,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFRe框架，通过将掩码生成模型中的部分去噪器替换为基于共享注意力层的定点求解器，并结合跨步一致性损失与三状态复用策略，实现了自适应深度、更少参数和更强低预算生成性能，在文本和图像任务上显著降低训练成本并提升质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码生成模型（MGMs）依赖全序列双向Transformer进行每一步去噪，训练成本高且在低采样预算下质量下降。虽然已有工作通过更好的采样器或固定深度去噪器提升效率，但每个精炼步骤仍分配固定计算量，缺乏自适应深度机制。

Method: 1) 将部分去噪器替换为基于共享注意力层的定点求解器，实现自适应深度且更少参数；2) 引入跨步一致性损失（cross-step consistency loss），对齐相邻去噪步骤的隐藏表示；3) 提出三状态复用策略（3SR），根据未变化、仍掩码和新揭示三种token状态分别初始化求解器；4) 支持从预训练MGM微调转换，避免完整重训练。

Result: 在OpenWebText上，参数量减少38.8%，训练时间减少11.5%，VRAM减少16.9%，在96次Transformer块前向传播预算下生成困惑度从830.8降至101.8（对比MDLM）。在ImageNette上，训练时间减少48.6%，VRAM减少50.7%，所有采样预算下的FID均有提升。

Conclusion: CoFRe提供了一种实用的训练到推理框架，在降低训练成本的同时，显著提升了低预算掩码生成的质量，适用于多种模态。

Abstract: Masked Generative Models (MGMs) enable parallel decoding and achieve strong performance across modalities, but require full-sequence bidirectional transformers at every step, making training costly and degrading quality under low sampling budgets. Existing work improves efficiency via better samplers or cheaper fixed-depth denoisers, but they still allocate a fixed amount of denoiser computation to each refinement step. We introduce Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs), which replace part of the denoiser with a fixed-point solver over shared attention layers to enable adaptive depth with fewer parameters. To make it more effective for masked generation, we first introduce a cross-step consistency loss, which aligns hidden representations at neighboring denoising steps and, second, three-state reuse (3SR) which warm-starts the solver using the previous solution by treating differently unchanged, still-masked, and newly revealed tokens respectively. Together, these components define our complete training-to-inference framework for fixed-point masked generation, \emph{CoFRe}. We also show that pre-trained MGMs can be converted into FP-MGMs with short fine-tuning, avoiding full retraining. Across modalities, CoFRe improves the quality and cost trade-off. On OpenWebText, CoFRe reduces parameters by 38.8\%, training time by 11.5\%, and VRAM by 16.9\%, while improving generative perplexity from 830.8 to 101.8 at a budget of $96$ transformer-block forward passes, compared to MDLM. In ImageNette, CoFRe reduces training time by 48.6\% and VRAM by 50.7\%, while improving FID in all sample budgets tested. Overall, CoFRe offers a practical framework for cheaper training and stronger low-budget masked generation.

</details>


### [160] [Multivariate Distributional Reinforcement Learning Using Sliced Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31222)
*Baptiste Debes,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出切片分布强化学习（SDRL），通过投影将一维散度扩展到多变量回报分布，证明了均匀切片在共享标量折扣下的贝尔曼收缩，以及最大切片在密集折扣矩阵下的收缩，并在多个环境中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习（DRL）建模完整回报分布而非期望，但扩展到多变量设置存在困难：常见度量难以自然推广到一维以上或失去计算可行性，且多变量情形引入通用矩阵折扣等额外挑战，尚无收缩结果可用。

Method: 提出SDRL框架，通过随机投影将多变量回报分布降至一维，利用一维散度（Wasserstein、Cramér、MMD）进行度量；设计了均匀切片和最大切片两种变体，并分别证明其在共享标量折扣和通用稠密折扣矩阵下的贝尔曼收缩性质；分析不同基散度与单样本贝尔曼更新的兼容性。

Result: 在玩具链问题、基于图像的网格世界环境以及部分Atari游戏上进行了实验评估，验证了SDRL的有效性。

Conclusion: SDRL通过切片技术成功将一维散度扩展至多变量DRL，首次为矩阵折扣下的分布DRL提供了贝尔曼收缩保证，且支持多种基散度选择，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) models the full return distribution rather than expectations, but extending it to multivariate settings remains challenging. Many common metrics do not naturally generalize beyond one dimension or lose computational tractability, and the multivariate case introduces additional difficulties such as general matrix discounting, for which no contraction results are available. We introduce Sliced Distributional Reinforcement Learning (SDRL), which lifts tractable one-dimensional divergences to multivariate return distributions via projections. We prove Bellman contraction for uniform slicing under shared scalar discounting, and introduce a maximum-slicing variant with contraction under general dense discount matrices. SDRL supports a broad class of base divergences; we analyze Wasserstein, Cramér, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), and characterize which SDRL variants suit the standard single-sample Bellman update used in distributional RL. We evaluate SDRL on a toy chain problem and a gridworld image-based environment as well as a subset of Atari games.

</details>


### [161] [Functional MRI Time Series Generation via Wavelet-Based Image Transform and Spectral Flow Matching for Brain Disorder Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30387)
*Hwa Hui Tew,Junn Yong Loo,Fang Yu Leong,Julia K. Lau,Ding Fan,Hernando Ombao,Raphaël C. -W. Phan,Chee Pin Tan,Chee-Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DSFM框架，通过离散小波变换和离散余弦变换的双重频谱表示结合流匹配生成模型，合成高保真fMRI BOLD信号，并在下游脑网络分类任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI采集资源密集，高质量样本稀缺，现有生成模型难以复现BOLD信号的非平稳性、复杂时空动态和生理变异性。

Method: (1) 利用DWT将BOLD信号转换为小波分解图捕获全局瞬态和多尺度变化；(2) 投影到DCT空间利用低频频谱能量压缩特性；(3) 训练频谱流匹配模型生成类别条件的余弦频率表示；(4) 通过逆DCT和逆DWT重建时域BOLD信号。

Result: 生成样本保留了生理上合理的BOLD动力学特征，在下游脑网络分类任务中表现提升。

Conclusion: 双重变换方法施加了结构化的频率先验，有效保留了关键生理脑动力学，提高了fMRI生成数据的质量及下游任务性能。

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging (fMRI) provides non-invasive access to dynamic brain activity by measuring blood oxygen level-dependent (BOLD) signals over time. However, the resource-intensive nature of fMRI acquisition limits the availability of high-fidelity samples required for data-driven brain analysis models. While modern generative models can synthesize fMRI data, they often remain challenging in replicating their inherent non-stationarity, intricate spatiotemporal dynamics, and physiological variations of raw BOLD signals. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Spectral Flow Matching (DSFM), a novel fMRI generative framework that cascades dual frequency representation of BOLD signals with spectral flow matching. Specifically, our framework first converts BOLD signals into a wavelet decomposition map via a discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to capture globalized transient and multi-scale variations, and projects into the discrete cosine transform (DCT) space across brain regions and time to exploit localized energy compaction of low-frequency dominant BOLD coefficients. Subsequently, a spectral flow matching model is trained to generate class-conditioned cosine-frequency representation. The generated samples are reconstructed through inverse DCT and inverse DWT operations to recover physiologically plausible time-domain BOLD signals. This dual-transform approach imposes structured frequency priors and preserves key physiological brain dynamics. Ultimately, we demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through improved downstream fMRI-based brain network classification. The code is available at https://github.com/htew0001/DSFM.git .

</details>


### [162] [What changes after deployment? A survey on On-device Learning in TinyML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31226)
*Massimo Pavan,Luca Pezzarossa,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri,Xenofon Fafoutis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了约70篇关于微控制器设备上机器学习（TinyML）的在线学习（ODL）研究工作，从分布变化模式这一统一原则出发，分析不同类型分布变化对应用、硬件和解决方案结构的影响，并指出方法基准与实际部署场景之间存在持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TinyML设备在部署后常面临分布变化（distribution change）问题，导致静态模型失效。现有文献缺乏对分布变化类型的系统刻画，也未阐明不同变化类型需要不同的解决方案。

Method: 系统综述约70篇在线学习（ODL）相关文献，采用"分布变化模式"作为统一分析框架，从分布变化类型、可解决的应用、采用的硬件、解决方案结构等维度进行对比分析。

Result: 识别出不同分布变化类型对ODL解决方案设计的影响，并发现方法学基准与实际部署场景之间存在持续差距。

Conclusion: 分布变化是TinyML在线学习的核心挑战，不同类型的分布变化需要针对性解决方案；未来研究应缩小基准测试与真实部署之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Machine learning models on microcontroller-class devices (TinyML) face a fundamental challenge: post-deployment distribution change undermines static models. On-device learning (ODL) addresses this by running the learning process directly on the device. The existing literature has not characterized how distribution change occurs or how different change types require different solutions. Approximately 70 ODL works are surveyed under one principle: the distribution change regime. The survey analyzes how different types of distribution change influence the applications addressable on-device, the hardware employed, and the structure of the solutions. A persistent gap between methodological benchmarks and real-world deployment scenarios is also identified.

</details>


### [163] [EchoRL: Reinforcement Learning via Rollout Echoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31228)
*Jinhe Bi,Aniri,Minglai Yang,Xingcheng Zhou,Wenke Huang,Sikuan Yan,Yujun Wang,Zixuan Cao,Michael Färber,Xun Xiao,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoRL方法，通过利用验证成功但优势退化的rollout中的熵模式信息，解决了RLVR训练中因奖励信号坍缩导致策略梯度消失的问题，在多个基准和模型上持续提升训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）方法在训练过程中，随着训练推进，越来越多prompt的rollout出现"优势退化"现象：所有自生成rollout均验证成功，导致奖励标准差为零，每个rollout的优势也随之退化，策略梯度最终消失，训练性能达到瓶颈。然而这些退化rollout中仍包含有价值的学习信号，被现有方法忽略了。

Method: 受外部专家模型生成的金标准轨迹中熵模式分析启发，提出EchoRL轻量级模块：首先从验证成功的rollout中基于逐层熵值识别EchoClip，然后将该片段作为辅助监督信号注入RL目标函数中。

Result: 在10个基准、5种LLM骨干网络和4种主流RLVR后训练方法上的大量实验表明，EchoRL能以最小开销持续提升RLVR后训练性能。

Conclusion: EchoRL通过挖掘被忽略的优势退化rollout中的熵信号，有效突破了RLVR训练的性能天花板，是一种轻量且通用的改进方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards is an effective route for post-training to strengthen the reasoning capability of large language models. However, as training proceeds, the learning signal can collapse thus makes the training gain become marginal and ineffective. Specifically, a growing fraction of prompts' rollouts become advantage-degenerated: all the self-generated rollouts show verified-success, making the standard deviation over their rewards be zero; accordingly each rollout's advantage becomes degenerated (zero) as well. Given such rollouts' advantages, the policy-gradient for model optimization eventually vanishes, capping the training performance. We argue that some of these rollouts still contain valuable learning signals but unfortunately omitted with the existing RLVR methods. In this paper, inspired through analyzing the entropy pattern behind golden trajectories produced by external expert models, we propose EchoRL for better exploiting the advantage-degenerated rollouts to further improve the training performance. EchoRL is a lightweight module that first identifies an EchoClip from verified-success rollouts based on their step-level entropy values, and then feeds this clip back as an auxiliary supervision signal in the RL objective. Extensive experiments across 10 benchmarks, 5 LLM backbones, and 4 popular RLVR post-training methods demonstrate that EchoRL consistently improves RLVR post-training with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [164] [A Context-Aware Middleware for Medical Image Based Reports: An approach based on image feature extraction and association rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30699)
*Erick O. Rodrigues,Jose Viterbo,Aura Conci,Trueman Mac Henry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种上下文感知中间件，通过分析医务人员的历史诊断数据和当前临床上下文，自动推断最适合接收特定医学影像的医师，以优化医疗工作流程并提高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医院、实验室和远程放射学公司中，将医学影像转发给特定医师的过程耗时且低效，需要反复判断谁是最合适的医师以及其当前可用性。

Method: 设计了一个上下文感知中间件，能够处理并收集每位医务人员分析过的影像数据，基于收集的历史数据和当前临床上下文，推断出最适合接收新医学影像的医务人员。

Result: 该中间件能够根据上下文自动推荐最合适的医师接收影像，减少了人工决策的时间消耗。

Conclusion: 所提出的上下文感知中间件可以有效提升医疗工作流程的组织效率和效率。

Abstract: This work proposes a context-aware middleware for medical workflow organization and efficiency improvement. In hospitals, laboratories and teleradiology companies, each physician or technician is specialized in a specific kind of diagnosis or analysis. Therefore, certain types of medical images are often forwarded to a certain physician or a certain group. This forwarding is time consuming. That is, repeatedly deciding who would be the best physician, whether he is available at a certain moment given a certain context is exhaustive and may be very inefficient. Thus, the proposed middleware has the ability to process and collect data from images analyzed by each medical staff. Based on the collected data and current clinical context, the middleware is able to infer who would be the best fit staff to receive a certain incoming medical image.

</details>


### [165] [Bifurcated Remaining Useful Life Prediction: A Hybrid Approach for Realistic Uncertainty Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31241)
*Xabier Belaunzaran,Antonio Nappa,Arkaitz Artetxe,Basilio Sierra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合预测框架，通过状态感知策略（将寿命分为健康与退化阶段）、LSTM自编码器状态分类、条件威布尔生存分析（健康期）和概率神经网络+蒙特卡洛丢弃法（退化期）进行不确定性感知的涡轮风扇发动机剩余寿命预测，生成物理一致性高的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RUL预测方法缺乏对运行状态（健康/退化）的显式区分，且难以同时捕获偶然不确定性和认知不确定性，无法为风险-informed维护提供可靠的置信区间。

Method: 1) LSTM自编码器在正常数据上训练，通过重构误差进行状态分类；2) 健康阶段使用条件威布尔生存分析估计平均剩余寿命；3) 退化阶段使用概率神经网络+蒙特卡洛丢弃法捕获两类不确定性；4) 通过校准sigmoid函数将自编码器输出转为连续状态概率，动态加权集成预测。

Result: 生成物理一致性高的不确定性带，在寿命末期预测置信度高，同时准确反映早期运行的内在方差。

Conclusion: 该框架为风险-informed维护提供了稳健工具，能够产生物理一致的不确定性区间。

Abstract: This study presents a novel hybrid prognostic framework for uncertainty-aware Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimation in turbofan engines using the NASA C-MAPSS dataset. The framework employs a state-aware strategy that bifurcates the engines operational lifespan into "healthy" and "degraded" regimes. An LSTM-based autoencoder, trained strictly on nominal data (RUL > 150 cycles), monitors reconstruction error to act as a robust state classifier. For the healthy regime, a Conditional Weibull Survival Analysis is used for Mean Residual Life estimation. For the degraded regime, a Probabilistic Neural Network with Monte Carlo Dropout captures both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. Rather than using rigid binary labels, a calibrated sigmoid function converts the autoencoders output into continuous state probabilities, dynamically weighting the final ensemble prediction. The primary strength of this framework is its generation of physically consistent uncertainty bands, yielding high-confidence predictions near end-of-life while accurately reflecting the inherent variance of early operation, providing a robust tool for risk-informed maintenance.

</details>


### [166] [Spectral Reach: Understanding Neural Scaling as Progress into the Spectral Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31244)
*Konstantin Nikolaou,Jonas Scheunemann,Sven Krippendorf,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"谱位置"（spectral position）作为可扩展度量，用于追踪经验神经切线核（eNTK）特征值对损失降低的贡献。研究发现训练过程中谱位置持续下降，学习从主导特征模式转向谱尾；更大模型具有更强的"谱覆盖"（spectral reach）能力，能利用更弱的谱信号；特征学习通过自适应放大梯度是谱覆盖的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律虽指导基础模型发展，但其背后的机制尚不清晰，且缺乏可扩展的分析工具来理解这些现象。

Method: 提出"谱位置"度量，用于量化eNTK中各特征值对损失降低的贡献动态；通过缩放实验分析谱位置随训练的变化，对比不同规模模型的行为差异。

Result: 谱位置在整个训练过程中持续下降（学习从主导模式转向谱尾）；更大模型能覆盖更深层的谱尾信号（谱覆盖能力更强）；特征学习通过自适应放大梯度维持谱尾学习进度，而冻结表示则停滞。

Conclusion: 更大模型因具备更强的谱覆盖能力而实现更低损失；特征学习是支撑谱覆盖的关键；可通过架构和优化器设计进行针对性干预。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe predictable power-law relationships between model size, dataset size, compute, and performance. While these laws guide the development of modern foundation models, the mechanisms underpinning them remain poorly understood, in part due to the absence of scalable analysis tools. To close this gap, we introduce "spectral position": a scalable measure of which eigenvalues of the empirical neural tangent kernel (eNTK) currently drive loss reduction. Applying this measure to scaling experiments, we find that spectral position decreases throughout training: learning shifts from dominant eigenmodes into the spectral tail. Larger models reach further into the tail than smaller models, revealing a size-dependent capacity we call "spectral reach". This suggests why larger models achieve lower losses: they sustain learning on weak spectral signals inaccessible to smaller models. We further identify feature learning as a key enabler of spectral reach. It adaptively amplifies gradient magnitudes as learning advances, sustaining progress where frozen representations stall. This points to concrete interventions through architecture and optimizer design.

</details>


### [167] [Beyond Accuracy: Evaluating Efficiency, Robustness and Explainability in Deep Learning for Malaria Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30734)
*Olivier Kanamugire,Kerol Djoumessi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在NLM-Malaria数据集上基准测试了四种深度学习模型，发现轻量级模型在性能上与重型模型无显著差异，但后验可解释性（XAI）在图像损坏下不可靠，模型置信度比准确率更易退化，为资源受限环境下的疟疾诊断提供了部署依据，同时揭示了解释可靠性在临床部署中的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾在撒哈拉以南非洲仍是主要致死原因，诊断基础设施稀缺；深度学习虽可用于自动化筛查，但计算成本和决策不透明阻碍了临床采用。

Method: 在NLM-Malaria数据集上对四种深度学习模型进行基准测试，联合评估预测性能、鲁棒性和事后可解释性；使用Friedman检验确认统计显著性；在三种图像损坏类型下评估鲁棒性；采用CAM-based和细粒度归因方法进行XAI分析。

Result: 轻量级高效模型与重型模型预测性能无显著差异；CAM-based XAI方法能一致定位诊断相关区域，细粒度归因方法解释性较差；模型置信度比准确率退化更快；所有XAI方法在图像损坏下均不可靠，即使预测仍准确时解释可靠性也已下降。

Conclusion: 支持在资源受限环境中部署轻量级架构进行疟疾诊断，同时强调后验解释的脆弱性是临床负责任部署的重要考量因素。

Abstract: Malaria remains a leading cause of mortality in sub-Saharan Africa, where scarce diagnostic infrastructure makes timely, accurate diagnosis particularly challenging. While deep learning offers a compelling path toward automated malaria screening, clinical adoption is hindered by computational cost and opacity in decision-making. This work benchmarks four deep learning models spanning a wide range of designed design architectures and model capacities on the NLM-Malaria dataset, jointly evaluating predictive performance, robustness, and post-hoc explainability. We find that lightweight, efficient-by-design models match their heavier counterparts in predictive performance, and the Friedman test confirms no statistically significant performance differences. CAM-based XAI methods consistently localize diagnostically relevant regions, while fine-grained attribution methods produce less targeted explanations, particularly with heavier backbones. Robustness evaluation under three types of image corruption further reveals that model confidence degrades faster than accuracy, providing a practical signal for human review. However, no XAI method is robust to corruption, with explanation reliability degrading at noise levels plausible in clinical practice, even when predictions remain accurate. These findings support the deployment of lightweight architectures for malaria diagnosis in resource-constrained settings, while highlighting the vulnerability of post-hoc explanations as an important consideration for responsible clinical deployment.

</details>


### [168] [Toward Identifiable Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31245)
*Walter Nelson,Theofanis Karaletsos,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAE存在训练不稳定性问题，本文通过理论分析和架构改进提出了iSAE（identifiable SAE），在降低重构误差的同时提高了稳定性，并从字典学习角度提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE在实践中虽然被广泛用于神经网络表示的解释和交互，但不同训练运行会产生不同的概念词典和稀疏编码，这种不稳定性严重影响了其可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 理论分析了导致SAE不稳定的模型特性，通过对标准TopK SAE的架构和训练过程进行最小化改进，提出iSAE的两个变体；从传统字典学习方法的角度建立理论连接，证明实践中学到的词典满足近似受限等距性质（Restricted Isometry Condition），从而使稀疏编码近乎可识别。

Result: iSAE在降低重构误差的同时显著提升了稳定性；从理论上解释了词典满足近似受限等距条件时稀疏编码的可识别性。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和架构改进有效解决了SAE的不稳定性问题，iSAE是一种更稳定、重构误差更低的稀疏自编码器变体，具有理论保证。

Abstract: Recently, sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as an attractive tool for interpreting and interacting with representations in practical neural networks. While it is common empirical folklore, we also show theoretically that SAEs are highly unstable: different training runs are likely to produce different concept dictionaries and sparse codes. We characterize the model properties that hinder the stability of real-world SAEs, and address each of these problems through minimal changes to the architecture and training procedure. Together, these changes yield two versions of an \textbf{i}dentifiable SAE (iSAE), a variant of the standard TopK SAE with lower reconstruction error and improved stability. We explain this improvement theoretically by connecting SAEs with traditional dictionary learning approaches, and show that the dictionaries learned in practice satisfy an approximate restricted isometry condition, rendering the corresponding sparse codes in those models near-identifiable.

</details>


### [169] [Parallel Tempering Initial Sampling in Inference-Time Reward Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30991)
*Myeongjun Oh,Gwangho Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATHS是一种基于并行回火的初始化方法，通过耦合多个采样链和Metropolis交换机制，解决了扩散模型在推理时奖励对齐中因复杂奖励景观导致的模式陷阱问题，显著提升了对稀有高奖励区域的探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMC方法在推理时奖励对齐中因从标准先验初始化粒子而表现不佳，因为高奖励区域在复杂奖励景观中极为稀有；且即使采用奖励感知的初始采样方法，仍易陷入多模态奖励景观的局部模式中。

Method: 提出PATHS方法，通过并行回火维护一组奖励温度不同的采样链，并周期性进行Metropolis交换，使粒子能够在平坦化的奖励景观上高效探索，从而缓解模式陷阱问题。

Result: 在布局到图像和数量感知生成任务上，PATHS在复杂提示词下持续提升了对齐质量。

Conclusion: PATHS的并行回火机制显著增强了有限预算下对稀有高奖励区域的探索能力，克服了标准SMC和奖励感知初始化方法的局限性。

Abstract: Inference-time reward alignment steers pretrained diffusion and flow-based generative models to satisfy user-specified rewards without retraining. Recently, Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) has emerged as a powerful framework for this task by iteratively filtering and propagating multiple particles. However, we show that standard SMC-based methods often suffer from poor performance because they initialize particles from a standard prior, whereas high-reward regions in complex reward landscapes are extremely rare. Further, we show that even recent reward-aware initial sampling approaches remain vulnerable to getting trapped in local modes, as complex reward landscapes are often multi-modal. To overcome these limitations, we propose PATHS (PArallel Tempering for High-complexity reward Sampling), a novel initialization method that couples multiple sampling chains through parallel tempering. PATHS maintains a ladder of reward-tempered chains and periodically performs Metropolis swaps, enabling efficient exploration across flattened reward landscapes, thereby mitigating the mode-trapping issues. Our analysis reveals that this mechanism substantially enhances the finite-budget exploration of rare, high-reward regions that are typically challenging to sample. Experiments on layout-to-image and quantity-aware generation show that PATHS achieves consistent gains in alignment quality, particularly on complex prompts.

</details>


### [170] [Learning Cardiac Latent Representations in Vectorcardiogram Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31249)
*Bosong Huang,Panzhen Zhao,Zengxiang Li,Patricia Lee,Wei Jin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Ming Jin,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LVCG框架，在Frank向量心电图（VCG）空间而非标准十二导联ECG空间进行自监督表示学习，以减少冗余、提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG表示学习方法在可观测的ECG信号空间操作，但十二导联ECG本质上是同一心电活动在不同空间方向的投影，存在大量冗余，可能导致伪相关和过拟合风险。

Method: 受Frank VCG模型启发，提出LVCG，首个在物理有据的潜在VCG空间中学习统一心电活动表示的自监督框架，通过学习视图不变的潜在VCG表示而非导联特定伪影来减少冗余。

Result: LVCG在多项任务中普遍优于ECG空间基线方法，在域偏移场景下展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在VCG空间进行表示学习能有效减少冗余、提升模型泛化能力，为心电信号表示学习提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is a cornerstone of cardiac assessment, making the learning of informative ECG representations fundamental to tasks ranging from disease diagnosis to clinical report generation. However, existing methods operate almost exclusively in the observable ECG signal space. In practice, the standard twelve-lead ECG represents multiple projections of the same underlying cardiac electrical activity from different spatial orientations. Therefore, representation learning in the ECG space inevitably introduces substantial redundancy, which may lead to spurious correlations and increased risk of overfitting. To address this and motivated by the Frank vectorcardiogram (VCG) model, we propose learning a unified latent representation of cardiac electrical activity directly in the VCG space. We introduce LVCG, the first general self-supervised representation learning framework designed to operate in this physically grounded latent space. By learning view-invariant latent VCG representations rather than lead-specific artifacts, VCG minimizes redundancy and improves generalization. LVCG generally outperforms ECG-space baselines across tasks, demonstrating enhanced robustness and generalization, especially in domain shift settings.

</details>


### [171] [Lightweight CNN-Based Anomaly Detection for High Voltage Converter Modulators in the Spallation Neutron Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31259)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Julián Luengo,Isaac Triguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高功率脉冲变换器故障检测，通过调整时序滤波与跨通道混合的顺序，并引入自适应通道重加权，在SNS加速器HVCM数据集上实现了比现有方法更优的故障检测性能（AUC-PR 0.816，AUC-ROC 0.934），揭示了归纳偏置对多通道信号故障检测的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率脉冲变换器的非计划停机是大型加速器设施停机的主要来源。在散裂中子源（SNS）中，高压变换器调制器（HVCM）是导致束流时间损失的第二大因素。现有深度学习方法通常从第一层就将时序和跨通道操作纠缠在一起，没有为模型提供显式机制来表示通道独立性或结构化的通道间交互，因此需要改进模型架构来提升故障检测性能。

Method: 提出通过改变时序滤波和跨通道混合两种操作的顺序来测试归纳偏置的影响，并引入每脉冲自适应通道重加权机制进一步增强检测灵敏度。在公共HVCM数据集上，针对所有四个SNS子系统（RFQ、DTL、CCL、SCL）进行实验评估。

Result: 最佳变体实现了池化AUC-PR为0.816，AUC-ROC为0.934，在大多数子系统和六个故障家族中的五个上超越了现有最优方法。消融实验识别出三个主导输入通道，并将每个故障家族的性能与其前兆表现方式（单个通道的幅度变化还是需要联合通道表示的微妙模式）关联起来。

Conclusion: 架构的归纳偏置（时序滤波和跨通道混合的顺序）对多通道信号故障检测性能起核心作用，自适应通道重加权可进一步提升性能。不同故障类型的前兆表现形式不同，需要针对性地设计检测策略。

Abstract: Unscheduled trips of high-power pulsed converters are a leading source of downtime at large accelerator facilities. At the Spallation Neutron Source (SNS), the High Voltage Converter Modulators (HVCMs) are consistently the second-largest contributor to lost beam time. Each HVCM pulse is recorded across sensor channels spanning currents, voltages, and magnetic fluxes, whose mutual interactions encode the operating state of the system. Fault precursors do not manifest uniformly across these channels: depending on fault type, they may alter the temporal structure of individual signals, change the statistical dependencies among channels, or both. Existing deep-learning approaches typically process multi-channel signals with standard convolutional pipelines that entangle temporal and cross-channel operations from the first layer, giving the model no explicit mechanism to represent channel independence or structured inter-channel interaction. We hypothesise that architectural inductive bias, specifically the ordering of temporal filtering and cross-channel mixing, plays a central role in detection performance on this class of data. To test this, we vary the order in which these two operations are applied, and examine whether per-pulse adaptive channel reweighting further improves sensitivity. Evaluated on the public HVCM dataset across all four SNS subsystems (RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL), our best variant achieves a pooled AUC-PR of 0.816 and AUC-ROC of 0.934, outperforming the state of the art on most subsystems and five of the six fault families. Ablations identify three dominant input channels and link per-fault-family performance to whether precursors manifest as amplitude shifts in individual channels or as subtler patterns requiring joint channel representations to surface.

</details>


### [172] [Graph Neural Networks Are Not Continuous Across Graph Resolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31315)
*Christian Koke,Yuesong Shen,Abhishek Saroha,Marvin Eisenberger,Bastian Rieck,Michael Bronstein,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明图神经网络（GNNs）在自然图收敛模式下并不连续，导致相似图（尤其是不同分辨率下的同一对象）生成差异巨大的潜在表示。作者将这一不连续性归因于常用信息传播方案的结构性障碍，并提出了一种原则性修改，使模型具备跨尺度的连续性，从而实现不同分辨率下的一致表示和可靠泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战社区中关于GNN连续性的传统认知，揭示GNN在跨尺度图相似性保持方面的根本缺陷，并为此提供理论解释和解决方案。

Method: 理论分析揭示了信息传播方案导致的结构性障碍，并提出一种原则性修改来赋予模型跨尺度连续性。

Result: 标准GNN在不同分辨率下生成显著不同的潜在表示；提出的修改实现了跨尺度的一致表示和可靠泛化。

Conclusion: GNN不连续性问题可通过原则性架构修改得到解决，从而实现跨尺度的连续性与泛化能力。

Abstract: We show that contrary to conventional wisdom in the community, graph neural networks (GNNs) are not continuous with respect to all natural modes of graph convergence. As a result, GNNs may generate substantially different latent representations for graphs that are very similar. In particular they assign vastly different latent embeddings to graphs that represent the same underlying object at different resolution scales. We trace this failure of continuity back to a structural obstruction arising from commonly used information-propagation schemes. Building on this insight we then derive a principled modification to standard GNN architectures which equips models with continuity across scales. The proposed modification enables consistent integration of distinct resolutions and reliable generalization between them. We systematically validate our theoretical findings in a wide range of numerical experiments.

</details>


### [173] [Fixed Universal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31423)
*Jingwen Liu,Alexandr Andoni,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通用Transformer（Universal Transformers）——固定参数的Transformer可通过适当的输入嵌入模拟指定类别中的任何Transformer，类似于通用图灵机。研究给出了嵌入维度足够大时的稀疏构造，并证明随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然具有通用性。实验在括号平衡和多跳推理任务上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer的表达能力是否主要来源于输入表示而非学习到的权重，以及验证是否存在固定参数但能模拟其他Transformer的通用模型。

Method: 理论构造了稀疏的通用Transformer架构，在嵌入维度足够大时实现通用性；通过概率论证证明随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然通用。

Result: 1) 给出了显式的稀疏通用Transformer构造；2) 证明了随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然通用；3) 在括号平衡和多跳推理任务上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: Transformer的表达能力很大程度上可能来源于输入表示而非学习到的权重，这为理解Transformer的泛化能力和表示学习提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{universal transformers}: fixed transformers that can simulate any transformer in a given class via a suitable input embedding. Analogous to a universal Turing machine, the input embedding encodes a description of the target model while all internal parameters remain fixed. We provide explicit sparse constructions achieving universality when the embedding dimension is sufficiently large, and further show that universality is generic: randomly initialized transformers are universal almost surely, which aligns with recent empirical results of Zhong and Andreas (2024). We empirically validate our theory on the algorithmic tasks of parenthesis balancing and multi-hop reasoning. Our results suggest that much of a transformer's expressive power may reside in its input representation rather than its learned weights.

</details>


### [174] [DG-CoLearn: An Efficient Collaborative Learning Framework for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31427)
*Ashley Hoi-Ting Au,Zikun Zhang,Ligang He,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DG-CoLearn，一种面向协作动态图学习的客户端无感知增量学习框架，通过增量快照处理和服务端中介嵌入交换，在保护跨客户端结构隐私的同时实现高达33.8倍训练加速和27.4倍通信开销降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图学习方法存在两大问题：一是重复全快照重训练导致计算开销大，二是不适用于数据分区的协作场景。跨分区边不可避免，但直接共享图结构会违反隐私约束。

Method: 基于增量图快照处理，将计算聚焦于时间更新影响的图区域，通过时序建模保留历史信息；采用服务器中介的嵌入交换机制，在不暴露原始跨客户端结构信息的前提下实现准确的多跳消息传递。

Result: 训练时间加速最高33.8倍，通信开销降低最高27.4倍；节点分类F1提升最高13.36%，链接预测MAP提升最高8.27%。

Conclusion: DG-CoLearn有效桥接了协作动态图学习中的效率、可扩展性和客户端间结构隐私保护。

Abstract: Dynamic graph learning (DGL) is essential for modelling evolving graph data, but existing methods suffer from significant computational overhead due to repeated full-snapshot retraining and are not well-suited for collaborative settings with partitioned data. In realistic graph systems, cross-partition edges are unavoidable, but direct sharing of graph structure between clients may violate privacy constraints. We propose DG-CoLearn, a client-oblivious collaborative dynamic graph learning framework built on incremental graph snapshot processing, which focuses computation on graph regions affected by temporal updates while preserving historical information through temporal modelling. This incremental design is consistently applied across the entire graph processing pipeline, including a server-mediated embedding exchange mechanism to enable accurate multi-hop message passing without exposing raw cross-client structural information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DG-CoLearn achieves up to 33.8$\times$ speedup in training time and 27.4$\times$ reduction in communication overhead, while consistently improving predictive performance on both node classification (up to 13.36% F1 improvement) and link prediction (up to 8.27% MAP improvement) tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of DG-CoLearn in bridging efficiency, scalability, and client-to-client structural privacy in collaborative dynamic graph learning.

</details>


### [175] [Flow map learning in nonlinear vector autoregressive models: influence of the feature-library structure on the training error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31438)
*Markus Gross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性向量自回归（NVAR/NG-RC）模型在时间序列预测中的可辨识性问题，揭示了训练误差随时间分辨率变化的标度律，并指出特征库与真实动力系统匹配性对泛化能力的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测需要处理非线性和时滞依赖，而NVAR/NG-RC模型通过显式特征库近似Koopman算子。然而，这类模型的可辨识性（即能否从数据中正确学习底层动力学）尚不清楚，尤其是训练误差与时间分辨率、非线性程度及延迟项之间的关系缺乏理论理解。

Method: 通过理论分析证明训练误差作为时间分辨率函数遵循特征（预）渐近标度律，该律依赖于特征库能否精确表示流映射（传播子）的早期Lie级数系数。针对多项式向量场，分别采用单项式和傅里叶特征库进行验证，并数值实验于多个混沌动力系统。

Result: 1）训练误差标度律取决于特征库对Lie级数系数的表示能力；2）延迟项可降低最优一步训练误差，但仅在特征库提供足够非线性时才改善长期预测；3）模型类与真实数据生成过程不匹配时，小训练误差与弱泛化共存。

Conclusion: 特征库与真实动力系统的匹配性是决定NVAR/NG-RC模型泛化能力的关键因素，而非单纯追求小训练误差；研究结果为设计更有效的动力系统学习模型提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Time series forecasting often requires learning nonlinear and time-delayed dependencies. A paradigmatic class of forecasting models are nonlinear vector autoregressive processes (NVAR), also known as next-generation reservoir computers (NG-RCs). These models approximate the Koopman operator on the space spanned by their explicit feature library. We consider the identifiability problem for learning Markovian nonlinear dynamical systems and show that the training error as a function of time resolution follows characteristic (pre-)asymptotic scaling laws. These laws depend on whether the feature library can represent the early Lie-series coefficients of the flow map (propagator) exactly or merely approximately. For dynamical systems governed by polynomial vector fields, we demonstrate the mechanism for NVAR/NG-RC models with monomial and Fourier feature libraries. We determine the dependence of the training error on the temporal resolution, the involved nonlinear degree, and the number of delay terms. While delay terms reduce the optimal one-step training error, they improve long-horizon forecasts only when the library provides sufficient nonlinearity. Thus, small training error coexists with weak generalization as the model class is mismatched to the true data-generating process. Numerical experiments on various chaotic dynamical systems confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [176] [DRIFT: Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Efficient Multi-Turn Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31455)
*Jian Mu,Tianyi Lin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT提出了一种将KL正则化RL目标转化为重要性加权监督学习的方法，通过离线采样轨迹并计算重要性权重，在保持SFT训练效率的同时达到在线RL的多轮交互性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互场景中，在线RL虽能有效处理多轮动态但成本高昂，离线SFT虽高效但存在分布偏移和行为崩溃问题，亟需一种兼顾效率与性能的方法。

Method: DRIFT框架将KL正则化RL目标等价转化为重要性加权监督学习，从固定参考策略离线采样交互轨迹，基于回报计算重要性权重，然后通过加权SFT优化策略。

Result: DRIFT在多轮交互任务上匹配或超越了多轮RL基线，同时保持了标准SFT的训练效率和简洁性。

Conclusion: DRIFT有效弥合了在线RL与离线SFT之间的差距，为多轮交互场景下的大语言模型训练提供了高效且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multi-turn interactive settings where users or environments can iteratively provide lightweight feedback. Unfortunately, optimizing such behavior presents a sharp dilemma in practice: online reinforcement learning is able to effectively address multi-turn dynamics but is prohibitively expensive due to the cost of generating full correction trajectories at every update, whereas offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is efficient but suffers from distribution shift and behavioral collapse. To this end, we novelly propose DRIFT (Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning), a framework that operationalizes the theoretical insight that the KL-regularized RL objective is equivalent to importance-weighted supervised learning. DRIFT decouples rollout from optimization by sampling offline interaction trajectories from a fixed reference policy, deriving return-based importance weights, and optimizing the policy via weighted SFT on the resulting dataset. Empirically, we demonstrate that DRIFT matches or exceeds the performance of multi-turn reinforcement learning baselines while maintaining the training efficiency and simplicity of standard supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/DRIFT.

</details>


### [177] [PithTrain: A Compact and Agent-Native MoE Training System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31463)
*Ruihang Lai,Hao Kang,Haozhan Tang,Akaash R. Parthasarathy,Zichun Yu,Junru Shao,Todd C. Mowry,Chenyan Xiong,Tianqi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了智能体任务效率（ATE）这一新指标，并基于四项智能体原生的设计原则构建了紧凑的MoE训练框架PithTrain，在保持与生产框架相当吞吐量的同时，显著降低了智能体理解、操作和扩展框架所需的成本（Agent Turns减少62%，Active GPU Time减少64%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产级MoE训练框架经过多年优化，但演进新架构和系统优化成本高昂。AI编码智能体虽能自动化部分开发工作，但应用于现有框架时存在被吞吐量评估所忽视的隐性成本，亟需一个新的评估维度和设计范式。

Method: 定义了智能体任务效率（ATE）指标；提出了四项智能体原生设计原则；基于这些原则构建了紧凑的MoE训练框架PithTrain；并设计了ATE-Bench基准测试，涵盖真实训练框架开发任务。

Result: PithTrain在吞吐量上与生产级框架持平；在ATE-Bench上，PithTrain实现了更高的智能体任务效率：Agent Turns减少62%，Active GPU Time减少64%。

Conclusion: 智能体任务效率（ATE）是评估训练框架智能体友好性的关键维度，智能体原生设计可在不牺牲吞吐量性能的前提下大幅降低智能体使用成本，为未来AI辅助框架开发提供了新方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the dominant architecture for frontier language models. To meet this demand, production frameworks have built optimized MoE training stacks over years of engineering effort. Yet evolving these stacks for new architectures and system optimizations remains expensive. With the rise of AI coding agents, they could automate parts of training-framework development and accelerate this evolution. But applying them to these existing frameworks carries hidden costs, invisible to today's throughput-only evaluations. We name this missing dimension agent-task efficiency (ATE): the cost of using coding agents to understand, operate, and extend a framework. Grounded in four agent-native design principles, we build PithTrain, a compact, agent-native MoE training framework. We further introduce ATE-Bench, covering real-world training-framework tasks. Our evaluation shows PithTrain matches the throughput of production frameworks, and on ATE-Bench, PithTrain enables higher agent-task efficiency, with up to 62% fewer Agent Turns and 64% less Active GPU Time.

</details>


### [178] [Balanced LoRA: Removing Parameter Invariance to Accelerate Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31484)
*Valérie Castin,Kimia Nadjahi,Pierre Ablin,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA存在过参数化问题，不同低秩因子对的条件数差异大，影响收敛速度。BaLoRA通过将迭代投影到平衡流形上改善条件数，实现更快收敛和更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在微调大语言模型时被广泛采用，但存在过参数化问题：多对低秩因子可以得到相同适应权重矩阵。这些因子对的条件数存在显著差异，导致收敛到不同损失极小值点时收敛速度不同。作者希望改善LoRA的收敛性能。

Method: 提出BaLoRA（Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation），通过将迭代投影到平衡流形上，在保持适应矩阵不变的同时改善损失景观的条件数。该投影步骤计算轻量，可无缝集成到现有微调流程中。

Result: 理论和实验均证明：不同低秩因子对的条件数存在显著差异，影响收敛速率。BaLoRA相比标准LoRA收敛更快，在多种微调任务上表现更优。

Conclusion: LoRA的过参数化特性导致条件数差异，进而影响收敛速度。BaLoRA通过平衡流形投影有效改善条件数，是一种计算高效且性能更优的LoRA改进方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is the most widely adopted method for fine-tuning large language models. Notably, LoRA is inherently overparameterized: multiple pairs of low-rank factors can yield the same adapted weight matrix. We show--both theoretically and empirically--that these pairs exhibit significantly different condition numbers. As a result, converging to different loss minimizers directly impacts the convergence rate of LoRA. Building on this observation, we introduce Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation (BaLoRA), a variant of LoRA that projects iterates onto a balanced manifold. This manifold improves the conditioning of the loss landscape while preserving the adapted matrix. The projection step is computationally lightweight and integrates seamlessly into existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirically, BaLoRA converges faster than standard LoRA and achieves superior performance across a range of fine-tuning tasks.

</details>


### [179] [Graphical einops: bridging tensor networks and computation graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31485)
*Vincent Wang-Maścianica,Nikhil Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化的图形演算方法，用于表示einops风格的张量编程结构片段，使架构图从纯表征性变为可证明性。该演算将张量轴表示为嵌套分级管，结合了无向张量网络视图和有向计算图视图，核心重写规则是分级自然性（滑动"眼镜"），可将标准等变性证明简化为简短图解推导，并能将注意力掩码转换为预处理操作，实现高效稀疏注意力块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习架构图通常仅具表征性，其建议的张量-程序恒等式仍需文字和轴操作证明，缺乏形式化、可证明的图形推理工具。

Method: 提出一种形式化的分级图形演算：将张量轴表示为围绕基类型的嵌套分级管（graded tubes），管边界恢复无向张量网络视图，管内部保留有向计算图的操作语义。核心重写规则为分级自然性（grade-naturality），即"滑动眼镜"操作。

Result: 标准等变性证明可简化为短小的图解推导；该重写系统可应用于将注意力掩码转换为预处理操作，从而恢复高效稀疏注意力块的实现。

Conclusion: 所提出的分级图形演算使架构图具备证明能力，为张量编程提供了形式化推理基础，并展示了其在优化注意力机制等实际应用中的价值。

Abstract: Architecture diagrams are ubiquitous in deep learning, but they are usually only representational: the tensor-program identities they suggest are still proved by prose and tensor-axis manipulation. We introduce a formal graphical calculus for the structural fragment of tensor programming underlying einops, making such diagrams proof-enabling. Our calculus represents tensor axes as nested graded tubes around a base type. The tube boundary recovers the undirected tensor-network view of axes, while the directed interior retains the operational reading of computation graphs. The key rewrite is grade-naturality: sliding spectacles over tubes. Standard equivariance proofs become short diagrammatic derivations. We additionally demonstrate how our rewrite system may be applied to convert attention masks into pre-processing operations, recovering efficient implementations of sparse attention blocks.

</details>


### [180] [Assign and Add: A Mechanistic Study of Compositional Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31497)
*Brady Exoo,Alberto Bietti,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过变量赋值和模加法的受控实验，研究了小型Transformer中组合泛化的机制。研究发现相同的模加法MLP模块在直接输入和间接变量赋值下均被复用，训练动态分为三个阶段：先学习模加法，再学习变量赋值结构，最后进行精化实现组合泛化。论文还提供了理论框架解释组合性如何从训练动态中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer中组合泛化（compositional generalization）的具体机制，揭示模型如何组合已学技能来执行训练中未见过的复杂任务。

Method: 采用变量赋值和模加法（modular addition）的受控实验设置，将训练数据划分为不相交集合，通过机制分析（mechanistic analysis）检查模型内部模块的复用情况，结合实证训练动态分析和理论框架解释组合性涌现。

Result: 小型Transformer能够泛化到训练中未见的变量与数字组合；相同的模加法MLP模块在直接输入和通过变量赋值间接输入时均被复用；训练动态呈现三阶段特征；提供了组合性涌现的理论解释。

Conclusion: 组合泛化可以是Transformer内部机制组合性（compositionality）的自然结果，而非需要特殊设计或外在条件。

Abstract: Large language models are able to compose skills in order to perform complex tasks, many of which might not have been seen during training. The details of how exactly this composition occurs remain elusive. In this paper, we study a mechanism for compositional generalization in transformers by considering a simple controlled setting involving variable assignment and modular addition. By partitioning our training data into disjoint sets, we observe that small transformers are able to generalize to previously unseen combinations of variables and numbers. Our mechanistic analysis shows that the same ``modular addition'' MLP module is used whether the inputs are given directly or indirectly through a separate variable assignment mechanism. We also analyze the training dynamics from an empirical lens, which reveals three phases of learning: first, modular addition is learned, then the structure required for variable assignment, and finally a refinement phase where the model generalizes to some hard sequences not seen in training. Finally, we provide a theoretical framework to explain how compositionality emerges from training dynamics. These results suggest that compositional generalization can be a natural consequence of the compositionality of internal mechanisms in~transformers.

</details>


### [181] [Scalable Inference-Time Annealing with Surrogate Likelihood Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31498)
*Daniel Peñaherrera,Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SITA提出了一种可扩展的推断时退火方法，通过基于能量的代理似然函数对流模型进行迭代微调，在避免计算昂贵的散度项的同时实现了分子玻尔兹曼分布的高效采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算化学和生物物理学中长期存在的挑战是高效采样分子的玻尔兹曼分布。现有基于扩散模型的退火方法在推断时需计算分数场上的散度来估计重要性权重，对于大分子体系在计算上不可行。

Method: SITA通过迭代微调基于流的生成模型，沿温度阶梯逐步生成更低温度的样本。该方法使用能量模型（EBM）作为快速代理似然函数，替代了传统方法中昂贵的散度项计算。

Result: 在丙氨酸二肽和丙氨酸三肽上取得了最先进的性能，同时避免了代价高昂的散度项。

Conclusion: SITA通过能量模型代理似然替代散度计算，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低了计算成本，使得推断时退火方法可扩展到更大的分子系统。

Abstract: A long standing challenge in computational chemistry and biophysics is efficiently sampling the Boltzmann distribution of molecules. Advances in generative modeling have been proposed to address the limitations of conventional sampling techniques by eliminating the computational cost of simulation. A promising direction is iteratively finetuning diffusion models along a temperature ladder whereby training data is generated via importance sampling during inference-time annealing. Unfortunately, these methods require computing a divergence over the score field to estimate importance weights, rendering them intractable for larger systems. Here we present scalable inference-time annealing (SITA), which retrains flow-based models to generate samples at progressively lower temperatures using an energy-based model to facilitate fast surrogate likelihoods. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both Alanine Dipeptide and Alanine Tripeptide while avoiding costly divergence terms. Our code is available at: https://github.com/countrsignal/sita.git

</details>


### [182] [On Efficient Scaling of GNNs via IO-Aware Layers Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31500)
*Daria Fomina,Daniil Krasylnikov,Alexey Boykov,Andrey Dolgovyazov,Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy,Fedor Velikonivtsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过I/O和算术强度分析，将GNN层分为三种核函数族（SpMM卷积、归约聚合、注意力层），针对每种族开发了减少数据移动、提升局部性的GPU内核，实现了最高8.5倍加速和76倍内存降低，并作为即插即用替代方案开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN框架（如DGL、PyG）因稀疏不规则的访存模式成为性能瓶颈，复杂层常物化边级中间结果，增加内存流量并限制大规模图上的可扩展性。

Method: 从I/O和算术强度角度出发，将常见GNN层归纳为三类核函数族：基于SpMM的卷积、基于归约的聚合、基于注意力的层（GATv2/Graph Transformer）。针对每类开发专用GPU内核，并研究图重排序对不同核函数映射的影响。

Result: 融合注意力核在Graph Transformer上最高3.9倍加速（中位数1.6倍），Tensor Core块稀疏变体在局部稠密图上最高7.3倍；GATv2最高8.5倍加速（中位数2.0倍），峰值内存最高降低76倍（中位数6倍）；度感知归约核最高10倍加速（中位数2.6倍）；SpMM层中适当缓存的cuSPARSE最高8倍加速。

Conclusion: 基于I/O和算术强度的视角能有效指导GNN GPU内核优化，不同核函数族需采用不同优化策略，所提方法可作为即插即用替代方案实现可复现的硬件感知GNN加速。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bottlenecked by sparse, irregular memory access. Popular frameworks such as DGL and PyTorch Geometric support general message passing, but complex layers often materialize edge-wise intermediates, increasing memory traffic and limiting scalability on large graphs. We take an I/O- and arithmetic-intensity--centric view and show that widely used layers fall into three kernel families: SpMM-based convolutions, reduction-based aggregations, and attention-based layers (GATv2/Graph Transformer). For each family, we develop GPU kernels that reduce data movement, improve locality, and remain robust across realistic graphs. We also study graph reordering and find that its impact depends on the kernel mapping: it benefits neighbor-parallel (gather-dominated) kernels more consistently than feature-parallel designs. Empirically, our fused attention kernels reach up to $\textbf{3.9}\times$ speedup for Graph Transformer (median $\textbf{1.6}\times$), with Tensor Core (block-sparse) variants up to $\textbf{7.3}\times$ on locally dense graphs; for GATv2 we reach up to $\textbf{8.5}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.0}\times$) while reducing peak memory by up to $\textbf{76}\times$ (median $\textbf{6}\times$). Our degree-aware reduction kernels achieve up to $\textbf{10}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.6}\times$). For SpMM-based layers, properly cached cuSPARSE achieves up to $\textbf{8}\times$ speedup over DGL and outperforms evaluated custom baselines in the majority of evaluations. We release our implementations as drop-in replacements to support reproducible, hardware-aware GNN acceleration.

</details>


### [183] [When Are Multimodal Predictions Biologically Supported? A Diagnostic Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31504)
*Dylan Steiner,Gustavo Arango-Argoty,Gerald Sun,Etai Jacob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DECAT是一个模型无关的事后评估框架，通过五个零参考指标和规则决策过程，将多模态表示分类为四种诊断场景，能在不需要混淆变量标签的情况下检测出AUROC无法发现的混淆效应，并在证据不足时返回不确定结果。在合成数据（2500+训练表示）和真实TCGA数据（8979名患者）上验证发现，CLIP等纠缠模型在共享生物学检测上接近完美，但在大多数缺失共享生物学的真实基础模型嵌入中会错误声称共享生物学，且这种错误率随混淆强度增加而上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态肿瘤学模型能产生准确预测，但准确预测无法揭示模型学到的是跨模态共享的生物学、单一模态特有的生物学，还是反映混杂因素而非真实生物学的伪相关。需要一种方法区分这些不同情况。

Method: 提出DECAT框架，使用五个零参考指标和基于规则的决策程序，将多模态表示分类为四个诊断场景。框架作用于学习到的表示，不需要知道具体存在哪个混淆变量，证据不足时返回不确定。在四种多模态模型类别的合成数据（2500+训练表示）和8979名TCGA患者的真实数据上验证。

Result: 纠缠模型（如CLIP）能近乎完美地检测共享生物学，但在大多数真实基础模型嵌入中，当共享生物学不存在时会错误声称存在。这种错误声称率随混淆强度增加而上升，更大规模和更强表示会产生更自信但依然错误的诊断。DECAT在无需混淆标签的情况下检测到AUROC无法发现的混淆效应。

Conclusion: DECAT能够有效区分多模态表示中的共享生物学、模态特有特征和伪相关，揭示了现有模型在不存在共享生物学时容易错误声称共享生物学的问题，且该框架无需混淆变量标签即可检测混淆效应。

Abstract: Multimodal models in oncology can produce accurate predictions, but accurate prediction does not reveal whether the model has learned biology that is shared across modalities, biology confined to one modality, or spurious correlations that reflect confounders rather than genuine biology. We introduce DECAT, a model-agnostic post-hoc evaluation framework that classifies multimodal representations into four diagnostic scenarios for a given task and modality, using five null-referenced metrics and a rule-based decision procedure. The framework operates on learned representations, requires no knowledge of which specific confounder is present, and returns indeterminate when the evidence is insufficient. We validate DECAT on synthetic data across four multimodal model classes (over 2,500 trained representations) and on real data from 8,979 TCGA patients, evaluating both multimodal embeddings and five pretrained pathology foundation models. Entangled models (e.g., CLIP) achieve near-perfect shared biology detection but falsely claim shared biology in the majority of cases where it is absent on real foundation model embeddings. This false claim rate increases with confound strength so that larger cohorts and stronger representations produce more confident but still incorrect diagnoses. Applied to both multimodal TCGA embeddings and five pathology foundation models without paired RNA, DECAT detects confounding invisible to AUROC without requiring the confounder labels, as confirmed by post-hoc stratification.

</details>


### [184] [Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31509)
*Zhikun Xu,Yu Feng,Jacob Dineen,Taiwei Shi,Jieyu Zhao,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ReuseRL方法，基于最小描述长度原则，通过提取可复用的技能字典并引入分割代价来惩罚低压缩性行为，从而提升强化学习智能体的泛化能力。在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise任务上，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外场景下均优于vanilla GRPO和强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型智能体在使用强化学习训练时，往往学习到脆弱的、任务特定的捷径行为，泛化能力差。作者假设当成功轨迹在结构上可压缩、可分解为一小组可复用的抽象模式时，智能体能够更好地泛化。

Method: 基于最小描述长度原则，从成功轨迹中提取共享技能字典，并在RL目标中添加分割代价，显式惩罚编码效率低下的特异性行为。同时证明了该压缩惩罚的PAC-Bayes泛化界。

Result: 在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise三个基准任务上，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外场景下的成功率均优于vanilla GRPO和强round-length基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式鼓励轨迹的结构化压缩（即可复用的抽象模式），可以有效提升RL训练的LLM智能体的泛化能力，避免学习过度特化的捷径行为。

Abstract: Large language model agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) often learn brittle, task-specific shortcuts. We hypothesize that agents generalize better when their successful trajectories are structurally compressible, decomposed into a small set of reusable abstract patterns. To formalize this, we introduce ReuseRL, which grounds agentic RL in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. ReuseRL extracts a shared skill dictionary from successful trajectories and augments the RL objective with a segmentation cost, explicitly penalizing idiosyncratic behaviors that encode poorly. We prove a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for this compression penalty. Across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise, ReuseRL improves in- and out-of-distribution success over vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines.

</details>


### [185] [On the Relationship Between Activation Outliers and Feature Death in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31518)
*Elana Simon,Etowah Adams,James Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAE中的特征死亡（feature death）源于激活层的维度级离群值（dimension-level activation outliers），其严重程度由γ=‖μ‖/‖σ‖量化；均值中心化（mean-centering）可彻底消除此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE学习到的许多特征从未激活（特征死亡），这浪费了字典容量并可能重新引入叠加现象（superposition）；且不同模型间死亡率的巨大差异原因不明。

Method: 提出维度级激活离群值概念，用γ=‖μ‖/‖σ‖量化离群严重程度；通过分析454个模型-层组合验证γ与初始死亡率的强相关性（Spearman ρ=0.89/0.82）；发现死特征可通过SAE偏置学习激活均值复活，但高γ下极慢；提出均值中心化作为预处理步骤。

Result: γ预测初始死亡率（死-by-TopK ρ=0.89，死-by-ReLU ρ=0.82）；均值中心化在所有测试模型上彻底消除了离群值引发的特征死亡。

Conclusion: 特征死亡的根本原因是激活层均值与特征对齐方向的相互作用，离群值γ是核心预测指标；均值中心化是消除该问题的简洁、有理论基础的预处理方法。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose neural network activations into interpretable features, but many learned features never activate, a problem called feature death that wastes dictionary capacity and can reintroduce superposition. Death rates vary dramatically between models: near-zero on GPT-2, over 70% on AlphaFold3 with identical configurations. We find that dimension-level activation outliers (dimensions whose mean magnitude is large relative to per-token variation) cause this by shifting pre-activations at initialization based on each feature's alignment with the activation mean. Features anti-aligned with the mean receive permanently negative pre-activations and never fire. We formalize outlier severity as $γ= \|μ\|/\|σ\|$; it predicts initial death rates (Spearman $ρ= 0.89$ for dead-by-TopK, $0.82$ for dead-by-ReLU) across 454 model-layer combinations spanning language, vision, protein, and genomic models. Dead features can revive during training, but recovery requires the SAE bias to learn the activation mean, a process that is prohibitively slow at high $γ$. Mean-centering (subtracting the activation mean) sidesteps this and eliminates outlier-induced death across all tested models, confirming the mechanism and providing a principled basis for when and why this preprocessing step is necessary.

</details>


### [186] [Chem-PerturBridge: a harmonized compendium of small molecule perturbation transcriptomic effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31522)
*Artur Szałata,Olga Novitskaia,Maiia Shulman,Matthew Mella,Altynbek Zhubanchaliyev,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chem-PerturBridge是一个包含37k+化合物、136种细胞环境和125万转录组样本的跨数据集整合资源，并利用该资源评估了跨数据集签名一致性和化合物表征学习预训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小分子建模的转录组数据资源分散在不同技术平台、元数据规范、对照设置、剂量和预处理流程之间，缺乏统一标准化的多数据集资源来支持大规模扰动模型训练。

Method: 整合多数据集构建标准化资源，包含统一标识符、元数据和可重复感知的条件级效应值；通过配对条件一致性和复现一致性评估跨数据集签名可靠性；将资源作为预训练数据评估化合物表征学习性能。

Result: 相同化合物的配对条件在细粒度logFC排序和幅度上跨数据集一致性普遍较弱，但logFC方向一致性显著更稳定；基于Chem-PerturBridge预训练的嵌入在化合物表征学习任务中优于L1000-only嵌入、Morgan指纹和基线。

Conclusion: Chem-PerturBridge既能支持跨数据集签名一致性的诊断评估，也能支持异构扰动转录组数据的模型重用。

Abstract: Large perturbation models require training data encompassing chemical, cellular, and assay diversity. Current transcriptomic resources for small-molecule modeling, however, are fragmented across technologies, metadata conventions, controls, doses, and preprocessing pipelines. We introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized multi-dataset resource comprising over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types, with standardized identifiers, metadata, and replicate-aware condition-level effects. We use the resource to evaluate matched-condition agreement across datasets and replicate agreement within datasets. Matched same-compound conditions generally show weak agreement in fine-grained logFC rankings and magnitudes across most dataset pairs, often falling below same-context different-compound baselines. In contrast, logFC direction agreement is substantially more stable and usually exceeds these baselines. We further evaluate Chem-PerturBridge as a pretraining resource for compound representation learning. Under a compound-held-out OP3 evaluation split, embeddings pretrained on Chem-PerturBridge improve over L1000-only embeddings, Morgan fingerprints, and the descriptor-free OP3 baseline across metrics. An extensive molecule-holdout evaluation across 11 datasets further shows that models trained on Chem-PerturBridge outperform or match those that are not. Chem-PerturBridge therefore supports both diagnostic evaluation of cross-dataset signature agreement and model-oriented reuse of heterogeneous perturbation transcriptomic data.

</details>


### [187] [Value Functions as Supermartingale Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31524)
*Alessandro Abate,Daniel Contro,Mirco Giacobbe,Agustín Martínez-Suñé,Diptarko Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了强化学习与随机系统形式化验证之间的理论联系：在适当奖励函数下，几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的策略所对应的值函数编码了Streett超鞅证书，从而为通过强化学习合成验证证书提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两类研究存在鸿沟：一方面，基于超鞅证书的验证方法可为随机系统（包括无限状态空间）提供ω-正则性质的几乎必然满足的形式化证明；另一方面，强化学习方法虽然能处理ω-正则任务，但通常缺乏对学习策略满足规范的形式化保证（尤其是对无限状态空间）。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 通过理论推导，建立了值函数与Streett超鞅证书之间的等价关系：对于几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的策略，其值函数（在适当奖励函数下）构成一个Streett超鞅证书。实验验证在有限马尔可夫决策过程上进行。

Result: 理论结果适用于有限、可数无限和连续状态空间；在有限MDP上通过实验验证了该理论连接的有效性。

Conclusion: 该理论连接为通过强化学习合成形式化验证证书提供了一条原则性路径，使得RL方法能够为更一般的状态空间生成具有形式化保证的证书。

Abstract: Certification methods for stochastic systems provide sufficient proof rules, based on real-valued supermartingale certificates, to determine the almost-sure satisfaction of $ω$-regular properties (and therefore of linear temporal logic) over general state spaces, encompassing both countably infinite and continuous state spaces. Conversely, reinforcement learning (RL) methods for $ω$-regular tasks have received considerable attention, but they typically lack formal guarantees that the learned policy satisfies the specification, except possibly for finite state and action spaces. We bridge these two lines of research by establishing a novel theoretical connection: under an appropriate reward, the value function associated to a policy that almost surely satisfies an $ω$-regular property encodes a Streett supermartingale certificate for that specification. Our results, validated experimentally on finite Markov decision processes, hold for finite, countably infinite, and continuous state spaces, suggesting a principled route to certificate synthesis via RL.

</details>


### [188] [The Dynamic-Probabilistic Consistency Gap in Chaotic Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31547)
*Andre Herz,Matthijs Pals,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动态系统重建中的动态-概率一致性（DPC）鸿沟：有限时域概率目标会损害动力学质量或解耦不确定性与局部切线动力学的关系。提出了KAFFEE框架，基于可微扩展卡尔曼滤波训练，通过创新似然和雅可比协方差传播弥合这一鸿沟，在超混沌Lorenz-96和13个混沌系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态系统重建（DSR）需要与学习动力学一致的 uncertainty 估计，但现有有限时域概率目标会导致不确定性估计与系统动力学脱节，即存在动态-概率一致性（DPC）鸿沟。

Method: 提出KAFFEE（Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation）框架，采用可微扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）训练，通过局部预测残差（innovation）评估似然，同时通过学习到的局部雅可比矩阵传输协方差。

Result: 在随机超混沌Lorenz-96上，KAFFEE减少了识别出的失败模式，改进了动力学不变量重建，保持了竞争性的预测分数。在13个混沌系统上，KAFFEE实现了上下文贝叶斯滤波，同时基本保持了零样本动力学。

Conclusion: KAFFEE能够有效弥合DPC鸿沟，在保持预测性能的同时，实现了与学习动力学更一致的 uncertainty 估计。

Abstract: Dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) aims to learn surrogate models that capture the dynamics underlying time-series data. Reliably deploying these surrogates requires uncertainty estimates consistent with the learned dynamics.
